### [1] [Decentralized Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning for Resilient Critical Infrastructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18359)
*Minghui Ding,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文论证了去中心化多智能体强化学习(MARL)因其在可扩展性、隐私支持、鲁棒性等方面与关键基础设施的韧性需求结构对齐，但实际部署需解决信用分配与通信两大核心条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施日益分布化、相互依赖且面临不断演变的干扰，韧性成为其运行控制的核心需求。现有观点将去中心化MARL仅视为集中训练分布执行的替代方案，而忽视了其与韧性关键基础设施需求之间的结构性契合。

Method: 通过分析去中心化MARL的特性与关键基础设施需求的对应关系，识别出信用分配和通信是实现实际部署的两大核心条件，并据此提出聚焦于结构感知、因果感知、韧性感知的信用分配，协调与信用分配的通信机制，以及安全及时的分布式学习的研究议程。

Result: 证明了去中心化MARL在大规模智能体可扩展性、隐私与本地自治支持、故障鲁棒性、相互依赖组件的交互驱动适应等方面与韧性关键基础设施需求存在结构性对齐；明确了信用分配和通信是实现这一范式转移的实践关键。

Conclusion: 去中心化MARL是韧性关键基础设施有前景但条件性的基础范式，其成功部署取决于能否有效解决信用分配与通信机制的核心挑战，需按照结构感知、因果感知、韧性感知的方向推进研究。

Abstract: Critical infrastructures are increasingly distributed, interdependent, and exposed to evolving disruptions, making resilience a central requirement for their operation and control. This paper argues that decentralized multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) should be understood not merely as a distributed alternative to centralized training with decentralized execution but as a paradigm structurally aligned with the requirements of resilient critical infrastructures. This perspective is grounded in an analysis of the properties of decentralized MARL and the requirements of critical infrastructures, including scalability to large numbers of agents, support for privacy and local autonomy, robustness to failures, and interaction-driven adaptation among interdependent components. However, structural alignment alone is insufficient for practical deployment. This paper identifies credit assignment and communication as two central conditions for its practical feasibility. Credit assignment determines whether local learning remains aligned with system-level objectives, while communication determines whether coordination can be learned and maintained under realistic operational constraints. Building on these challenges, this paper proposes a research agenda focused on structure-aware, causality-aware, and resilience-aware credit assignment; communication for both coordination and credit assignment; and safe, timely, and recoverable decentralized learning under deployment constraints. Overall, this paper reframes decentralized MARL as a promising but conditional foundation for resilient critical infrastructures.

</details>


### [2] [Scalable Policy Optimization for Networked Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning with Continuous State-Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18554)
*Dongming Wang,Pengcheng Dai,Wenwu Yu,Wei Ren*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了CDCPG算法，用于网络化马尔可夫决策过程中的协作强化学习，实现了与网络规模无关的局部计算和通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化多智能体系统中的协作强化学习面临可扩展性挑战：传统方法的计算和通信成本随网络规模增长，难以应用于大规模网络。现有截断方法存在continuation-kernel不匹配问题，且时间差分稳定性缺乏理论保证。

Method: 提出连续分布式耦合策略梯度（CDCPG）算法：1) 每个智能体维护局部演员网络，在有限图邻域内运行；2) 使用局部最小二乘时间差分评论家，通过转移核的谱随机特征表示评估截断动作价值函数；3) 采用自适应局部性规则选择邻域半径，平衡截断误差与精度需求。

Result: 在指数空间衰减的智能体交互、激励条件和目标平滑性假设下，CDCPG算法达到与非凸一阶优化相同的平滑速率$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$收敛至逼近地板；每个智能体的计算和通信成本由邻域大小而非网络规模决定；在网格化线性二次基准上验证了局部性和特征维度预测。

Conclusion: CDCPG算法通过精心设计的截断动作价值函数和谱随机特征表示，在保持理论保证的同时实现了可扩展的分布式协作强化学习，为大规模网络化系统提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We develop the Continuous Distributed Coupled Policy Gradient (CDCPG) algorithm for cooperative reinforcement learning in networked Markov decision processes with continuous state and action spaces. Each agent maintains a local actor over a bounded graph neighborhood, and a localized least-squares temporal-difference critic evaluates a truncated action-value function through a spectral random-feature representation of the local transition kernel. The analysis makes four contributions. First, the truncated action-value function is constructed as a conditional expectation over the neighborhood, yielding a well-posed localized Bellman theory that removes the continuation-kernel mismatch of naive truncation arguments. Second, we expose a dimensional obstruction to temporal-difference stability for normalized random features and prove an unconditional excitation bound that reduces stability to a symmetric persistence-of-excitation condition, monitorable through an online matrix-concentration certificate. Third, under exponential spatial decay of agent interactions, the excitation condition, and smoothness of the objective, CDCPG drives an averaged per-agent stationarity measure to within any excess $ε$ of an explicitly characterized approximation floor using $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ shared-oracle samples, and the excess dependence matches the smooth nonconvex first-order rate; per-agent computation and communication are governed by the neighborhood size rather than the network size. Fourth, an adaptive-locality rule selects the radius that balances truncation and graph-decay residuals against the target accuracy. Experiments on a networked linear-quadratic benchmark corroborate the locality and feature-dimension predictions.

</details>


### [3] [Strategy-Following Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning Considering Control Strategies Provided to Other Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18719)
*Yamato Takahagi,Gentoku Nakasone,Yoshinari Motokawa,Toshiharu Sugawara*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一种多智能体学习方法，允许部分智能体接收人类指令，其余智能体自动补全任务，实现了更灵活高效的人机协作控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体深度学习方法难以让人类管理者简单有效地控制智能体行为以适应环境和社会变化，且已有控制方法需对所有智能体下达指令，效率低且难以设计良好的协作机制。

Method: 扩展了多智能体深度强化学习中的可控性研究，设计了一种学习机制，使部分受指令控制的智能体执行人类管理者的关键指令，未受指令的智能体根据其他智能体的动作自适应地补充被忽视的任务和区域。

Result: 实验表明，采用该方法的智能体能够转移到另一种协作结构，并且性能优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了对多智能体系统更高效、灵活的控制，使人类能通过简单指令调控部分智能体，其余智能体自动配合，提升了系统适应性和协作效果。

Abstract: This study proposes a learning method for multi-agent systems that allows agents to be controlled through human manager instructions after learning and enables uninstructed agents to implicitly complement the overall work based on the actions of other agents. Multi-agent applications using deep learning have shown potential; thus, to achieve extensive social applications, humans should be able to control learned agents using simple methods to respond to environmental and social changes. Even without such changes, learned coordination often does not match the expectations of human managers, making it preferable to control coordination structures to match human intentions. Some studies have aimed to control agent behavior using simple instructions. However, they assumed that instructions are provided to all agents, which is time-consuming and not evident when designing a better cooperation regime. Ideally, specific agents should receive key action instructions, while others should automatically complete the remaining tasks. The proposed method, which extends previous work on controllability in multi-agent deep reinforcement learning, enables uninstructed agents to adaptively complement overlooked tasks and areas. The experimental results show that agents using the proposed method can shift to another cooperative structure and achieve better performance than those using conventional methods.

</details>

### [4] [A Progressive Approach to Synthesizable RTL Design Generation Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18519)
*Xiangfei Kong,Tasnim Tabassum,Marwan Abdelwahab,Hao Zheng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了VeriRefine框架，通过将RTL设计生成过程中的规范细化阶段变为可验证环节，在生成代码前就明确和验证设计意图，显著提高了大语言模型生成RTL设计的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型直接从自然语言规范生成RTL设计时，失败主要源于对规范的理解问题而非编码问题。由于规范是非正式且模糊的，模型的解释保持隐式，每个误读都被静默地写入Verilog代码，只有通过仿真才能暴露错误。现有工作使用中间表示使解释部分显式，但没有验证解释与规范的一致性，且在代码层面修复仿真失败，而不管误读源于何处。

Method: VeriRefine将规范细化视为RTL生成的可验证阶段。它逐步将文本规范细化为显式的、受模式约束的设计意图描述，表示为每个信号的抽象信号转换函数(ASTF)，在生成代码前确定每个信号的逻辑风格、时钟域和复位行为，并将每个行为基于逐字规范语句。细化后的规范通过五层审计（包括正确性、完整性、一致性、FSM完整性和核心RTL设计规则），在生成Verilog前修复解释错误。生成代码后，每个仿真失败被分类为理解错误或编码错误，并路由到相应阶段进行针对性修复。由于每个信号的硬件类型在细化阶段就已固定，可综合性成为管道的结构属性而非事后检查。

Result: 使用Claude Sonnet 4.6，VeriRefine在RTLLM v2.0数据集上达到94.0%的功能正确性，在VerilogEval-Human v2数据集上达到98.1%的功能正确性。

Conclusion: 通过将规范细化转化为可验证的显式过程并分层验证，VeriRefine有效解决了大语言模型生成RTL设计时的理解错误问题，显著提升了功能正确性，为自动化硬件设计提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Large language models can generate register-transfer-level (RTL) designs directly from natural language specifications. Their failures, however, arise mostly from understanding rather than coding \cite{zhang2026understanding, qiu2025towards}. A specification is informal and ambiguous, the model's interpretation stays implicit, and every misreading is committed silently into Verilog, where only simulation can expose it. Intermediate representations make the interpretation partly explicit, yet existing works don't verify the interpretation against the specification, and repair simulation failures at the code level regardless of where the misreading originated. VeriRefine instead treats specification refinement as a verifiable stage of RTL generation. It progressively refines the prose specification into an explicit, schema-constrained account of design intent, expressed as per-signal Abstract Signal Transition Functions (ASTFs) that commit each signal's logic style, clock domain, and reset behavior before any code exists and ground every behavior in a verbatim specification sentence. The refined specification then passes a five-layer audit spanning soundness, completeness, consistency, FSM integrity, and core RTL design rules, so interpretation errors are repaired at the representation level before any Verilog is generated. Once code is generated, each simulation failure is classified as an understanding error or a coding error and routed back to the corresponding stage for targeted repair. Because every signal's hardware class is fixed during refinement, synthesizability becomes a structural property of the pipeline rather than a post-hoc check. With Claude Sonnet 4.6, VeriRefine reaches 94.0\% functional correctness on RTLLM v2.0 and 98.1\% on VerilogEval-Human v2.

</details>


### [5] [PIP-NTT: Towards a Scalable Memory-Parallelized Accelerator for Iterative NTT in PQC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18533)
*Malik Imran,Ayesha Khalid,Ciara Rafferty,Safiullah Khan,Muhammad Rashid,Maire O'Neill*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PIP-NTT的高效格基后量子密码学NTT加速器，通过内存并行化策略和无乘法逆NTT重缩放架构，实现了比现有面积优化和高速加速器分别高2.67倍和1.48倍的平均面积-时间产品效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的迭代NTT加速器通常依赖于乒乓内存方案和与分圆环相关的大型内存块，这限制了整体效率。这些设计在面积和性能之间存在权衡，缺乏高效的内存并行化策略，亟需改进以提高计算吞吐量。

Method: 提出内存并行化策略，使用四个较小的n/4大小内存替代大型内存，保持总内存占用量；引入无乘法逆NTT重缩放架构；对统一Cooley-Tukey和Gentleman-Sande蝶形单元进行全面的硬件设计空间探索，评估粗粒度和细粒度流水线策略；集成两个优化蝶形单元和内存并行化方案构建PIP-NTT加速器。

Result: 在FPGA平台上的实验结果表明，PIP-NTT相比文献中最面积优化的NTT加速器，平均面积-时间产品效率提高2.67倍；相比最高速NTT加速器，效率提高1.48倍。该设计具有良好的可扩展性和适应性。

Conclusion: PIP-NTT加速器成功解决了传统迭代NTT加速器的内存效率瓶颈，通过创新的内存并行化和优化的蝶形单元设计，在严格的面积约束下实现了显著的计算吞吐量提升，为未来密码学硬件提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The iterative forward and inverse number theoretic transform (NTT) is a key component in lattice-based post-quantum cryptography (PQC), typically implemented using Cooley-Tukey and Gentleman-Sande butterfly units. Existing iterative NTT accelerators often rely on ping-pong memory schemes and large memory blocks tied to the cyclotomic ring, which limits overall efficiency. To overcome this, we propose a memory-parallelization strategy using four smaller n/4-sized memories for ring size n, preserving the total memory footprint of conventional designs. We also introduce a multiplication-free rescaling architecture for the inverse NTT. Building on these innovations, we perform a comprehensive hardware-based design space exploration of unified Cooley-Tukey and Gentleman-Sande butterfly units, evaluating both coarse- and fine-grained pipelining strategies. The resulting optimized butterfly unit forms the core of our proposed pipelined and memory-parallelized NTT accelerator, "PIP-NTT". It integrates two such units alongside the memory-parallelization scheme to boost computational throughput under tight area constraints. Experimental results on FPGA platforms show that PIP-NTT achieves 2.67x and 1.48x higher efficiency in average Area-Time Product compared to the most area-optimized and high-speed NTT accelerators in the literature. The design is scalable across butterfly radices and adaptable to other PQC schemes, making it a versatile solution for future cryptographic hardware

</details>


### [6] [An Efficient Fault-Tolerance Scheme for CKKS Computation on CPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18720)
*Jianan Mu,Ge Yu,Tenghui Hua,Liang Kong,Jing Ye,Xing Hu,Meng Li,Xiaowei Li,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向CPU的CKKS全同态加密高效容错方案，通过模感知桶校验和、算子内检查融合、跨算子检查融合三级优化，实现100%故障检测率，平均运行开销仅6.8%，保护开销降低4.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全同态加密(FHE)支持密文计算，但其长密文数据流和高维模运算易受瞬态硬件故障影响导致静默数据损坏。现有保护方法或面向专用加速器，或在CPU上引入显著的执行、模运算和内存访问开销，缺乏高效的CPU端容错方案。

Method: 提出三级优化方案：1)模感知桶校验和利用CPU宽累加器减少昂贵的模运算；2)算子内数据流融合检查将校验和累积嵌入算子数据流，避免对长密文多项式的单独扫描；3)跨算子检查融合消除相邻算子间冗余校验和计算，同时保持端到端检查不变量。在OpenFHE中实现该方案。

Result: 在随机单比特瞬态故障下，对150,000个非崩溃错误结果实现100%经验检测率；在广泛故障率范围内保持应用精度接近无故障基线；运行开销仅6.0%-8.4%（平均6.8%），相比直接校验和保护平均降低4.9倍保护开销。

Conclusion: 该方案有效解决了CPU上CKKS计算的容错问题，在保证高检测率和准确性的同时显著降低了性能开销，为FHE在实际CPU环境中的可靠部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) enables computation on encrypted data, but its long ciphertext dataflow and high-dimensional modular arithmetic make it vulnerable to silent data corruption caused by transient hardware faults. Existing protection methods either target dedicated accelerators or impose substantial execution, modular-arithmetic, and memory-access overheads on CPUs.
  This work presents an efficient fault-tolerance scheme for CPU-based CKKS computation. It checks the input-output consistency of polynomial operators while reducing protection overhead at three levels. First, modulus-aware bucket checksum exploits wide CPU accumulators to reduce expensive modular reductions. Second, dataflow-fused in-operator checking embeds checksum accumulation into operator dataflows, avoiding separate scans of long ciphertext polynomials. Third, cross-operator check fusion eliminates redundant checksum computations between adjacent operators while preserving end-to-end checking invariants.
  We implement the scheme in OpenFHE and evaluate it on representative encrypted applications and ciphertext primitives under random single-bit transient faults. It achieves a 100 percent empirical detection rate across 150,000 non-crashing corrupted-result cases and maintains application accuracy close to the fault-free baseline over a wide range of fault rates. The scheme incurs only 6.0 percent to 8.4 percent runtime overhead, averaging 6.8 percent, and reduces average protection overhead by 4.9 times compared with direct checksum-based protection.

</details>


### [7] [A Flexible Sparsity-Aware FPGA Accelerator with Column-Wise Compression for Efficient CNN Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19248)
*Amirhossein Zarei,Shervin Vakili*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了SparHiXcel-v2，一种基于FPGA的高可配置CNN加速器，通过二维MAC阵列和列级内核压缩技术实现了稀疏灵活性与硬件效率的平衡，在保持模型精度的同时显著提升了吞吐量和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限平台上高效加速CNN仍然具有挑战性。非结构化稀疏性虽然能保持较高的模型精度，但在硬件映射中引入了显著的不效率；而结构化稀疏性虽然简化了执行过程，却以降低灵活性为代价。现有的稀疏模式处理方法无法在稀疏灵活性、硬件效率和模型精度之间实现良好的平衡。

Method: 提出了SparHiXcel-v2架构，围绕可扩展的二维MAC阵列构建，引入了列级内核压缩技术，能够以最小的硬件开销高效处理不规则的稀疏模式。同时提出了硬件-算法协同设计框架，包括排序优化方案和针对微架构定制的多阶段结构化剪枝与恢复算法。在AMD Kintex UltraScale+ FPGA平台上实现了该加速器。

Result: 在VGG16模型上，结构化稀疏模式下达到超过2.5 TOPS的处理吞吐量和210 GOP/s/W的能效；在ResNet18模型上达到超过1.1 TOPS和72 GOP/s/W。在保持适度精度下降的同时，通过所提出的优化实现了处理吞吐量和能效的显著提升。

Conclusion: SparHiXcel-v2成功地在稀疏灵活性和硬件效率之间实现了改进的平衡，证明了所提出的列级内核压缩技术和硬件-算法协同设计框架的有效性，为资源受限平台上的CNN加速提供了一种成本效益高的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient acceleration of convolutional neural networks (CNNs) on resource-constrained platforms remains challenging due to the irregularity of sparsity patterns and the associated hardware overhead. While unstructured sparsity offers high model accuracy, it introduces significant inefficiencies in hardware mapping, whereas structured sparsity simplifies execution at the cost of reduced flexibility. This paper presents SparHiXcel-v2, a cost-effective and highly configurable FPGA-based CNN accelerator that achieves an improved balance between sparsity flexibility and hardware efficiency. The proposed architecture is built around a scalable two-dimensional MAC array and introduces a column-wise kernel compression technique that enables efficient handling of irregular sparsity patterns with minimal hardware overhead. To further enhance performance, we propose a hardware-algorithm co-design framework, including an ordering optimization scheme and a multi-phase structured pruning and revival algorithm tailored to the microarchitecture. Extensive evaluations on VGG16 and ResNet18 demonstrate that SparHiXcel-v2 achieves substantial improvements in processing throughput and energy efficiency through the proposed optimizations. In structured sparsity mode, the accelerator reaches over 2.5 TOPS and 210 GOP/s/W for VGG16, and over 1.1 TOPS and 72 GOP/s/W for ResNet18 on a cost-effective AMD Kintex UltraScale+ FPGA, while maintaining modest accuracy degradation.

</details>


### [8] [Distributed Delay-Based BIST for Mixed-Signal Circuits in Flexible Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19310)
*Paula Carolina Lozano Duarte,Sule Ozev,Mehdi Tahoori*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对柔性电子中IGZO-TFT的测试挑战，提出一种双用途环振荡器和压控振荡器，实现分布式延迟BIST框架，在极低的面积和功耗开销下达到高缺陷覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于IGZO-TFT的柔性电子产品在超低功耗可穿戴应用中日益重要，但缺乏封装、引脚受限以及器件差异性大，使得传统自动测试设备难以有效测试其模拟/混合信号电路，迫切需要一种适合柔性电子的内建自测试方法。

Method: 设计一种双用途环振荡器（RO）和压控振荡器（VCO），既作为功能时序模块，又作为分布式延迟BIST的核心结构。每个RO阶段嵌入轻量级数字BIST，实现阶段级延迟监测以检测缺陷。

Result: 所提振荡器相比以往IGZO-TFT设计实现了1100倍面积缩减和5600倍功耗降低；BIST对单个缺陷覆盖率达93%，对多重同时缺陷覆盖率达88%，且功耗开销仅约3%。

Conclusion: 该工作显著降低了柔性电子测试的功耗和面积开销，同时保持高缺陷检测率，为IGZO-TFT柔性电子的实用化提供了有效的测试解决方案。

Abstract: Flexible electronics (FE) based on indium gallium zinc oxide thin-film transistors (IGZO-TFTs) are emerging for ultra-low-power wearable applications. However, lack of packaging, limited pins, and high device variability make conventional Automatic Test Equipment (ATE) impractical for testing analog/mixed-signal circuits in FE. This work presents a dual-purpose ring oscillator (RO) and voltage-controlled oscillator (VCO) serving as functional timing blocks and core structures for a distributed delay-based BIST framework. The oscillators achieve 1100x area reduction and 5600x lower power than previous IGZO-TFT designs. Lightweight digital BIST embedded within each RO stage enables stage-wise delay monitoring for defect detection. The BIST achieves 93% defect coverage for individual defects and 88% for multiple simultaneous defects, with only 3% power overhead

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [9] [FALCON-Discover: Discovering Concentrated False-Confidence Regions for Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18278)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FALCON-Discover框架，用于发现模型预测空间中高置信度错误预测的集中区域。通过多种差异信号对预测进行排序，在强学习器上显著优于传统校准方法，表明危险过度自信应被视为区域发现问题而非单一分数校准问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统校准评估通常是整体性的，但最危险的失败往往是局部的：尽管预测错误但仍保持高置信度的预测。这种假置信度集中现象——高置信度错误占据紧凑、可发现的预测空间区域——是一个重要但未被充分研究的失败模式。

Method: 提出FALCON-Discover，一个事后、模型无关的框架，利用置信度、局部支持度、邻域一致性和扰动稳定性等多种差异信号对预测进行排序，从而识别高置信度错误预测的集中区域。

Result: 在7个二分类表格数据集上，使用XGBoost和CatBoost等强学习器进行五折交叉拟合实验，发现假置信度集中现象普遍存在但具有情境依赖性。基于差异的排序在强情境下显著优于校准和信任评分基线，而原始置信度几乎无法恢复危险错误。最佳检测器因数据集而异：需要组合多线索时学习差异方法最强，局部决策脆弱性主导时稳定性排序最佳。

Conclusion: 危险过度自信更适合作为族级别发现问题而非单一分数校准问题来处理，这启发了显式针对置信度、支持度和稳定性出现分歧区域的校准策略。

Abstract: Calibration is usually evaluated in aggregate, but the most dangerous failures are often local: predictions that remain highly confident despite being wrong. We study this failure mode as false-confidence concentration, the extent to which confident errors occupy compact, discoverable regions of prediction space. We introduce FALCON-Discover, a post-hoc, model-agnostic framework that ranks predictions using discrepancy signals from confidence, local support, neighborhood agreement, and perturbation stability. Across seven binary tabular datasets, four seeds, five-fold cross-fitting, and strong learners including XGBoost and CatBoost, we find that false-confidence concentration is recurrent but regime-dependent. At the main confidence threshold, discrepancy-based ranking substantially outperforms the strongest validation-selected calibration or trust-scoring baseline in the strongest regimes, while raw confidence recovers little dangerous-error mass. The best detector varies across datasets: learned discrepancy is strongest when multiple cues must be combined, whereas stability-centered ranking works best when local decisional fragility dominates. These results show that dangerous overconfidence is better treated as a family-level discovery problem than as a single-score calibration problem, and motivate calibration strategies that explicitly target regions where confidence, support, and stability diverge.

</details>


### [10] [Beyond Output-Space Calibration: Spectral Evidence Bundling for Selective Reliability Estimation in Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18279)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种验证门控的固定标签可靠性策略，通过结合输出置信度与频谱证据来提升时间序列分类器的可靠性估计，在保持预测不变的情况下判断是否值得信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列分类后处理校准方法仅重新映射输出分数，但部署决策（如信任、弃权和审查）需要判断高置信度预测是否有当前时序信号的支持。存在三个可靠性差距：相同置信度值可能掩盖不同的时序支持；平均校准可能遗漏虚假高置信度错误；输出空间重校准提供的输入关联可审计性有限。

Method: 提出验证门控固定标签可靠性策略，保持骨干预测不变，仅估计其是否值得信任。方法结合输出侧线索与全样本频谱描述符（包括频带能量、熵、峰值主导性、周期支持和相位稳定性），形成标量可靠性估计和诊断级频带证据。验证门控仅在正确性排序改善且不违反FalseConf@0.9或AURC容忍度时启用频谱调节，否则回退到更安全的输出空间基线。

Result: 在8个异构UCR/UEA数据集、8个时间序列骨干家族和标准重校准器上，无约束方法将匹配评估子集上的Corr-AURC从0.693提升至0.779；验证门控策略进一步将Corr-AURC提升至0.786，并将FalseConf@0.9降低至0.094。

Conclusion: 时间序列分类器的可靠性估计受益于将输出置信度与频谱证据捆绑使用，验证门控机制可防止缺乏支持的频谱调节，从而实现更可靠的部署决策。

Abstract: Post-hoc calibration for time-series classification usually remaps output scores, but deployment decisions such as trust, abstention, and review depend on whether a confident prediction is supported by the current temporal signal. We address three time-series reliability gaps: identical confidence values can hide different temporal support, average calibration can miss false high-confidence errors, and output-space recalibration offers limited input-linked auditability. We introduce a validation-gated fixed-label reliability policy that keeps the backbone prediction unchanged while estimating whether it should be trusted. The method combines output-side cues with whole-sample spectral descriptors, including band energy, entropy, peak dominance, period support, and phase stability, to form a scalar reliability estimate and diagnostic band-level evidence. A validation gate enables spectral conditioning only when correctness ranking improves without breaching FalseConf@0.9 or AURC tolerances; otherwise it reverts to the safer output-space baseline. Across eight heterogeneous UCR/UEA datasets, eight time-series backbone families, and standard recalibrators, the unconstrained method improves fixed-label selective-reliability metrics on the matched evaluation subset, raising Corr-AURC from 0.693 to 0.779. The validation-gated policy further improves Corr-AURC to 0.786 and reduces FalseConf@0.9 to 0.094. These results suggest that reliability estimation for time-series classifiers benefits from bundling output confidence with spectral evidence, while validation gating prevents unsupported spectral conditioning.

</details>


### [11] [Beyond Single-Dimensional Compression: The Compound Sparsity Frontier of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18280)
*Chao Han,Haozhe Hu,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出复合稀疏框架，结合静态参数剪枝和动态token级跳过，在相同总稀疏度下优于单一机制压缩，有效延缓性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM压缩方法（静态参数剪枝或动态token级计算）在超过关键稀疏度边界后会触发快速性能下降。本研究探索是否可以通过结合这两种机制来分散压缩负担，从而延缓性能退化。

Method: 提出极简复合稀疏框架：1）首先应用低秩近似和通道剪枝获得静态压缩骨干网络；2）引入轻量级路由器实现逐token动态层跳过。该设计可独立控制参数稀疏度和token级计算稀疏度。

Result: 在语言理解和建模基准测试中，复合稀疏在相同总稀疏度下始终优于单一机制压缩：在理解任务上延缓了衰减点，保持了更强的建模性能。进一步分析发现参数剪枝和token跳过之间存在跨维度干扰，在固定稀疏预算下近平衡分配最有效。

Conclusion: 复合压缩为改进LLM压缩提供了实用方案，同时揭示了更广泛的跨维度稀疏边界最终限制进一步压缩。代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often compressed through static parameter pruning or dynamic token-level computation, yet aggressive sparsification can trigger rapid performance degradation beyond an essential sparsity boundary. This work asks \emph{whether combining these two mechanisms can delay such degradation by distributing the compression burden}. We study a minimalist compound sparsity framework that first applies low-rank approximation and channel pruning to obtain a statically compressed backbone, and then introduces lightweight routers for per-token dynamic layer skipping. This design enables independent control of parameter sparsity and token-level computation sparsity. Experiments across language understanding and modeling benchmarks show that compound sparsity consistently outperforms single-mechanism compression under the same total sparsity, delaying the decay point on understanding tasks and preserving stronger modeling performance. Further analysis reveals cross-dimensional interference between parameter pruning and token skipping, and shows that near-balanced allocation is most effective under a fixed sparsity budget. These results demonstrate that compound compression provides a practical way to improve LLM compression, while revealing a broader cross-dimensional sparsity boundary that ultimately limits further compression. Code will be available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/LLM-Pruning.

</details>


### [12] [ALAS: Additive Learnable Alpha-Stable Kernels for Flexible Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18282)
*Weibo Huang,Cheng Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ALAS，一种基于对称α稳定谱分量的灵活高斯过程核函数族，能够通过学习稳定性参数α自适应地调整平滑度，在贝叶斯优化中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化在处理昂贵黑盒优化问题时，其成功很大程度上依赖于选择与目标函数未知结构相匹配的核函数。然而，传统方法往往难以准确选择合适的核函数来匹配目标的未知结构。

Method: 提出了ALAS核函数族，基于对称α稳定谱分量构建，能够通过学习稳定性参数α来自适应调整有效平滑度。提出了两种参数化形式：(1) ALAS：单一平稳分量，采用联合谱调制；(2) ALAS-Sep：可分离变体，学习维度方向的尾部行为以提高在近似可分解目标上的鲁棒性。

Result: 在标准基准测试和真实世界代理模型上的实验表明，ALAS方法在各种不同设置下都表现出强大且鲁棒的性能，能够同时捕捉平滑趋势和尖锐的不规则性。

Conclusion: ALAS提供了一种灵活且有效的解决方案，通过自适应学习核函数的平滑度特性，成功解决了贝叶斯优化中核函数选择的关键问题，展现出了良好的通用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization is widely used for expensive black-box optimization, yet its success often depends on choosing a kernel that matches the objective's unknown structure. In this work, we propose ALAS, a flexible Gaussian Process kernel family built from symmetric $α$-stable spectral components. By learning the stability parameter $α$, ALAS adapts its effective smoothness from data, capturing both smooth trends and sharp irregularities. We present two parameterizations: ALAS, a single stationary component with joint spectral modulation, and ALAS-Sep, a separable variant that learns dimension-wise tail behavior to improve robustness on approximately decomposable objectives. Experiments on standard benchmarks and real-world surrogates demonstrate strong and robust performance across diverse settings.

</details>


### [13] [FedCC: A Low-Resource Federated Adaptation of Foundation Models for Robust Corpus Callosum localization in Fetal Ultrasound Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18283)
*Alessandro Di Matteo,Sara Moccia,Giuseppe Rizzo,Gianpaolo Grisolia,Ricciarda Raffaelli,Lorenzo Vasciaveo,Francesco D'Antonio,Maria Chiara Fiorentino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCC框架，通过结合DINOv2骨干网络、YOLO检测头和LoRA模块，在保护数据隐私的联邦学习设置下实现胎儿超声图像中胼胝体的精准定位，参数量减少8.5倍，mAP@50达到0.857。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿超声图像中胼胝体的精准定位对于早期识别神经发育异常至关重要，但由于超声成像的固有限制（低对比度、斑点噪声）以及CC的解剖变异性，该任务极具挑战性。同时，现实的多中心和资源受限的临床环境要求在保护数据隐私的前提下进行模型训练。

Method: 提出FedCC联邦学习框架，集成冻结的DINOv2骨干网络与轻量级YOLO检测头；引入低秩适应（LoRA）模块实现参数高效适应，仅优化和交换少量参数子集，显著降低计算和通信开销。

Result: 在包含三临床站点10,970帧超声图像的多中心数据集上，DINOv2+LoRA结合FedAvg策略达到mAP@50为0.857、F1分数为0.803；可训练参数从24.4M降至2.9M（减少约8.5倍），优于全微调和编码器冻结基线。

Conclusion: FedCC框架在资源受限环境下实现了高效、隐私保护的胼胝体定位，为胎儿神经超声检查的可扩展、临床可部署AI系统迈出重要一步。

Abstract: Accurate localization of the corpus callosum (CC) in fetal ultrasound (US) images is crucial for the early identification of neurodevelopmental abnormalities. However, this task remains highly challenging due to the intrinsic limitations of US imaging, including low contrast, speckle noise, and the considerable anatomical variability of the CC. We propose FedCC, a federated learning (FL)-based framework for CC localization in fetal US images, specifically designed for realistic multi-center and resource-constrained clinical settings without requiring data sharing. The framework integrates a frozen DINOv2 backbone with a lightweight YOLO-based detection head. To enable parameter-efficient adaptation, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules are incorporated, allowing only a small subset of parameters to be optimized and exchanged among clients. This strategy substantially reduces both computational and communication overhead, making the framework suitable for low-resource environments. The proposed approach was evaluated on a multi-center dataset comprising 10,970 ultrasound frames acquired from 58 pregnant women during routine neurosonographic examinations across three clinical sites using heterogeneous imaging devices. The proposed framework achieved strong performance in the federated setting. In particular, the combination of DINOv2 and LoRA under the FedAvg strategy achieved an average mAP@50 of 0.857 and an F1-score of 0.803, outperforming both full fine-tuning and encoder-freezing baselines. Notably, the proposed approach reduced the number of trainable parameters to 2.9M compared with 24.4M in full fine-tuning, corresponding to an approximately 8.5$\times$ reduction in communication cost. These findings represent a promising step toward scalable, privacy-preserving, and clinically deployable AI systems for fetal neurosonography.

</details>


### [14] [A Self-Evolving Default Action for Cooperative Tasks with Continuous Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18597)
*Shuangyao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SAFE框架，首次将反事实信用分配有效扩展至连续动作空间，解决了传统蒙特卡洛采样方法的偏差和收敛问题，在协作车辆控制任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反事实信用分配方法仅适用于离散动作空间，而将其扩展至连续动作空间时面临挑战：基于蒙特卡洛采样的基线近似方法会引入策略梯度偏差，无法保证收敛到局部最优，且采样动作可能缺乏充分训练。

Method: 提出SAFE框架，使用基于智能体经验缓冲区中自演化默认动作的条件反事实基线。该方法无需额外仿真、奖励模型或特定环境的先验知识，即可自然扩展至连续动作空间，且不引入偏差。

Result: 理论证明该方法不会在确定性策略梯度中引入偏差，确保收敛到局部最优，能够准确量化每个智能体的贡献。在协作车辆控制任务上的大量实验表明，SAFE持续优于现有最先进模型。

Conclusion: SAFE成功解决了反事实信用分配在连续动作空间中的应用难题，为多智能体强化学习在连续控制领域的信用分配问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Counterfactual credit assignment has proven effective in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) for discrete action spaces, yet its extension to continuous-action cooperative tasks remains challenging. Existing methods that approximate the counterfactual baseline via Monte Carlo sampling often introduce bias into policy gradients and fail to guarantee convergence to local optima, as the sampled actions may not have been sufficiently trained. To address these limitations, we propose SAFE, a novel MARL framework that employs a counterfactual baseline conditioned on a self-evolving default action sampled from each agent's experience buffer. This design naturally extends to continuous action spaces without relying on additional simulations, reward models, or environment-specific prior knowledge. The baseline accurately quantifies each agent's contribution, and introduces no bias into the deterministic policy gradient, ensuring convergence to local optima. Extensive experiments on cooperative vehicular tasks demonstrate that SAFE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [15] [Compressing What Matters: Neuron Importance Meets Data-Aware Low Rank Approximation for Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18284)
*Athanasios Ntovas,Alexandros Doumanoglou,Petros Drakoulis,Dimitris Zarpalas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合参数重要性和功能等价性的神经网络压缩方法，并设计了一种高效的动态压缩率分配算法，在高压缩率下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型参数量巨大（数十亿），导致内存需求高，限制了其在资源受限环境中的应用。现有基于SVD的压缩方法要么关注参数重要性，要么关注层级功能等价性，两者相互独立。此外，现有方法在压缩率分配上多采用均匀分配或计算昂贵的启发式搜索，效果和效率有待提升。

Method: 提出一种统一的优化目标，将参数重要性和功能等价性两个视角结合起来进行低秩近似；同时设计了一种增强且计算高效的算法，用于动态分配各层和参数的压缩率，替代传统的均匀分配或昂贵搜索方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法与之前的最先进方法相当或显著更优，尤其在高压缩率场景下表现突出，验证了结合两种视角和动态压缩率分配的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合参数重要性与功能等价性的统一目标，配合高效的动态压缩率分配算法，能够有效提升神经网络压缩性能，尤其在资源受限场景下具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: To excel at their domain large language models are comprised of billions of parameters. Yet this comes at the cost of huge memory requirements restricting their applicability in resource-constrained environments. To address the problem of neural network (NN) compression Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) has played a key role as a fundamental component for matrix compression through decomposition. To minimize compression error and to maximize the efficacy of the compressed model on the downstream tasks previous works focused on low-rank approximation of the NN's weight matrices either from the perspective of parameter importance or per-layer functional equivalence. While previous works studied the aforementioned perspectives in isolation in this work we are investigating the effectiveness of an approach that combines ideas from these two perspectives in a single objective. In parallel to this an important aspect that affects the compression quality is the distribution of the compression rate across layers and NN parameters. Earlier works mostly considered distributing the compression rate uniformly across layers and network weights or relied on computationally expensive heuristic search. Contrary to them in this work we propose an enhanced and computationally efficient algorithm for dynamic compression rate allocation. Experimental results support the efficacy of the proposed approach which performs on par or substantially better than the previous state-of-the-art especially under high compression ratios.

</details>


### [16] [Edge-Efficient Transformer for End-to-End RF Spectrum Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18285)
*Zhifan Song,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos,Hassan Aboushady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出E-SpecFormer模型，通过创新的LiTAN注意力机制实现高效、轻量的射频信号调制识别和隐蔽信道检测，在保持高精度的同时显著降低模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对物联网设备上的实时频谱智能应用需求，现有的边缘模型往往参数量大、计算复杂度高，难以在资源受限的硬件上实现高效部署。本研究旨在开发一种轻量化、高精度的端到端自动调制识别和隐蔽信道识别解决方案。

Method: 1. 提出E-SpecFormer架构，专为射频频谱监测设计；2. 引入LiTAN（Linear Tanh Attention Network），一种无需Softmax和LayerNorm的注意力机制，降低复杂度并提升射频任务精度；3. 设计四个可扩展的参数变体（Nano、Small、Medium、Large）以适应不同硬件约束。

Result: 1. 在RadioML2018调制识别数据集上，Nano变体在SNR>0dB条件下达到86.5%平均准确率；2. 在硬件木马隐蔽信道数据集上达到94.2%准确率；3. 参数量少于10k，FPGA/CPU协同执行速度达92μs/帧；4. 以极低的计算成本超越现有边缘模型。

Conclusion: E-SpecFormer成功证明了在物联网边缘设备上实现实时、高效的频谱智能是可行的，其创新的LiTAN注意力机制在降低复杂度的同时保持了高精度，为资源受限环境下的射频信号处理提供了新的技术方案。

Abstract: We present E-SpecFormer (Edge Spectrum monitoring Transformer) for end-to-end automatic modulation and covert channel (CC) recognition. We introduce LiTAN (Linear Tanh Attention Network), a Softmax- and LayerNorm-free attention mechanism that reduces complexity while increasing accuracy in RF tasks. E-SpecFormer is parameterized in four scalable variants (Nano, Small, Medium, Large) to accommodate diverse hardware constraints. Using the RadioML2018 dataset for modulation recognition, the Nano variant achieves 86.5% average accuracy for Signal-to-Noise Ratios (SNRs)>0 dB, and on the hardware Trojan (HT)-based CC dataset it reaches 94.2% accuracy, both with fewer than 10k parameters and up to speed of 92 μs per frame on FPGA/CPU co-execution, surpassing state-of-the-art edge models at a fraction of their cost. These results establish E-SpecFormer as an edge-efficient solution for real-time spectrum intelligence on Internet of Things (IoT) devices. GitHub link to the repository: https://github.com/zsniko/E-SpecFormer.

</details>


### [17] [S3: Stable Subgoal Selection by Constraining Uncertainty of Coarse Dynamics in Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19232)
*Kshitij Kumar Srivastava,Kshitij Jerath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于粗粒度动力学的内在动机机制，用于改进层次强化学习（HRL）中高层策略的学习，在非平稳长时序任务上超越了现有的先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在层次强化学习中，高层智能体面临稀疏且延迟的环境反馈，且其性能依赖于低层执行能力。现有方法中高层策略学习效率低下，缺乏有效的策略性子目标选择机制，因此需要为高层智能体提供更有效的内在动机。

Method: 提出使用粗粒度动力学（coarse dynamics），即在高层智能体操作的时间尺度上聚合多步环境转移，通过混合密度网络（MDN）建模预测不确定性，并将预测不确定性的分散度度量作为内在奖励信号，引导高层策略学习风险规避的子目标选择。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的密集、动力学感知的内在奖励机制使高层智能体能够进行风险规避的子目标选择，在非平稳长时序环境中表现优于现有最先进的层次强化学习方法。

Conclusion: 通过为高层智能体提供基于粗粒度动力学的内在动机，可以有效稳定高层策略学习，提供导航引导结构，显著提升层次强化学习在复杂长时序任务中的性能。

Abstract: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) intends to separate strategic planning from primitive execution. It has been widely successful in solving long-horizon and complex tasks, where flat-RL algorithms have difficulty in learning. However, while the low-level agent in HRL benefits from dense feedback and abundant trial opportunities, the high-level agent receives sparse, delayed feedback from the environment and its performance depends on the low-level execution capability. In this paper, we study whether subgoal selection by the high-level agent can be performed more strategically, by providing it with dynamics-aware intrinsic motivation. Since motivation based on primitive transition dynamics would require broad coverage of the state-action space, we propose to use coarse dynamics, i.e., environment transitions aggregated over multiple steps at the temporal scale at which the high-level agent operates. This approach stabilizes the high-level policy by learning to minimize the predictive uncertainty associated with the coarse dynamics, and provides a guided structure for navigation. We model the predictive uncertainty by evaluating different dispersion metrics as approximated by a Mixture Density Network (MDN). Empirically, we observe that a dense, dynamics-aware intrinsic reward leads to risk-averse subgoal selection, enabling it to outperform state-of-the-art HRL methods in non-stationary long-horizon environments.

</details>


### [18] [Preference-Conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Runtime-Tunable Transit Signal Priority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18286)
*Philip-Roman Adam,Stefanie Schmidtner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于偏好条件的公交信号优先控制方法，可在运行时动态调节公交优先与整体交通延误之间的权衡，无需重新训练模型即可适应不同的运营优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的公交信号优先控制方法通常使用固定奖励函数或固定权重标量化方法，当公交机构的优先级在不同时段或扰动条件下发生变化时，缺乏运营灵活性。需要一种能够在运行时动态调整优先级权衡的方法。

Method: 提出偏好条件的公交信号优先控制器π(a|s,w)，在最小/最大绿灯时间和相位转换可行性约束下选择下一信号相位，通过偏好参数w在运行时调节公交优先强调与整体交通延误之间的权衡。基于IntersectionZoo平台实现，引入约束信号控制包装器，并扩展场景生成方法以解决训练过程中公交优先事件稀疏的问题。

Result: 单一学习条件策略在运行时偏好设置上跨越平滑的经验权衡前沿，优于定时控制和规则化基准方法，保持约束可行性。尾部延误诊断显示，在中等偏好设置下非公交外部性保持有限，但在高公交优先权重下会显著增加。

Conclusion: 偏好条件方法成功实现了运行时可调的公交信号优先控制，在保持约束满足的同时提供了灵活的优先级权衡能力，为公交机构应对不同运营条件提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Transit signal priority (TSP) requires balancing competing objectives: reducing bus delay while limiting adverse impacts on non-bus traffic and avoiding extreme waits for a subset of vehicles. Existing reinforcement-learning (RL) approaches to TSP typically encode transit-aware features (e.g., occupancy and schedule deviation) but optimize a fixed reward or fixed scalarization, which limits operational flexibility when agency priorities change across time-of-day or disruption conditions. We present a preference-conditioned TSP controller, $π(a \mid s,w)$, that selects the next signal phase under minimum/maximum green and transition-feasibility constraints and can be tuned at runtime via a preference parameter $w$ to trade off bus-priority emphasis against overall traffic delay without retraining. We implement this on top of IntersectionZoo by introducing a constrained signal-control/TSP wrapper, and we extend scenario generation with bus-prevalence augmentation and timetable-based bus insertion to address sparse transit-priority events during training. Experiments against fixed-time control, a rule-based TSP overlay, and fixed-weight PPO specialists show that a single learned conditioned policy spans a smooth empirical trade-off frontier across runtime preferences, outperforms fixed-time and rule-based baselines, and maintains constraint feasibility, while tail-delay diagnostics reveal that non-bus externalities remain limited for moderate preference settings but can increase substantially under high bus-priority weights. The source code of this work is available at https://github.com/urbanAIthi/morl-tsp.

</details>


### [19] [BearingNAS: Obtaining In-Sensor Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems for Bearings Using a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18287)
*Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Edoardo Ragusa,Paolo Gastaldo,Antonio Frisoli,Rodolfo Zunino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了BearingNAS框架，用于在极小内存预算（4-8 kiB RAM）下搜索并部署高精度（99.50%）的轴承故障诊断神经网络到传感器芯片内部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的神经网络架构搜索（NAS）依赖于昂贵的GPU资源，且搜索出的模型往往难以在极度资源受限的微控制器或智能传感器上部署。为了实现低成本、大规模的轴承故障诊断，有必要将计算负载从云端或外部处理器直接转移到传感器封装内部（in-sensor processing）。

Method: 提出了BearingNAS，一种硬件感知的神经架构搜索（HW-NAS）框架。该方法将搜索建模为针对极小内存预算（4-8 kiB RAM, 16-32 kiB Flash）的约束优化问题。为了摆脱对GPU的依赖，采用了轻量级的无导数搜索策略，并结合单一数据流搜索空间和衰减核增长公式，以防止参数爆炸。

Result: 在CWRU轴承基准数据集上评估了该框架，并针对三款意法半导体芯片（包括LSM6DSO16IS智能传感器处理单元）进行了优化。搜索过程完全在笔记本电脑CPU上运行，一小时内收敛。最终在ISPU上部署的最佳架构诊断准确率达到99.50%。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将机器学习工作负载转移到传感器封装内部的可行性，能够在极度受限的硬件预算下实现低成本、生产级的轴承故障诊断。

Abstract: This paper introduces BearingNAS, a Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search (HW-NAS) framework designed to shift the intelligence directly onto the sensor die via in-sensor processing. BearingNAS frames the search as a constrained optimization problem targeting extreme micro-budgets (4 to 8 kiB of RAM and 16 to 32 kiB of Flash). To eliminate the reliance on expensive discrete GPUs, we propose a lightweight, derivative-free search strategy paired with a single data-flow search space that leverages a decaying kernel growth formulation to prevent parameter explosion. We evaluate our framework on the Case Western Reserve University (CWRU) bearing benchmark, optimizing architectures for three STMicroelectronics targets: two commodity microcontrollers and the LSM6DSO16IS Intelligent Sensor Processing Unit (ISPU). Running entirely on a laptop CPU, the search converges in less than an hour. The resulting best in-sensor architecture achieves a highly competitive diagnostic accuracy of 99.50\% on the ISPU. These results demonstrate the viability of shifting the machine learning workload inside the sensor package, enabling low-cost, production-scale bearing fault diagnosis.

</details>


### [20] [Multi-Timescale Latent-Action DRL for Joint Optimization in Edge-Cloud Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18288)
*Vo Phi Son,Van-Dinh Nguyen,Ngoc Hung Nguyen,Trinh Van Chien,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双时间尺度多层深度强化学习的联合优化框架（2T-MDRL-LA），通过优化服务放置、计算委托、任务卸载和功率控制，在分层边缘云计算系统中实现了显著的延迟降低和资源利用率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分层边缘云计算（HECC）系统中，动态任务到达和异构资源条件下的负载不平衡会导致严重的排队延迟和低效的资源利用率，从而降低端到端延迟性能。现有方法未能有效解决这一挑战。

Method: 将联合服务放置、计算委托和功率控制（JSCP）问题分解为长期系统配置和短期资源分配两个子问题。提出双时间尺度多层深度强化学习框架（2T-MDRL-LA），并引入基于变分自编码器的潜在动作表示来压缩高维组合动作空间，联合优化服务放置、用户关联、计算委托、任务卸载和用户发射功率。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该框架能够有效适应动态网络条件，相比分支定界解法达到近最优性能。与不含计算委托的方案相比，平均端到端延迟降低高达20.8%，资源利用率提升13%；相比传统近端策略优化算法，收敛速度约快50%。

Conclusion: 所提出的2T-MDRL-LA框架通过双时间尺度分解和潜在动作空间表示，成功解决了HECC系统中复杂的联合优化问题，有效降低了端到端延迟并提升了资源利用效率，为动态网络环境下的资源管理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Load imbalance across edge and cloud layers degrades latency performance in hierarchical edge-cloud computing (HECC) systems under dynamic task arrivals and heterogeneous resources, leading to severe queuing delays and inefficient resource utilization. To address this challenge, we study a joint service placement, computational delegation, and power control (JSCP) problem to minimize the average end-to-end (e2e) latency. The resulting JSCP problem is a mixed-integer nonconvex and NP-hard optimization problem due to the strong coupling between discrete and continuous variables. To enable tractable optimization and stable system adaptation, we exploit the inherent difference in decision dynamics and decompose the problem into long-term system configuration and short-term resource allocation subproblems. Based on this formulation, we propose a two-timescale multi-layer deep reinforcement learning framework with a latent action space (2T-MDRL-LA) to jointly optimize service placement, user association, computational delegation, task offloading, and user transmit power. A latent action representation based on a variational autoencoder is introduced to efficiently compress the high-dimensional combinatorial action space. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively adapts to dynamic network conditions and achieves near-optimal performance compared to branch-and-bound solutions. It achieves up to a 20.8% reduction in average e2e latency and a 13% improvement in resource utilization over the scheme without the computational delegation, while converging approximately 50% faster than conventional proximal policy optimization.

</details>


### [21] [Towards Principled Continual Anomaly Detection: A Systematic Framework and Benchmark Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18289)
*Kamil Faber,Mateusz Smendowski,Roberto Corizzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对表格数据持续异常检测的系统化基准测试框架，能够从现有数据集中发现、过滤和排序任务，构建可复现的基准测试场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的持续异常检测基准测试在任务定义、过滤、排序和验证方面存在关键性问题。特别是在表格数据领域中，任务边界很少被明确给出，任意的划分可能创建出不可学习、冗余或过度可迁移的任务，从而掩盖了真实的持续学习行为。

Method: 提出了一个系统化框架，用于从现有表格异常检测数据集中设计可复现的基准测试场景。该框架包含三个关键步骤：（1）发现候选任务；（2）过滤不合适的任务；（3）推导出能够揭示多样化动态的原则性任务排序。

Result: 基于三个大规模网络安全异常检测数据集，成功构建了五个基准测试就绪的场景，包括单数据集和多数据集的CAD设置。

Conclusion: 该框架为表格数据持续异常检测提供了规范化的基准测试设计方法，解决了任务边界不明确和任意划分导致的评估不可靠问题，推动了CAD研究的标准化发展。

Abstract: Continual anomaly detection (CAD) studies how models can adapt to evolving data distributions while retaining performance on previously observed regimes. CAD benchmarks, however, depend critically on how tasks are defined, filtered, ordered, and validated. In tabular domains, task boundaries are rarely given, and arbitrary splits can create unlearnable, redundant, or overly transferable tasks that obscure genuine continual-learning behavior. To this end, we introduce a systematic framework for reproducible benchmark scenario design from existing tabular anomaly-detection datasets. The framework discovers candidate tasks, filters unsuitable tasks, and derives principled orderings that expose diverse dynamics. The framework allows us to deliver five benchmark-ready scenarios from three large-scale cybersecurity anomaly detection datasets, yielding both single-dataset and multi-dataset CAD settings.

</details>


### [22] [SechKAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with Hyperbolic Secant Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18290)
*Hoang-Thang Ta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SechKAN，一种基于双曲正割函数的Kolmogorov-Arnold网络架构，在保持与MLPs相当参数量的情况下，在函数拟合、PDE问题和图像分类任务上取得了优于MLPs和其他KAN变体的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs)因其在机器学习和科学计算任务中的有效性而受到越来越多的关注，为神经网络设计提供了新的范式。研究者希望开发更有效的KAN变体以提升性能。

Method: 提出SechKAN架构，采用双曲正割(sech)函数作为基函数，利用其平滑的钟形形式、局部化响应和稳定梯度的特性。通过使用1D线性变换来减少参数数量，使SechKAN的模型规模与多层感知机(MLPs)相当。

Result: 在函数拟合、PDE问题和图像分类任务(包括MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集)上的实验结果表明，SechKAN在保持相似参数数量的情况下，性能优于MLPs和其他KAN变体。运行时间方面，SechKAN优于其他KAN变体但略长于MLPs。

Conclusion: SechKAN成功地将双曲正割函数的优势融入KAN架构中，在多个任务上实现了性能提升，证明了其在神经网络设计中的有效性，为KAN架构的发展提供了新的方向。

Abstract: In recent years, Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) have attracted increasing attention due to their effectiveness in machine learning and scientific computing tasks, offering a new paradigm for neural network design. In this paper, we present SechKAN, a KAN architecture based on hyperbolic secant (sech) functions. The hyperbolic secant basis is used for its smooth bell-shaped form, localized responses, and stable gradients. We employ 1D linear transformations to reduce the number of parameters, allowing SechKAN to remain comparable to multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) in model size. Experimental results indicate the effectiveness of SechKAN in function fitting, PDE problems, and image classification tasks on benchmark datasets, including MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100. SechKAN achieves superior performance compared to MLPs and other KAN variants while maintaining a similar number of parameters. However, its running time, while better than that of other KAN variants, is slightly longer than that of MLPs.

</details>


### [23] [Dual-domain fused LSTM modeling for efficient time-dependent reliability analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18291)
*Yixin Zhang,Mingyang Li,Zichao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种双域融合长短期记忆（DDF-LSTM）模型，用于高效准确地进行时变可靠性分析，解决了传统代理模型难以处理时不变随机变量与时变随机过程复杂交互的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变可靠性分析对工程系统长期安全至关重要，但传统代理模型方法难以有效整合时不变随机变量并捕捉其与时变随机过程间的复杂交互作用。

Method: 提出DDF-LSTM模型，构建新型网络架构联合处理时变与时不变域信息：将时不变变量嵌入初始隐藏状态，引入全连接层将LSTM输出和时不变变量映射至最终输出空间；设计改进损失函数以增强模型对最小响应的敏感性，提高失效概率估计精度。训练后可高效进行蒙特卡洛模拟估算时变失效概率。

Result: 所提方法能有效捕捉随机变量、随机过程及极限状态函数时变行为间的依赖关系。四个案例研究验证了该方法在计算效率和预测精度方面的显著提升。

Conclusion: DDF-LSTM模型为时变可靠性分析提供了一种高效准确的新方法，在计算成本最小化的同时实现了较高的失效概率估计精度，具有良好的工程应用价值。

Abstract: Time-dependent reliability analysis is crucial for ensuring the long-term safety and performance of engineering systems under uncertainties. However, traditional surrogate model methods often struggle to incorporate time-independent random variables and capture their complex interactions with time-dependent stochastic processes. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes a dual-domain fused long short-term memory (DDF-LSTM) model for efficient and accurate time-dependent reliability analysis. A novel network architecture is developed to jointly process information from both time-dependent and time-independent domains. Specifically, the time-independent variables are embedded into the initial hidden states, and a fully connected layer is introduced to map both LSTM outputs and time-independent variables into the final output space. Furthermore, an improved loss function is designed to emphasize the model's sensitivity to minimum responses, thereby improving the precision of failure probability estimation. The proposed method effectively captures the dependencies among random variables, stochastic processes, and the temporal behavior of limit state functions. Once trained, the DDF-LSTM model enables efficient Monte Carlo simulation to estimate time-dependent failure probabilities with minimal computational cost. Four case studies validate the proposed method's enhanced computational efficiency and predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [24] [Reliability Scales Inversely: Bigger Models Compound Mistakes Faster via a Hidden Auto-Regressive Risk Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18292)
*Kushal Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示了语言模型 Scaling Law 的一个关键缺陷：规模扩大提升能力却削弱可靠性。模型在生成低概率 token 后会产生“滚雪球”效应，导致幻觉问题，且该故障模式对现有自我监测方法不可见，但可通过方差控制策略有效缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为语言模型的问题源于“知识缺口”，主张通过增加数据、检索或模型规模来解决。然而，这种观点忽略了自回归生成机制内在的风险：模型在生成低概率 token 后，会将其作为既定事实进行条件化，导致错误累积和放大。研究旨在揭示 Scaling Law 下这一被忽视的故障模式。

Method: 提出通过每个位置的分歧度 $\delta = \log p_M - \log p_O$ 来追踪模型生成过程，与同系列的更强 oracle 模型对比。利用二阶矩分解，将分歧度分解为偏置项 $\mathrm{KL}(p_M \| p_O)^2$ 和风险项 $\mathrm{Var}[\delta]$。通过实验分析 scaling 对这两项指标的影响，并设计 on-policy 的固定 KL 方差收缩策略来验证风险机制的因果性。

Result: 发现四项核心结论： Scaling 使知识缺口下降约6倍，但知识退化增长 11-39倍；幻觉发生时，模型的感知不确定性快速下降，而 oracle 参照的风险持续高企，形成“自信但岌岌可危”的风险状态，将连续幻觉关联度提升 69%；on-policy 固定-KL 方差收缩策略可将网页验证的幻觉减少 35-74%；该风险状态结构性规避自我监测，依赖 $p_M$ 的检测器（如语义熵）在风险分支上的触发率降低约 30%，而该分支包含近 4 倍的幻觉。

Conclusion: 大规模语言模型会加速错误的滚雪球效应，这一故障模式具有主导性、自持续、因果性和对模型本身不可见四大特征。研究揭示了单纯依赖 scale 无法解决的可靠性危机，并提供了针对性的缓解方案。

Abstract: As language models scale, answers start truer but degrade faster: scaling buys capability but erodes reliability. The knowledge-gap account - more data, retrieval, or scale - misses an auto-regressive risk residual that scale sharpens: the model commits to a low-probability token, conditions on it as established, and snowballs. We track this through per-position disagreement $δ= \log p_M - \log p_O$ against a stronger same-family oracle, whose second moment splits exactly into bias$^2$ $\mathrm{KL}(p_M \,\|\, p_O)^2$ and risk $\mathrm{Var}[δ]$. We present four findings: (i) under scaling, the knowledge gap falls $\approx$$6\times$ while knowledge degradation grows $11$-$39\times$; (ii) at a fabrication, felt uncertainty $H(p_M)$ relaxes quickly while oracle-referenced risk persists up to $17\times$ longer, leaving a confident-but-precarious risk regime that bridges consecutive fabrications ($+69\%$ at $14$B); (iii) this regime is causal - an on-policy, fixed-$\mathrm{KL}$ variance contraction cuts web-verified hallucination by $35$-$74\%$ across three model families; and, (iv) it structurally evades self-monitoring, with $p_M$-only detectors (e.g. semantic entropy) firing $\approx$$30\%$ less ($p<10^{-16}$) on the risky branch holding nearly $4\times$ more fabrications. Bigger models snowball mistakes faster, through a failure mode that is dominant, self-perpetuating, causal and invisible to the model itself.

</details>


### [25] [One Student, Many Teachers: Multi-Task On-Policy Distillation via Soft-Prompt Privileged Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18293)
*Yingzi Ma,Zichen Zhu,Ming Jiang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Prompt-Teacher (PT)方法，通过仅训练可学习的软提示来构建教师模型，在保持学生模型表示几何结构的同时，实现了高效的知识蒸馏，在多任务场景下显著优于传统顺序微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在策略自蒸馏（OPSD）方法存在两个主要问题：（1）在输入端注入特权上下文会导致事后合理化问题；（2）微调权重会累积漂移并导致跨任务遗忘。需要一种既能保留学生模型表示结构，又能避免灾难性遗忘的教师模型构建方法。

Method: 提出Prompt-Teacher (PT)方法：教师模型与学生模型的唯一区别是一个可学习的软提示。在冻结主干网络的情况下，基于(x, y_gold)对训练软提示，生成任务特定的教师模型，完全保留学生模型的表示几何结构。在多任务场景下，通过将合并语料库中的每个样本路由到相应的软提示教师，使单个学生能够并行吸收K个教师的知识。推理时丢弃所有提示。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B-Base和Phi-4-mini-instruct上的四个任务（Science、Tool Use、Biology、Math）上验证：单任务变体（OPD with PT teacher）在训练参数量减少数个数量级的情况下，性能达到或超过全量微调；多任务变体在Qwen3-1.7B-Base上取得最佳平均分56.2，同时保持通用能力基准测试性能，而顺序SFT在两方面均出现退化。

Conclusion: Prompt-Teacher方法通过仅训练软提示成功解决了传统OPSD方法的核心问题，在单任务和多任务场景下均表现出色，实现了参数高效的知识传递且避免了灾难性遗忘，为大语言模型的持续学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) teaches large language models new skills through a teacher that shares the student's backbone and supervises its own rollouts. Existing teachers either inject privileged context at the input -- inducing post-hoc rationalization -- or fine-tune weights, accumulating drift and forgetting across tasks. We propose \method, whose teacher differs from the student only by a learnable soft prompt: trained on $(x, y_\text{gold})$ pairs with the backbone frozen, the prompt yields a task-specific teacher that preserves the student's exact representational geometry. \method\ extends naturally to multi-task settings by routing each example in a merged corpus to its corresponding soft-prompt teacher, allowing a single student to absorb knowledge from $K$ teachers in parallel; at inference, all prompts are discarded. On Qwen3-1.7B-Base and Phi-4-mini-instruct across four tasks (Science, Tool Use, Biology, Math), the single-task variant (OPD with a PT teacher) matches or exceeds full fine-tuning while training orders of magnitude fewer parameters, and the multi-task variant achieves the best overall average ($56.2$ on Qwen3-1.7B-Base) while preserving general-capability benchmarks -- in contrast to sequential SFT, which degrades both.

</details>


### [26] [Uncertainty Quantification for AI-Driven Crash Simulation Surrogates: A Comparative Study of Monte Carlo Dropout and Deep Ensemble on Open-Source Bumper Beam Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18294)
*Sudeep Chavare*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较了Monte Carlo Dropout和深度集成两种不确定性量化方法在汽车碰撞仿真代理模型中的表现，发现通过PhysicsNeMo的concrete dropout功能可实现无超参数调优的不确定性估计，挑战了深度集成作为默认最佳方案的传统观念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习代理模型在工程产品开发中的应用日益增加，但在安全关键工作流程中的采用受到限制，主要原因在于点预测缺乏不确定性估计，无法告知工程师模型何时不可信，这导致模型在安全关键场景中的可信度评估存在困难。

Method: 基于NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo构建开源代理模型管道，采用concrete dropout技术自动学习dropout率，消除手动超参数调优需求；以汽车碰撞仿真中的钢制保险杠冲击问题为基准，在相同的保留仿真数据上系统对比Monte Carlo Dropout和深度集成两种不确定性量化方法。

Result: 研究揭示了准确性和校准性之间的基本权衡关系，挑战了深度集成作为代理模型不确定性量化默认金标准的常见假设；证明了在完全开源的工程工作流程中，能够以远低于集成方法的计算成本实现良好校准的、无需超参数调优的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 通过PhysicsNeMo的concrete dropout功能，可以在计算成本大幅降低的情况下实现可靠的不确定性量化，为安全关键工程应用提供了可行的开源解决方案，同时为代理模型不确定性估计方法的选择提供了新的见解和权衡考量。

Abstract: Machine learning surrogate models are increasingly being explored in engineering product development to augment simulation-driven design, offering near-instantaneous predictions that complement computationally expensive high-fidelity analyses. However, a critical gap limits their adoption in safety-critical workflows: a point prediction without an accompanying uncertainty estimate cannot tell an engineer when the model should not be trusted. This work presents a systematic, head-to-head comparison of two widely used uncertainty quantification approaches -- Monte Carlo Dropout and Deep Ensembles -- applied to an open-source surrogate pipeline built on NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo. A key contribution is the use of concrete dropout, a built-in PhysicsNeMo capability that eliminates the dropout rate as a manual hyperparameter by learning it end-to-end during training, directly addressing the most common criticism of Monte Carlo Dropout-based uncertainty quantification. Automotive crash simulation is used as the application domain, with a steel bumper beam impact problem serving as the benchmark. Both methods are evaluated on identical held-out simulations and compared on point accuracy, uncertainty band calibration, and computational cost. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between accuracy and calibration that challenges the common assumption that deep ensembles are the default gold standard for surrogate uncertainty quantification. The findings demonstrate that well-calibrated, hyperparameter-free uncertainty estimates are achievable within a fully open-source engineering workflow at a fraction of the computational cost of ensemble approaches.

</details>


### [27] [On the Limits of Support-Preserving Alignment and Bounded Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18295)
*Aryan Dutt,Rui Mao,Anupam Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对齐方案与有界安全过滤器结合是否能使大语言模型的有害行为概率降为零。理论和实验结果表明，在特定约束下，有界过滤无法完全消除有害输出，存在一个持续的经验性危害下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，基于偏好的对齐无法完全消除有害行为，外部过滤在最坏情况下计算困难。然而，实际的对齐流程是否能在很大程度上保留内部表示的同时完全消除有害行为，还是仅仅抑制其最可见的形式，仍不清楚。

Method: 形式化了保留支持的对齐算子和有界过滤算法（在黑盒、白盒和统计查询访问下），并分析其逼近理想消除器（移除所有有害质量）的能力。基于该框架，提供了计算和信息论论证。实证评估了多种开源和托管LLM在不同过滤器和查询预算下的表现，使用了网络安全场景和PKU-SafeRLHF中的对抗性提示。

Result: 理论和论证表明，在给定约束下，有界过滤可能无法消除基模型分布支持的所有有害输出。实验结果显示，估计的有害输出率随过滤计算量增加而下降，但始终在零以上趋于平稳，表明存在一个持续的经验性危害下限。

Conclusion: 对齐方案与有界安全过滤器的结合虽然能降低有害输出率，但无法将其降至零。存在一个经验性的危害下限，暗示有害行为可能以潜在形式持续存在，而不仅是最可见形式的被抑制。

Abstract: We study whether alignment schemes that reshape a base model's output distribution, combined with bounded safety filters, can drive the probability of harmful behavior to zero in modern large language models. Recent research suggests that harmful behaviors can persist under preference-based alignment and that external filtering can be computationally hard in the worst case, but it remains unclear whether practical alignment pipelines that largely preserve internal representations can eliminate harmful behavior entirely rather than merely suppressing its most visible forms. We formalize this setting using support-preserving alignment operators together with bounded filtering algorithms under black-box, white-box, and statistical-query access, and analyze their ability to approximate an ideal eliminator that removes all harmful mass. Building on this framework, we provide computational and information-theoretic arguments indicating that, under these constraints, bounded filtering may fail to eliminate all harmful outputs supported by the base model's distribution. To evaluate these limits empirically, we analyze a range of state-of-the-art open-weight and hosted LLMs accessed via OpenRouter under bounded black-box, white-box, and statistical-query filters on adversarial prompts drawn from curated cybersecurity scenarios and PKU-SafeRLHF. Across models, filter classes, and query budgets, the estimated harmful-output rate decreases with additional filtering compute but consistently plateaus above zero, suggesting a persistent empirical harm floor.

</details>


### [28] [A Better Start for Language Models: Domain-Conditional Position Offsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18302)
*Ye Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出域条件位置偏移方法，通过在序列起始位置添加一个可学习向量来缓解自回归语言模型的冷启动惩罚，在几乎不增加参数和延迟的情况下显著降低域内困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型在序列开始阶段准确率最低，由于缺乏上下文而被迫依赖通用的预训练先验，这种冷启动惩罚具有域依赖性。

Method: 提出域条件位置偏移方法，即在序列起始位置的嵌入激活中添加一个单一的可学习向量，同时保持所有模型权重冻结。该偏移向量仅需约100个文档、数分钟即可完成训练，且切换域时无需额外的序列状态。

Result: 在410M至8B参数的Mamba、GPT-NeoX和Llama模型上，域内困惑度最高降低27%，效果在70B模型上持续存在。相比匹配的对数几率偏置校正（最多仅7.9%），该方法效果显著更优，证明偏移通过模型状态传播而非仅重新校准输出先验。虽然调优的LoRA能达到更低困惑度，但参数量多2-3个数量级；软提示则需增加序列位置。在检索重排序和域分类等依赖早期token的任务上有改进，对少样本推理保持不变。

Conclusion: 域条件位置偏移并非最强的适配器，而是一种轻量级、可热切换的工具，适用于短期域内评分和校准任务。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models are least accurate at the beginning of a sequence, where little context forces reliance on a generic pretraining prior. We show that this cold-start penalty is domain dependent and reduce it with a domain-conditional position offset: a single learned vector added to the embedding activation at the first sequence positions while all model weights remain frozen. The offset trains in minutes on roughly one hundred documents, switches between domains without added sequence state, and has no measurable latency overhead. Across eight Mamba, GPT-NeoX, and Llama models spanning 410M to 8B parameters, it reduces held-out in-domain perplexity by up to 27%; the effect persists at 70B, and one position captures most of the benefit. A matched, converged direct logit-bias correction reaches at most only 7.9% and leaves later-token loss unchanged, showing that the offset propagates through model state rather than merely recalibrating the output prior. A tuned LoRA reaches lower perplexity but uses two to three orders of magnitude more parameters and an active low-rank weight path, while soft prompts add sequence positions. With wrong-domain controls, offsets improve retrieval reranking and domain classification when decisions depend on early in-domain tokens, For the few-shot reasoning whose signal occurs later, the results maintains unchanged. Position-aware prefill application also help generation tasks, whereas naive application at every cached decoding step causes repetition. The offset is therefore not the strongest adapter, but a lightweight, hot switchable tool for short in-domain scoring and calibration.

</details>


### [29] [TD-DPO: Difference-Aware Preference Optimization for Mitigating Sycophancy in Clinical Autism Intervention Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18304)
*Shuzhong Lai,Junhong Lai,Chenxi Li,Qing Zhou,Haifeng Li,Gang Pan,Lin Yao,Yueming Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出TD-DPO方法，通过令牌级差异优化，在减少大语言模型谄媚行为的同时保持自闭症干预能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的谄媚行为会增加自闭症儿童干预对话的安全风险。现有监督微调方法仅依赖正例难以有效纠正失败模式，而传统的序列级偏好优化容易过度更新无关令牌，导致干预能力下降。研究者观察到谄媚行为通常局限于模型响应中的有限片段，这为更精细的优化提供了可能。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：1）MEDA策略，构建受控、稳定、最小编辑的偏好对；2）TD-DPO方法，上权重选中响应与拒绝响应之间的差异令牌，同时下权重共享令牌，从而抑制背景漂移。该方法实现了令牌级的差异直接偏好优化，避免了序列级方法的过度更新问题。

Result: 在多个骨干模型和评估器上的广泛实验表明，TD-DPO在离线设置下实现了谄媚缓解与干预能力保持之间更好的平衡。

Conclusion: TD-DPO作为一种实用的对齐方法，在自闭症干预场景中展现出良好潜力，能够有效平衡模型安全性（谄媚行为缓解）与功能性（干预能力保持）之间的矛盾。

Abstract: The sycophancy of large language models can increase the safety risk in intervention dialogue for autistic children. Supervised fine-tuning can somewhat reduce sycophancy, but relying solely on positive examples is often insufficient to identify and correct failure patterns. We observe that sycophancy behaviors can often be localized to a limited span within the model response. In this regime, sequence-level preference optimization can over-update preference-irrelevant tokens and degrade intervention ability. To address this, we propose the \textbf{M}inimal \textbf{E}dit \textbf{D}ata \textbf{A}ugmentation (MEDA) strategy to construct controlled, stable, minimal edit preference pairs and \textbf{T}oken-level \textbf{D}ifference \textbf{D}irect \textbf{P}reference \textbf{O}ptimization (TD-DPO), which upweights difference tokens between chosen and rejected responses while downweighting shared tokens to suppress background drift. Extensive experiments across multiple backbones and evaluators show that TD-DPO achieves a better trade-off between sycophancy mitigation and intervention ability retention in our offline settings, highlighting its potential as a practical alignment approach for autism intervention.

</details>


### [30] [The Information Shadow: Measuring Structural Limits on What Language Models Can Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18305)
*Priyansh Srivastava,Romit Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“信息阴影”概念，指出语言模型存在无法通过规模扩展解决的三类结构性限制：(I) 语言无法表达的结构，(II) 从训练分布统计上不可识别的函数，(III) 可表示但梯度训练无法到达的函数，并通过三类探针实验验证了这些限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的知识限制并非数据覆盖不足，而是从文本学习的结构性属性。研究者旨在识别并形式化“信息阴影”——即无论模型规模如何扩大，仅基于文本训练的模型都无法获取的现象区域，为理解语言模型的根本能力边界提供理论框架。

Method: 提出三类探针分别验证三种限制类型：Type I采用语言压缩残差（LCR），对比文本学习器与全信号学习器在损失编码下的表现差距；Type II使用反事实区分测试（CDT），训练模型于与两条不兼容规则一致的数据，观察归纳偏好在反事实上的表现及少量歧义数据的影响；Type III设计盆地逃逸映射（BEM），展示一个可表示函数在标准训练下从未被到达，但可从邻近初始化瞬间到达。每个实验均设置控制组排除容量或模态伪影。

Result: Type I中，文本学习者在可计算表达上限处停滞，而全信号学习者在数据增加300倍后差距保持平稳，说明缺陷源自信道而非训练；Type II中，模型在反事实上的行为由归纳偏好决定，5%的歧义数据可将学习规则双向引导至目标（r=±1.0，p<1e-10）；Type III中，一个100%可表示的函数在标准训练下从未被到达，而宽度扩展无效（p=1.6×10^-14）。

Conclusion: 信息阴影揭示了语言模型的根本能力边界，这些边界源于语言信道、统计不可识别性和优化动态，而非数据量或模型规模。研究对基准设计、能力审计和不确定性评估提出了新要求，建议采用阴影感知的方法。研究者发布了探针套件以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Some limits on what language models know are not gaps in data coverage but structural properties of learning from text. We introduce the information shadow: the region of phenomena that a text-trained learner cannot acquire regardless of scale, comprising (I) structures language cannot express, (II) functions that are statistically non-identifiable from the training distribution, and (III) functions that are representable but unreachable by gradient-based training. We give each type a probe that is decisive because the premise of the shadow is, in that setting, provable. For Type I, Language Compression Residuals compare a text learner, which sees only a lossy text-like encoding of the signal, against a full-signal learner, which sees the underlying signal directly. The text learner sits at a computable expressibility ceiling while the full-signal learner pulls away by a gap that stays flat across 300x more data, so the deficit is a property of the channel, not of training. For Type II, the Counterfactual Distinction Test trains models on data exactly consistent with two incompatible rules. Across a provable string task and a language-like agreement task, behavior on counterfactuals is set by the model's inductive bias, while 5% disambiguating data steers the learned rule bidirectionally to either target (r = +/-1.0, p < 1e-10). For Type III, Basin Escape Mapping exhibits a function that is representable at 100% (by hand construction) yet reached 0% of the time by standard training and instantly from a nearby initialization, with width scaling providing no help (p = 1.6 x 10^-14). Each effect is isolated by a control that rules out a capacity or modality artifact. We release the probe suite and discuss implications for benchmark design, capability auditing, and shadow-aware uncertainty.

</details>


### [31] [Gradient-Energy Guided Block-Wise Perturbations for Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18306)
*Zhen Huang,Jiaxin Deng,Junbiao Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GEAR-SAM，一种改进的锐度感知最小化方法，通过维护梯度平方的指数移动平均来动态分配扰动预算，在多种任务上提升了泛化性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的SAM方法在分配全局扰动预算时，根据瞬时的小批量梯度范数隐式地分配到各个参数块，这种分配方式存在噪声问题，且无法反映参数块在整个训练过程中累积的敏感度信息。

Method: 提出GEAR-SAM方法，维护梯度平方的指数移动平均(EMA)作为轻量级的、与曲率相关的敏感度信号，通过闭式约束优化来分配固定的SAM预算。该方法保持全局SAM半径，不需要Hessian-向量乘积或显式的Fisher估计，仅在SAM基础上增加标量状态。

Result: 在图像分类、迁移学习、噪声标签学习和分区研究等实验中，GEAR-SAM在各种架构和任务上都展现出了更好的泛化性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GEAR-SAM为锐度感知优化提供了动态视角：固定的扰动预算应当随着功能网络块敏感度在训练过程中的演化而重新分配。这种方法在保持计算效率的同时，通过更合理的预算分配策略改善了模型性能。

Abstract: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) improves generalization by minimizing the worst-case loss in a local parameter neighborhood. Standard SAM implicitly allocates its global perturbation budget across parameter blocks according to instantaneous minibatch gradient norms. Such an allocation can be noisy and may not reflect the sensitivity that blocks accumulate throughout training. We propose Gradient-Energy Adaptive Radius SAM (GEAR-SAM), which maintains an exponential moving average (EMA) of squared block gradients as a lightweight, curvature-related sensitivity signal and allocates the fixed SAM budget through a closed-form constrained optimization. GEAR-SAM preserves the global SAM radius, requires no Hessian-vector products or explicit Fisher estimation, and adds only scalar state beyond SAM. Experiments on image classification, transfer learning, noisy-label learning, and partition studies demonstrate improved generalization and robustness across architectures and tasks. More broadly, GEAR-SAM provides a dynamic view of sharpness-aware optimization: a fixed perturbation budget should be redistributed as the sensitivity of functional network blocks evolves during training.

</details>

*David Gómez-Guillén,Mireia Diaz,Josep Lluis Arcos,Jesús Cerquides*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体校准方法，用于灰盒仿真模型的参数优化。该方法在无约束校准中表现显著优于贝叶斯优化和Nelder-Mead，在约束校准中达到可比性能，且模型评估次数更少。约束处理通过自然语言提示实现，无需额外建模机制，但增加了单次迭代的推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灰盒仿真模型的校准是一个受约束的优化问题，面临模型评估代价高昂、参数空间高维以及需遵守合理性约束等挑战。传统优化器（如Nelder-Mead）易于部署但在约束下样本效率低；现代贝叶斯优化方法虽然评估次数少，但约束处理需要复杂的建模机制。即使仿真代码完全可用，多参数的联合效应仍难以解析预测，因此需要一种既能高效搜索又能简便处理约束的新方法。

Method: 作者引入了一种智能体校准方法，将大语言模型作为优化器。约束条件直接以自然语言形式嵌入系统提示中，使模型在优化过程中自然地遵守这些约束。该方法被应用于肛门癌仿真模型的校准任务，分别在无约束和临床约束两种场景下，与Nelder-Mead和贝叶斯优化进行了系统性的对比评估。

Result: 在无约束校准场景下，智能体方法取得了比贝叶斯优化和Nelder-Mead更低的最佳误差，同时所需的模型评估次数更少。在有临床约束的校准场景下，智能体方法达到了与贝叶斯优化可比的误差水平，两者均优于Nelder-Mead。这些性能优势的代价是每次迭代的推理时间增加。此外，该方法生成的每次迭代推理过程使得搜索过程可审计、可解释。

Conclusion: 智能体校准方法在显著减少模型评估次数的前提下实现了具有竞争力的性能，约束处理通过简单的文本规范即可实现，无需面向建模者的额外建模机制。主要的权衡在于增加了每次迭代的推理成本，这使其特别适用于仿真时间占主导地位的场景。除了性能优势外，该方法提供的逐次迭代推理使搜索过程可审计、可解释，便于向第三方审查和论证其决策依据。

Abstract: Calibration of grey-box simulation models is a constrained optimization problem in which model evaluations are expensive, the parameter space can be high-dimensional, and the search must respect plausibility constraints. Although the simulation code is fully available to the analyst, the joint effect of multiple parameters remains difficult to predict analytically. Classical optimizers such as Nelder--Mead (NM) are simple to deploy but sample-inefficient, particularly under constraints. Modern Bayesian Optimization methods achieve competitive solutions with far fewer evaluations but require non-trivial modeling machinery for constraint handling. We introduce an agentic calibration method in which a large language model acts as the optimizer, with constraints incorporated as a plain-language section of the system prompt. We evaluate the agentic method, NM, and Bayesian Optimization (BO) on an anal cancer simulation model under both unconstrained and clinically constrained calibration. Under unconstrained calibration, the agentic method achieves substantially lower best error than BO and NM, while requiring fewer model evaluations. Under constrained calibration, the agentic method reaches comparable error levels and both outperform NM. These results are obtained at the cost of increased inference time per iteration. Agentic calibration achieves competitive performance with substantially fewer model evaluations, and constraint handling is essentially free at the modeller-facing interface through simple textual specifications rather than additional modelling machinery. The main trade-off lies in increased per-iteration inference cost, making the approach particularly suitable when simulation time dominates. Beyond performance, the per-iteration rationale makes the search auditable and explainable, so its decisions can be scrutinised and justified to third parties.

</details>


### [33] [Spatio-Temporal Prediction of Unsteady Airfoil Aerodynamics Using Augmented Graph Neural Ordinary Differential Equations with Exogenous Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18309)
*Henrik Lange,Reik Thormann,Philipp Bekemeyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GNODE的方法，通过结合图神经网络与增强神经常微分方程，实现了对俯仰翼型表面力的时序稳定预测，解决了自回归GNN在长期预测中的误差累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空器飞行中存在阵风、湍流和流固耦合等非定常气动现象，这些效应对设计优化和适航认证至关重要。现有的工业标准CFD方法（如非定常雷诺平均Navier-Stokes方程求解或线性频域方法）要么计算成本高昂，要么受限于线性假设等约束。机器学习方法在训练后能快速计算非线性关系，适合作为代理模型，但自回归图神经网络存在误差累积导致时间推进不稳定的问题。

Method: 提出GNODE（图神经常微分方程）方法，将图神经网络与增强神经常微分方程相结合，在离散空间域上操作。通过增加潜在维度来增强模型的表达能力和准确性，以捕捉潜在的历史效应。该方法能够处理带有外生输入的非线性时空系统。

Result: GNODE方法相比自回归GNN基线，在包含跨音速激波、瞬态行为和动态非线性的俯仰翼型仿真数据集上，提供了时间上更稳定、空间上更平滑、整体更准确的表面力预测结果。增强潜在维度进一步提升了模型的表达能力和准确性。

Conclusion: GNODE方法成功解决了自回归GNN的误差累积和不稳定性问题，为建模带有外生输入的非线性时空系统提供了一种有效的途径，在非定常气动力学建模方面展现出良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: Unsteady aerodynamic phenomena, such as gusts, turbulence, and fluid-structure interactions affect an aircraft during flight. For design, optimisation and certification, it is indispensable to quantify such unsteady aerodynamic effects. Industry-standard computational fluid dynamics methods, such as solving the unsteady Reynolds-averaged Navier-Stokes equations or the linearized frequency domain method, are either computationally expensive or restricted by assumptions like linearity. Once trained, machine learning methods are capable of computing non-linear relationships very fast, making them suitable as surrogate models. By autoregressively applying graph neural networks (GNNs), operating on a discretised spatial domain, spatio-temporal predictions can be made. However, autoregressive GNNs suffer from error accumulation leading to unstable rollouts over time. Here we show that combining GNNs with augmented Neural Ordinary Differential Equations yields temporally stable predictions of the surface forces on a pitching airfoil. We found that our approach, called GNODE, based on Graph Neural Ordinary Differential Equations, provides temporally more stable, spatially smoother, and overall more accurate results than an autoregressive GNN baseline. Tests are conducted on a dataset consisting of a simulations of a pitching airfoil, including transonic shocks, transient behaviour and dynamic non-linearities. Augmenting GNODEs with additional latent dimensions improves the expressivity and accuracy by capturing underlying history effects. The developed method demonstrates an approach that is suitable to model non-linear spatio-temporal systems with exogenous inputs.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: Interactive Training 2为可审计的人类和智能体引导训练提供了可复用的基础框架，实现了训练过程的交互式控制，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Experiment trackers show how training is progressing, but changing a live run still usually requires trainer-specific code. We present Interactive Training 2, an open-source control plane for steering training through a shared protocol. Training applications declare which settings and actions they expose, humans and automated controllers submit requests through the same interface, and the training loop validates and applies them at safe control points. A customized Aim workspace combines live metrics and controls with a chronological record of requests and outcomes. We demonstrate the system across five NLP and reinforcement-learning workflows. The released code and traces provide a reusable foundation for auditable human- and agent-guided training.

</details>
