*Abdallah Khemais*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对神经网络计算图上的穷举式干预操作（如激活补丁扫描、电路发现搜索、系统性消融研究），在支持精确失效传播的反应式图引擎上进行了完整的成本分析。理论分析了扫描加速比的渐近性质、持续变异序列的精确成本、以及反向传播的镜像特性，并通过NeuroDSL验证了所有理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络计算图上的穷举式干预操作（激活补丁扫描、电路发现搜索、系统性消融研究）需要在每个候选位置变异计算图，其成本主要由每次变异后的重计算决定。现有的独立完全重计算方式效率低下，需要理解反应式图引擎上的精确成本特性和优化潜力。

Method: 在支持精确失效传播（仅触及变异节点的下游锥）的反应式图引擎上，建立了穷举式干预工作负载的完整成本分析框架。首先分析穷举扫描相对于独立完全重计算的聚合加速比，证明其与层权重随深度变化的Karamata指数q相关；其次证明持续变异序列的精确成本，分析不同插入顺序的影响；最后证明反向传播的前向局部性镜像特性。所有理论结果在Julia实现的NeuroDSL反应式图引擎上进行了验证。

Result: 主要发现包括：（1）聚合加速比并非普适常数，当权重随深度以Karamata指数q正则变化时，加速比收敛到(q+2)/(q+1)（权重集中近输出端）或q+2（权重集中近输入端），仅在深度均匀时恢复为2；实际预测上限约为1.79。（2）持续变异序列的交错成本超过孤立和，存在可比较位置对的精确过度计数，有插入顺序的闭式极值；批处理应用顺序无关且满足次可加性。（3）反向传播具有前向局部性的镜像特性，在无长跳跃连接架构下会将聚合加速比坍缩至1。（4）NeuroDSL验证显示所有测量收敛至预测极限，18个每嫁接顺序成本及批处理总和以零容差匹配闭式结果。

Conclusion: 本文为神经网络计算图上的穷举式干预操作建立了精确的成本理论框架，揭示了加速比的渐近性质、持续变异的成本特性以及反向传播的局部性镜像。这些理论结果不仅解释了现有观察到的性能上界，还为优化此类工作负载提供了指导。NeuroDSL的实现和验证证明了反应式图引擎在支持精确失效传播方面的有效性。

Abstract: Exhaustive site-by-site interventions on a neural network's computational graph -- activation-patching sweeps, circuit-discovery searches, systematic ablation studies -- mutate the graph at every candidate site, and their cost is dominated by recomputation after each mutation. On a reactive graph engine whose invalidation provably touches exactly the downstream cone of a mutated node, we give a complete cost accounting for such workloads. First, the aggregate speedup of an exhaustive sweep over independent full recomputations is not a universal constant: if per-layer weight varies regularly with depth at Karamata index q, the ratio converges to (q+2)/(q+1) when weight concentrates near the output and to q+2 near the input, recovering 2 only in the depth-uniform case; a wall-clock corollary predicts a ceiling of about 1.79, below 2, until interpreter overhead is compiled away. Second, we prove the exact cost of a sequence of persistent mutations, never undone between insertions: the interleaved cost exceeds the isolated sum by an exact overcount summed over comparable site pairs, with closed-form extremes over insertion orders, while batched application is order-independent and sub-additive, costing exactly the union of the sites' cones plus the fresh nodes. Third, we prove the exact mirror of forward locality for the backward pass, showing it collapses the aggregate speedup to 1 under backpropagation on architectures without long skip connections. Every identity is validated on NeuroDSL, a reactive graph engine in Julia: measured sweep ratios converge to the predicted limits under four cost profiles; the training-mode ratio collapses to 1 at the predicted rate; and all 18 per-graft sequential costs and the batched total match the closed forms at zero tolerance across three insertion orders.

</details>


### [36] [Dynamic Loss Balancing for Joint SOH and RUL Prediction of Lithium-Ion Batteries via a Rotary SOH-Injected Prior Battery Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18329)
*Shuhao Chen,Tianyu Shi,Yiwen Huang,Chengyi Tu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了RoSIP-Batt框架，解决了锂离子电池健康状态(SOH)和剩余使用寿命(RUL)联合预测中的任务异方差问题，通过贝叶斯多任务目标和同方差不确定性加权机制，在多个数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂离子电池管理系统对加速电气化至关重要，但SOH和RUL的联合预测受到任务异方差性的严重阻碍。传统的多任务学习框架无法平衡SOH估计的有界低方差噪声与长期RUL预测的无界非线性扩展不确定性之间的矛盾。

Method: 提出RoSIP-Batt统一协同估计框架：(1)采用贝叶斯多任务目标，引入同方差不确定性加权机制动态缩放任务特定梯度；(2)使用解耦双分类token和逐维度门控融合机制，通过梯度分离操作防止高方差RUL更新破坏稳定SOH表示空间；(3)将旋转位置编码(RoPE)融入共享Transformer骨干以建模平移不变的相对时序特征；(4)将中间SOH估计直接注入RUL回归头作为物理退化先验。

Result: 在NASA、MIT-Stanford和HUST数据集上显著优于现有最先进基线方法：在NASA数据集上将SOH估计误差降至1.994% MAE，在Stanford数据集上将RUL预测误差限制在62.85个循环周期。

Conclusion: RoSIP-Batt作为高度泛化、计算高效的解决方案，适用于实时嵌入式电池管理系统部署，为锂离子电池SOH和RUL的联合预测提供了有效的技术途径。

Abstract: The deployment of reliable lithium-ion battery management systems is crucial for accelerating electrification, yet the joint prognosis of State of Health (SOH) and Remaining Useful Life (RUL) remains severely hindered by task heteroscedasticity. Conventional multi-task learning frameworks fail to balance the bounded, low-variance noise of SOH estimation with the unbounded, nonlinearly expanding uncertainty of long-term RUL predictions. Here, we present the Rotary SOH-Injected Prior Battery Transformer (RoSIP-Batt), a unified co-estimation framework that resolves these optimization conflicts. By formulating joint prediction as a Bayesian multi-task objective, RoSIP-Batt introduces a homoscedastic uncertainty weighting mechanism to dynamically scale task-specific gradients based on learned residual noise levels. The architecture leverages decoupled dual classification tokens and a per-dimension gated fusion mechanism, secured by a gradient-detachment operator to prevent high-variance RUL updates from corrupting the stable SOH representation space. To capture electrochemical degradation patterns without relying on absolute cycle steps, Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) is incorporated into a shared Transformer backbone to model translation-invariant relative temporal profiles. Crucially, the intermediate SOH estimate is directly injected into the RUL regression head as a physical degradation prior. Evaluations across the NASA, MIT-Stanford, and HUST datasets show that RoSIP-Batt significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing SOH estimation error to 1.994% MAE on NASA and restricting RUL prediction error to 62.85 cycles on Stanford. These findings establish RoSIP-Batt as a highly generalizable, computationally efficient solution suitable for real-time embedded BMS deployment.

</details>

Here, we present the Rotary SOH-Injected Prior Battery Transformer (RoSIP-Batt), a unified co-estimation framework that resolves these optimization conflicts. By formulating joint prediction as a Bayesian multi-task objective, RoSIP-Batt introduces a homoscedastic uncertainty weighting mechanism to dynamically scale task-specific gradients based on learned residual noise levels. The architecture leverages decoupled dual classification tokens and a per-dimension gated fusion mechanism, secured by a gradient-detachment operator to prevent high-variance RUL updates from corrupting the stable SOH representation space. ### [38] [ChemHyperMag: Physics-informed magnetic hypergraph learning improves molecular ADMET prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18332)
*Hexiao Ding,Hongzhao Chen,Jing Lan,Yufeng Jiang,Zihong Luo,Zehua Xiong,Tianlong Ruan,Yunlin Mao,Nga Chun Ng,Gwing Kei Yip,Gerald W. Y. Cheng,Kate Inyoung Oh,Jing Cai,Liang-Ting Lin,Jung Sun Yoo*

TL;DR: 提出了ChemHyperMag模型，通过功能团超图和磁拉普拉斯算子改进ADMET预测，在少标签情况下超越现有方法。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ADMET预测方法使用无向分子图和成对边，忽略了不对称相互作用、不可逆动力学以及功能团和环系统的模体级效应，导致预测准确性受限。

Method: 构建包含环、BRICS片段、Bemis-Murcko骨架和化学键的功能团超图；定义由电负性和Gasteiger部分电荷驱动的势能驱动不可逆流；使用埃尔米特磁拉普拉斯算子编码环流，配合磁切比雪夫编码器处理；通过扰动磁相位形成随机视图，采用InfoNCE目标训练。

Result: 在多个ADMET基准测试中，相比近期方法在标签样本更少且无需构象体的情况下取得改进；模型具有可扩展性，磁相位提供可解释的方向性信号。

Conclusion: ChemHyperMag通过引入功能团超图结构和磁拉普拉斯表示，有效捕捉了分子中的不对称相互作用和模体效应，为药物发现中的ADMET预测提供了更准确、可解释且数据高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of ADMET (Absorption, Distribution, Metabolism, Excretion, and Toxicity) is important for drug discovery. Most predictors use undirected molecular graphs and pairwise edges. This choice misses asymmetric interactions, nonreversible dynamics, and motif level effects from functional groups and ring systems. We propose ChemHyperMag for multitask ADMET prediction under missing labels. ChemHyperMag builds a functional group hypergraph from rings, BRICS fragments, Bemis-Murcko scaffolds, and bonds. It also defines a potential driven nonreversible flow guided by electronegativity and Gasteiger partial charges. The resulting circulation is encoded by a Hermitian magnetic Laplacian and processed with a magnetic Chebyshev encoder. We perturb magnetic phases to form stochastic views and train with an InfoNCE objective. Experiments on multiple ADMET benchmarks show improvements over recent methods with fewer labeled samples and no conformers. ChemHyperMag is scalable and provides interpretable directional signals through its magnetic phases.

</details>


### [39] [Federated Lightweight Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18343)
*Radhakrishna Achanta,Will Reed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FLITE方法，通过低秩映射网络实现联邦学习中的极低通信带宽，仅需传输约5KB数据（8718倍压缩），同时保持与全权重FedAvg相当的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调中存在通信瓶颈：FedAvg和伪梯度方案的通信量随模型规模增长，梯度压缩仅能实现常数倍的缩减，需要一种从根本上降低通信带宽的新方法。

Method: 提出基于映射网络的低带宽联邦学习方案：1）使用低秩、可种子再生的投影矩阵分解，将生成器内存从80GB降至10MB；2）采用delta公式学习预训练基础模型周围的加性修正；3）引入冻结正交分类头以提升准确率并减少通信量。通过共享的线性映射，平均潜在向量等价于平均生成权重。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ResNet-18+GroupNorm上，FLITE每轮每客户端仅传输1280个浮点数（约5KB），实现8718倍压缩，准确率达74.67%（与全权重FedAvg相差约0.5个百分点）。平均等式在浮点精度下成立（误差6×10^-8）；在带宽-准确率Pareto前沿上比PowerSGD和top-k低1-2个数量级；在强非IID偏斜下匹配或超越全权重FedAvg。int4潜在向量可达每轮648字节且准确率不变，而int4全权重FedAvg准确率降至随机水平。

Conclusion: FLITE通过低秩映射网络实现了联邦微调的极低通信带宽，在大幅降低通信成本的同时保持了与全权重方法相当的性能，为大规模联邦学习提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning is bottlenecked by communication: FedAvg and pseudo-gradient schemes transmit a payload that scales with the model, and gradient compression shrinks it by only a constant factor. We take a different lever. Mapping networks generate a network's weights from a small trainable latent through a frozen affine projection; because the map is shared and affine, averaging latents is exactly averaging the generated weights. We turn this into a practical low-bandwidth federated channel with two changes: a low-rank, seed-regenerable factorisation of the projection (cutting generator memory from ~80 GB to ~10 MB), and a delta formulation $θ= θ^{\mathrm{pre}} + U V^{\top} z$ that learns an additive correction around a shared centrally-pretrained base -- federated fine-tuning, which is what makes the method work at scale. A frozen orthogonal classifier head further removes the head from the payload while improving accuracy. On CIFAR-100 with ResNet-18+GroupNorm, our method (FLITE, Federated Low-rank Iterative Training Engine) communicates 1,280 floats (~5 KB) per client per round -- an 8718x reduction -- and reaches 74.67%, within ~0.5 pp of full-weight FedAvg. The averaging identity holds to floating-point precision ($6 \times 10^{-8}$); the method sits one to two orders of magnitude below PowerSGD and top-k on the bandwidth-accuracy Pareto; it matches or exceeds full-weight FedAvg under strong non-IID skew. int4 latents reach 648 bytes per round at unchanged accuracy, whereas int4 full-weight FedAvg collapses to chance.

</details>


### [40] [An Analysis of Residual-Stream Geometry Across Transformer Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18348)
*Sunit Bhattacharya,Ravi Shankar Kolli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以层间转换为中心的几何分析框架，通过相对位移和正交Procrustes分析研究Transformer残差流的层间转换，在六种指令调优模型中发现可复现的深度规律性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注Transformer模型的静态表示，而对层与层之间表示如何变化（转换过程）的几何特性缺乏系统性的测量框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供一个定量的几何分析工具来刻画残差流的层间转换模式。

Method: 提出了两种几何测量方法：(1) 相对位移（Relative displacement）衡量表示在连续层之间移动的"距离"；(2) 正交Procrustes分析将每个转换分解为刚性旋转和非刚性残差两部分。在六个指令调优模型上，针对代码生成和跨语言翻译任务进行了系统分析。

Result: 发现可复现的深度规律性：相对位移具有强层依赖性，通常在早期和晚期层较大，中间三分之一较平静；旋转幅度在深度上几乎恒定，而Procrustes残差和角度集中度保持深度调制特征；非英语目标在最终层的位移和残差大于英语目标；深度曲线主要依赖模型且对条件稳定。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个残差流转换的测量框架，揭示了在所研究设置中，深度曲线具有模型依赖性和条件稳定性。这些发现作为描述性几何规律呈现，而非计算努力度量的或因果解释，为理解Transformer内部表示转换机制提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: We propose a transition-centred geometric analysis of transformer residual streams. Relative displacement measures how \emph{far} representations move between consecutive layers, and orthogonal Procrustes analysis separates each transition into a rigid rotation and a non-rigid residual. Across six instruction-tuned models, on code generation and cross-lingual translation, these measurements reveal reproducible depth regularities. Relative displacement is strongly layer-dependent; typically larger early and late, with a quieter middle third; and nearly invariant across conditions within each model. Rotation magnitude is nearly constant across depth, while Procrustes residual and angle concentration remain depth-modulated, with residual peaking at the final transition. During generation, non-English targets show larger final-layer displacement and residual than English targets. We present these as descriptive geometric regularities, not as measures of computational effort or causal explanations. The contribution is a measurement framework for residual-stream transitions and evidence that, in the settings studied here, depth curves are model-dependent and largely condition-stable.

</details>


### [41] [MambaLSTM: A Spatio-Temporal Framework for Enhanced Traffic Accident Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18353)
*Zhen Yu,Yachao Yuan,Zixiang Peng,Muting Li,Thar Baker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MambaLSTM框架，通过挤压激励时序特征融合模块、补丁嵌入模块、Mamba块和MambaLSTM单元解决交通事故风险预测中的噪声和全局关联捕获问题，实验证明优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通事故风险预测研究在融合时空特征时会引入额外噪声，且部分模型难以捕捉空间区域间的全局关联性。

Method: 1. 开发挤压激励时序特征融合模块，在不破坏时空完整性的前提下融合时间信息；2. 引入新的补丁嵌入模块捕捉空间相邻区域间的语义关系；3. 引入基于状态空间模型的Mamba块对城市区域的全局空间语义进行建模；4. 提出MambaLSTM单元高效捕捉长短期时间依赖以识别动态风险模式。

Result: 在真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，所提模型优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: MambaLSTM框架有效解决了时空特征融合中的噪声问题和全局空间关联建模难题，显著提升了交通事故风险预测的性能。

Abstract: In traffic accident risk prediction, most studies overlook the extra noise that could be incorporated when fusing temporal features into spatial features, and some models struggle to capture global correlations among spatial regions. To address these challenges, we propose a novel traffic accident risk prediction framework named MambaLSTM. First, we develop a squeeze-and-excitation temporal feature fusion module to integrate temporal information without compromising spatio-temporal integrity. Second, we introduce a new patch embedding module for effectively capturing semantic relationships among spatially adjacent regions. Additionally, we introduce a Mamba block based on state-space models to model global spatial semantics in urban regions. Finally, we propose a MambaLSTM unit to efficiently capture long- and short-term temporal dependencies for identifying dynamic risk patterns. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate the proposed model's superiority over state-of-the-art methods. The code is released at https://github.com/Zhenzovo/MambaLSTM.

</details>


### [42] [Multi-layer MIMO Relay as Deep Physical Neural Networks: Power Amplifiers as Activation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18354)
*Meng Hua,Itsik Bergel,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种深度无线物理神经网络(WPNN)，利用多跳MIMO中继网络实现非线性激活，通过功率放大器的固有非线性作为激活函数，实现了端到端可训练的空中全连接网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数字神经网络实现存在高能耗和高延迟问题，而无线物理神经网络将神经计算直接嵌入模拟硬件中，可以显著降低能耗和延迟。本文旨在设计一种深度WPNN架构，利用硬件固有的非线性特性来增强推理能力。

Method: 提出了一种深度WPNN架构，使用多跳MIMO中继网络实现非线性激活。每个中继实现可训练的复数线性增益和偏置，功率放大器的固有非线性作为激活函数。开发了两种收发机设计：基于最小二乘(LS)的方案（仅需接收端CSI）和基于奇异值分解(SVD)的方案（需要收发端CSI）。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提架构能够实现准确的空中图像分类推理。结果特别突出了利用硬件非线性特性来增强推理能力的优势。

Conclusion: 本文成功提出了一种深度无线物理神经网络架构，证明了利用硬件非线性进行空中推理的可行性，为降低神经网络实现的能耗和延迟提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Wireless physical neural networks (WPNNs) embed neural computation directly into analog hardware, offering lower energy consumption and latency than conventional digital implementations. In this paper, we propose a deep WPNN in which nonlinear activations are realized by a multi-hop multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) relay network, in which each relay implements a trainable complex linear gain and bias, followed by the power amplifier's intrinsic nonlinearity acting as an activation function. The cascade of multiple relays therefore realizes an over-the-air fully connected network whose parameters can be trained end-to-end. We develop two transceiver designs for different channel state information (CSI) availability scenarios: a least squares (LS)-based scheme requiring only receiver-side CSI, and a singular-value-decomposition (SVD)-based scheme requiring both transmitter-side and receiver-side CSI. Simulation results show that the proposed architecture enables accurate over-the-air inference for image classification. In particular, the results highlight the advantage of exploiting hardware nonlinearity for enhanced inference capability.

</details>


### [43] [Physical Self-Supervised Learning: IMU Sensing without Manual Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18361)
*Yuyang Leng,Renyuan Liu,Shaohan Hu,Peijun Zhao,Chun-Fu Chen,Songqing Chen,Shuochao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理自监督学习框架，通过可学习的物理解码器替代传统神经网络解码器，在无需标签数据的情况下实现IMU感知，在惯性追踪和全身动作捕捉任务上误差分别降低5倍和4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在IMU感知中面临标注数据成本高、对异构设备/佩戴位置/用户鲁棒性差的限制；现有无监督和自监督方法仍需标签数据进行域适应，且忽略了已知的物理结构先验。

Method: 提出物理自监督学习范式：(1)用自适应物理解码器（可学习的运动学方程族）替代神经网络解码器，强制显式物理结构并跨环境自适应；(2)采用混合两阶段IMU编码器，在结构化潜在空间重构以缓解传感器噪声；(3)引入概率频谱-空间约束解耦传感器与物体运动；(4)设计多视图运动学树利用稀疏物理自监督信号；(5)采用不确定性感知公式处理IMU推理的固有模糊性。

Result: 在公开数据集和真实部署场景的惯性追踪与全身动作捕捉任务上，物理自监督学习在挑战性泛化场景下分别将追踪误差降低最多5倍、动作捕捉误差降低最多4倍，持续优于现有监督和自监督基线方法，且完全无需标签数据。

Conclusion: 通过融合物理先验与自监督学习，该方法成功突破深度学习在IMU感知领域的数据依赖与泛化瓶颈，为无需标注数据的鲁棒IMU感知提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Deep neural networks have become a promising approach for IMU-based sensing, but their scalability is fundamentally limited by costly labeled data and poor robustness to heterogeneous devices, placements, and users. Existing unsupervised and self-supervised methods reduce but do not remove this dependence, still requiring labeled data for domain adaptation and largely ignoring known physical structure. We propose physical self-supervised learning, an autoencoder-style paradigm for label-free IMU sensing. We replace the conventional neural decoder with an auto-adaptive physics decoder, a learnable family of kinematic equations that enforces explicit physical structure while adapting across environments, and adopt a hybrid two-stage IMU encoder with reconstruction in a structured latent space to mitigate sensor noise. Our framework further introduces probabilistic frequency-spatial constraints to disentangle sensor and object motion, a multi-view kinematic tree to exploit sparse physical self-supervised signals, and an uncertainty-aware formulation to handle the inherent ambiguity of IMU inference. Evaluated on inertial tracking and full-body motion capture over public datasets and realistic deployments, physical self-supervised learning reduces errors by up to 5x for tracking and 4x for motion capture in challenging generalization scenarios, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art supervised and self-supervised baselines without any labels.

</details>


### [44] [A Controlled Study of Attention-Only Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18363)
*Henry Ndubuaku,Karen Mosoyan,Jakub Mroz,Noah Cylich,Satyajit Kumar,Parkirat Sandhu,Roman Shemet,Justin H Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过严格对比实验发现，去除前馈网络的纯注意力模型在重新分配计算预算后，与标准Transformer性能差距极小（仅0.006 nats），主要差异来源于参数化知识召回能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈网络占据Transformer非嵌入参数的三分之二，但目前缺乏同时控制参数量、计算量和深度的必要性验证研究，因此需要系统性评估前馈网络是否为Transformer架构的必要组件。

Method: 构建纯注意力解码器模型SANs，与标准Transformer在参数量、训练FLOPs和深度（2-48层）三个维度上分别匹配对比；使用105B token训练规模，通过重新分配前馈网络释放的计算预算来增加注意力层深度，并分析权重谱和各项性能指标。

Result: 直接删除前馈层会导致0.26-0.47 nats的性能损失；但重新分配计算预算后，参数匹配时差距缩小至0.006 nats（损失的0.27%），且在不同规模下保持稳定。纯注意力模型在需要权重知识的任务上表现较差，而在依赖上下文的任务上表现更好；QK归一化而非前馈层或残差门控保证了深层模型的训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 前馈网络并非Transformer的必要组件，其主要功能是参数化知识存储；在测试范围内，注意力机制能够承担其余功能，但会牺牲一定的知识召回能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward networks hold two thirds of a transformer's non-embedding parameters, yet the architecture has not received a necessity test that controls parameters, compute, and depth at once. We pretrain attention-only decoder transformers (Simple Attention Networks, SANs) against standard transformers matched separately for parameter count, training FLOPs, and depth (2 to 48 layers), for up to 105B tokens at 6M to 87M parameters. Deleting feed-forward layers in place is costly: the standard transformer leads by 0.47 nats at matched depth and 0.26 nats at matched FLOPs. Reallocating the freed budget into attention depth closes the gap: at matched parameters the difference is 0.006 nats (0.27 percent of loss), reproducible to one part in ten thousand across seed pairs, shrinking across 5B, 30B, and 105B budgets, and holding near 0.02 nats across a 29x size range. Three measurements localize the remaining gap to parametric recall: attention-only models are better on context-grounded answers and worse where knowledge must come from weights. Weight spectra show why: routing matrices (Q/K) crystallize early, content matrices accumulate rank slowly, and removing feed-forward layers relocates this accumulation to the attention output projection. QK-normalization, not feed-forward layers or residual gating, keeps 48-layer attention-only stacks trainable. The deficit concentrates on low-context query prediction and localizes there entirely by the largest budget. A pre-registered test confirms the account: it predicts a 0.02 to 0.05 nat gap on knowledge-dense web text; a matched pair trained on fineweb-edu measures 0.040. Within the tested regime, attention does the rest.

</details>


### [45] [Scalable and Efficient Joint Spiking Embedding Predictive Architecture for Large-Scale Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18412)
*Huizhe Zhang,Yuchang Zhu,Huazhen Zhong,Liang Chen,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SG-JEPA，一种用于大规模动态图的自监督学习框架，通过时空预测架构和脉冲神经网络实现了高效、可扩展的图嵌入学习，在节点分类任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态图自监督学习方法（如生成式或对比学习范式）依赖复杂的边级重构目标和定制的图增强策略，导致在大规模动态图上计算开销巨大，且难以适应下游任务的不同计算约束。

Method: 提出SG-JEPA联合脉冲嵌入预测架构：1）沿时间维度将节点划分为上下文集和目标集，通过时空信息实现相互预测；2）利用脉冲神经元将序列输入编码为从粗到细的脉冲计数嵌入，适应下游任务的计算约束；3）避免了负采样、图增强和边级重构等复杂操作。

Result: 在节点分类任务上达到与判别式基线方法相当或更优的性能；成功扩展到包含1300万条边的动态图；在训练效率和内存可扩展性方面显著优于现有自监督动态图基线方法。

Conclusion: SG-JEPA通过创新的预测架构和脉冲神经网络机制，在保持性能竞争力的同时大幅提升了计算效率和可扩展性，为大规模动态图学习提供了一种高效解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamic graph learning aims to capture evolving structural and semantic patterns in real-world systems, such as fraud detection and recommender systems. Due to the scarcity of labeled data in real-world dynamic graphs, recent studies have introduced generative or contrastive paradigms (e.g., masked graph autoencoders or graph contrastive learning) to generate task-agnostic graph embeddings. However, these methods typically rely on complex edge-level reconstruction objectives and tailored graph augmentation strategies. This incurs substantial computational overhead when scaling to large-scale dynamic graphs. In this paper, we propose SG-JEPA, a joint spiking embedding predictive architecture for large-scale dynamic graphs. In contrast to existing self-supervised methods, SG-JEPA partitions nodes into context and target sets along the temporal dimension to learn embeddings that are predictive of each other via additional spatial-temporal information. Furthermore, through encoding sequential inputs into coarse-to-fine spike count embeddings, spiking neurons enable SG-JEPA to adapt to the varying computational constraints of downstream tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SG-JEPA achieves competitive or even superior performance over discriminative baselines on node classification, while effectively scaling to the dynamic graph with 13 million edges. SG-JEPA avoids the complex machinery (negative sampling, graph augmentations, edge-level reconstruction, etc.), resulting in superior training efficiency and memory scalability compared with prior self-supervised dynamic graph baselines.

</details>


### [46] [PAC--Bayes Bounds on Quotient Parameter Spaces: Geometry-induced Implicit-Bias Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18422)
*Nicola Aladrah,Fabio Anselmi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在商空间进行PAC-Bayesian分析，提出了一种利用模型几何结构构建先验的方法，有效降低了泛化误差界，在实验中将PAC-Bayes证书平均减少了21.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化模型存在连续参数对称性，不同参数可能对应相同的预测器。传统PAC-Bayesian分析在参数空间进行时，KL散度项中会包含仅由同一预测器的不同参数化方式引起的非负贡献，导致泛化界不够紧致。这一现象促使研究者考虑如何在预测器商空间进行分析以消除冗余的KL贡献。

Method: 作者提出在商预测器空间进行PAC-Bayesian分析，将先验和后验分布推到商空间以保留经验风险和总体Gibbs风险，同时消除由同一预测器的不同参数化方式引起的KL贡献。进一步地，作者为每个预测器构建了一个规范参数化选择，并考虑其等效参数化的几何体积，从而将中性参考先验转化为反映模型隐式偏置的数据无关先验。这种几何诱导的先验近似了理想的但不可实施的后验匹配先验。

Result: 在Fourier-Hadamard回归实验中，隐式偏置先验将商空间KL散度平均减少40.69%，将PAC-Bayes证书平均减少21.40%。在Query-Key注意力机制实验中，获得了较小且依赖于先验尺度的改进，验证了该效果的条件的本质。两个实验均使用普通SGD训练，无显式正则化器。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在商空间进行PAC-Bayesian分析并利用几何诱导先验的有效性。当几何诱导先验与学习的商后验之间的KL散度小于中性先验时，得到的泛化证书更紧致。该方法成功捕捉并量化了模型的隐式偏置对泛化性能的贡献。

Abstract: Overparameterized models often have continuous parameter symmetries, so different parameters define the same predictor. We show that PAC--Bayesian analysis should be performed on the quotient predictor space: pushing a prior and posterior to the quotient preserves the empirical and population Gibbs risks while removing the nonnegative KL contribution caused solely by how the two distributions differ among parameterizations of the same predictor.
  Quotienting alone does not determine which prior to use. We construct a canonical choice of one parameterization for each predictor and account for the geometric volume of its equivalent parameterizations. This transforms a neutral reference prior into a data-independent prior that reflects the model's implicit bias. It approximates the ideal but inadmissible posterior-matched prior, which would minimize the KL term by depending on the training data. The resulting certificate is tighter exactly when this geometry-induced prior has smaller KL divergence from the learned quotient posterior than the neutral prior.
  We test this prediction in Fourier regression with a Hadamard parameterization and in Query-Key attention, using ordinary SGD without an explicit regularizer. The implicit-bias prior reduces the mean quotient-space KL by \(40.69\%\) and the mean PAC--Bayes certificate by \(21.40\%\) in the Fourier-Hadamard experiment. The smaller, prior-scale-dependent improvement in Query-Key attention confirms the predicted conditional nature of the effect.

</details>


### [47] [Intelligence from Learnable Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18433)
*Yanbo Zhang,Michael Levin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可学习新奇性"概念，统一了不同领域对智能的不同定义，并开发了一个基于储备池计算机的无监督估计器，在复杂性分类、特征抽象和探索增强等方面均取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能在不同领域有不同定义：统计学和机器学习中表现为数据压缩，动力系统中表现为通用计算，智能体中表现为适应性行为。现有的两个主要目标存在镜像失败：寻求惊奇的新奇性搜索会被嘈杂电视屏幕吸引，避免惊奇的自由能原理则满足于黑暗房间。这两种失败源于同一原因：将学习者能转化为知识的惊奇和永远无法转化的惊奇混为一谈。

Method: 提出"可学习新奇性"概念，即信息中可学习的部分，并基于廉价且可微的储备池计算机构建了一个闭式估计器。该估计器无需任何监督，可同时用作度量和目标函数。

Result: 作为度量时，无监督恢复了几十年的复杂性分类结果，在基本元胞自动机中将图灵完备的规则110排在最高；作为目标函数时，其梯度引导神经元胞自动机从简单动力学进入孤子区域，无监督地将MNIST图像编码器组织为十个数字类别；作为强化学习智能体的内在奖励时，在十个环境中的九个环境上超越了任务基线，且没有任何环境出现性能崩溃。

Conclusion: 复杂性生成、抽象和探索——通常在不同领域用不相关目标分别追求——可以通过对单一可微量的梯度上升实现，智能的不同表现形式获得了共同的定量基础。

Abstract: Intelligence appears under different names in different fields: as data compression in statistics and machine learning, as universal computation in dynamical systems, and as adaptive behavior in agents. Each field carries its own objective, and the two most influential drives often fail in mirror image: novelty search, which seeks surprise, is transfixed by a noisy television screen, while the free-energy principle, which avoids surprise, is most content in a dark room. Both failures have a single cause: each objective treats as one quantity the surprise a learner can convert into knowledge and the surprise it never can. Here we show that the learnable part of that information, which we call learnable novelty, yields the seemingly disparate projections of intelligence, and we give a closed-form estimator of it built on a cheap and differentiable reservoir computer. Used as a measure, with no supervision of any kind, the estimator recovers decades of complexity classification, ranking the Turing-complete rule~110 highest among the elementary cellular automata. Used as an objective, its gradient carries a neural cellular automaton from simple dynamics into a regime of solitons, the traveling, colliding structures by which rule~110 computes, as well as organizes the representation of an image encoder around the ten digit classes of MNIST, fully unsupervised: no label ever enters training. Handed to a reinforcement-learning agent as an intrinsic reward, it supplies the exploration that task rewards lack, improving on the task baseline in nine of ten environments and collapsing in none. Complexity generation, abstraction, and exploration, ordinarily pursued with unrelated objectives in separate fields, thus emerge from ascent on one differentiable quantity, and the projections of intelligence gain a common quantitative footing.

</details>


### [48] [CANDOR: Chance-Calibrated Discordance in Frozen Foundation Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18451)
*Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh,Sven Nebelung,Daniel Truhn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了CANDOR——一种新的编码器质量评估指标，修正了现有方法因类别不平衡导致的偏差。大规模实验发现现有编码器在医学影像任务上表现不佳，性能差距源于下游任务选择而非编码器信息缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码器质量评估方法依赖轻量级头部的分类能力，而非特征空间的几何分离性。最近邻不和谐度虽能评估几何分离性，但存在类别不平衡时会因样本密度而非几何特性导致偏差，使得编码器在某些任务上看似“盲目”，实则是评估指标的问题。

Method: 提出CANDOR（类别感知最近邻不和谐度），一种新的评估指标。通过构建等大小银行，使其在标签交换下对称，将机会水平固定为精确的1/2，从而消除类别不平衡带来的偏差，能够独立于头部训练来评估编码器对特定发现的支持程度。

Result: 在22个编码器、20个数据集（来自7个领域）、605,443张图像上的实验表明：塌陷普遍低于机会水平，证明没有编码器是盲目的，但所有编码器都很弱。最佳胸部模型在气胸任务上AUROC为84.5，但仍有18.4%的阳性样本与相反标签图像更接近。同一编码器在鸟类物种分类上达到4.5的分数，但在胸部发现上为42.8，青光眼上为49.8（低于随机权重）。信息并非缺失，而是任务选择问题。

Conclusion: 现有编码器在医学影像任务上普遍表现不佳，性能差距源于下游任务选择而非编码器信息缺失。CANDOR因机会水平固定，可在训练任何头部之前预测编码器对特定发现的薄弱支持，为模型选择和部署提供指导。

Abstract: Frozen encoders are chosen by how well a lightweight head reads a finding from their features, not whether the geometry separates it. Nearest-neighbor discordance does, but with unequal banks the opposite-label neighbor wins on density, not geometry, so prevalence alone makes an uninformed encoder look blind. We introduce CANDOR, a discordance measure whose equal-size banks are symmetric under a label swap, fixing its chance level at exactly one half. Across 22 encoders, 20 datasets from 7 domains, and 605,443 images, this correction reverses the conclusion. Collapse falls below chance almost everywhere, so no encoder is blind, yet all are weak: the best chest model reads pneumothorax at 84.5 AUROC and still places 18.4% of those positives nearer an opposite-label film than its own kind in the same hospital. The same encoder that resolves bird species at 4.5 leaves chest findings at 42.8 and glaucoma at 49.8, at chance and worse than random weights. Such a case caps the normalized margin of any Lipschitz head, yet some head among eleven is correct on all but 2.8% of cases where one head misses 35.9%: the deficit is selection, not information. Erasure retention is associated with collapse; we detect no association with the objective, scale, recency, or size of the finding. Because the chance level is fixed, CANDOR can be read before any head is trained, flagging which findings a frozen encoder supports poorly.

</details>


### [49] [Estimating Rare Events in Language Models with Proper Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18454)
*Nikita Y. Parulekar,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GA-AMLS方法用于估计语言模型中的极低概率事件，解决了现有方法在稀有事件估计中的零估计崩溃和偏差问题，并引入了可调节非对称惩罚的SPB损失函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化语言模型中罕见故障的风险需要估计极小概率，而现有方法在极端稀有事件场景下存在脆弱性：估计器可能出现零估计崩溃或系统性偏差，标准评估损失在不对称安全成本下可能不稳定或匹配不当。

Method: 提出梯度激活自适应多层分裂(GA-AMLS)，将稀有事件蒙特卡罗方法适应到语言模型的连续激活空间；使用基于梯度的MCMC核导航激活空间，在显式的重尾激活先验下进行条件采样；同时提出Shifted-Power Bregman(SPB)损失，一种对零估计保持有限值且可调节低估和高估惩罚不对称性的评分规则。

Result: 在小规模Transformer模型上的实验揭示了偏差-方差权衡：GA-AMLS在对称评估下实现最低损失，在各个模型规模上相对于最强基线减少了平均对数空间平方误差；而在非对称惩罚下，具有高估偏差的方法表现更优。

Conclusion: 估计器的选择应与部署场景相匹配；本研究建立了激活空间作为语言模型稀有事件估计的可处理域，规避了离散输入空间搜索的脆弱性。

Abstract: Quantifying the risk of rare failures in language models, such as those triggered by adversarial distribution shifts or very large-scale deployments, requires estimating probabilities far too small for random sampling. While recent work has formalized Low Probability Estimation, existing pipelines remain fragile in the rarest regimes: estimators can suffer zero-estimate collapse or systematic bias, and standard evaluation losses can become unstable or poorly matched to asymmetric safety costs. In this work, we introduce Gradient Activation Adaptive Multi-Level Splitting (GA-AMLS), which adapts rare-event Monte Carlo methods to the continuous activation space of language models. Specifically, GA-AMLS uses a gradient-based MCMC kernel to navigate activation space, eliminating the zero-estimate collapse of input-space search and replacing the independence assumptions of prior activation-space estimators with conditional sampling under an explicit, heavier-tailed activation prior. We also propose the Shifted-Power Bregman (SPB) Loss, a proper scoring rule that remains finite for zero-estimates and offers tunable asymmetry between underestimation and overestimation penalties. Experiments on small transformer models reveal a bias-variance tradeoff: GA-AMLS achieves the lowest loss under symmetric evaluation, reducing average log-space squared error relative to the strongest baseline across model sizes, while methods with overestimation bias prevail under asymmetric penalties. Our findings highlight that estimator choice should be matched to deployment context. More broadly, our work establishes activation space as a tractable domain for rare-event estimation in language models, circumventing the brittleness of discrete input-space search.

</details>


### [50] [AHEAD: Advancing Multi-Class Label Aggregation with Interpretable Cross-Annotator Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18465)
*Ju Chen,Sijia Xu,Jun Feng,Zhiqiang Gao,Zhengyi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AHEAD框架解决众包标注中标注者数据稀疏问题，通过跨标注者学习和高置信度标注者引导，在多类标签聚合任务上显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标签聚合方法在实际应用中面临关键瓶颈：大多数独立标注者只标注了任务的一小部分，使得准确估计标注者可靠性变得非常困难，特别是在更具挑战性的多类标签聚合场景中。

Method: 提出AHEAD框架：(1)通过图神经网络学习高维跨标注者上下文，聚合个体级标注者特征与上下文信息，得到多视图、互补的标注者嵌入；(2)将这些嵌入解码为可解释的标注者特定混淆矩阵以拟合观察标签；(3)设计包含高置信度标注者的组合目标函数，缓解先前模型面临的无监督训练问题。

Result: 在涵盖NLP、计算机视觉、视频和音频的10个真实数据集上，AHEAD显著提升标签准确率，平均准确率从68.75%提升至73.23%，最佳情况下提升达14.9%；在最大数据集上的可扩展性实验进一步证明了方法的优越性。

Conclusion: AHEAD通过利用群体级数据和跨标注者学习有效解决了标注者数据稀疏问题，在多类标签聚合任务上取得了实质性性能提升，具有良好的可扩展性和实用价值。

Abstract: Crowdsourced labeling provides valuable labeled data for domains across natural language processing, computer vision, and video. Label aggregation aims to infer latent true labels from noisy and biased annotations, with the key lying in annotator reliability estimation. Despite promising progress, existing approaches struggle with one real-world bottleneck: most individual annotators label only a small subset of tasks, making accurate annotator estimation highly intractable. In this paper, we focus on the considerably more challenging multi-class label aggregation and propose AHEAD (cross-Annotator learning and High-confidEnce Annotator-guideD label aggregation), a cross-annotator learning framework that advances annotator reliability estimation by leveraging the population-level data. Specifically, AHEAD first learns high-dimensional cross-annotator contexts via a graph neural network, deriving multi-view, complementary annotator embeddings by aggregating individual-level annotator features with contextual information. These embeddings are then decoded into interpretable annotator-specific confusion matrices to fit the observed labels. We formulate a composite objective incorporating high-confidence annotators to alleviate the unsupervised training issues faced by prior models. Experiments on 10 real-world datasets spanning NLP, CV, Video, and Audio show that AHEAD substantially improves label accuracy, increasing average accuracy from 68.75% to 73.23%, with gains of up to 14.9% in the best case. Meanwhile, scalability experiments on the largest dataset further demonstrate the overall superiority of our method.

</details>


### [51] [Weak-to-Strong Learning in Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18467)
*Jingwei Ji,Renyuan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种决策感知的弱到强(W2S)框架，通过利用未标注数据来改善情境随机优化，并给出了W2S优于纯强模型的理论条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际运营决策中，预测模型的训练面临根本性的数据不对称问题：标注结果稀缺或获取成本高，而情境协变量数据充足。这种数据不对称促使研究者寻找能够同时利用标注和未标注数据的方法来改进情境随机优化。

Method: 提出决策感知的弱到强(W2S)框架：首先用有限的标注数据训练弱模型，然后用弱模型在未标注情境上生成预测结果分布，这些分布为训练强模型提供软监督。研究建立了W2S超额决策风险的非渐近上界和纯强模型基准的下界，通过比较得出W2S改善下游决策性能的充分条件。关键量是弱模型和强模型特征表示之间的相关性维度。

Result: 当弱模型和强模型特征表示之间的相关性维度较小时，大量未标注数据可以减少教师模型在非重叠方向上的错误影响。理论分析给出了W2S优于纯强模型基准的显式充分条件。合成报童实验和基于真实数据的评论审核实验提供了与理论一致的实证证据。

Conclusion: W2S框架能够有效利用数据不对称性，在标注数据稀缺的情况下，通过结合大量未标注数据来改善情境随机优化的决策性能。该框架的成功关键在于弱模型和强模型特征表示之间的相关性维度。

Abstract: Many operational decisions rely on predictive models that estimate uncertain outcomes conditional on observable contexts. Training such models, however, often faces a fundamental data asymmetry: labeled outcomes are scarce or costly to obtain, while contextual covariates are abundant. Motivated by this data asymmetry, we develop a decision-aware weak-to-strong (W2S) framework that leverages both labeled and unlabeled data to improve contextual stochastic optimization. Specifically, we first train a weak model using limited labeled data and then use it to generate predicted outcome distributions on unlabeled contexts. These distributions provide soft supervision for training a strong model. We establish a non-asymptotic upper bound on the excess decision risk of W2S and a complementary lower bound for a strong-only benchmark. Their comparison yields explicit sufficient conditions under which W2S improves downstream decision performance. The key quantity is the correlation dimension between the weak and strong feature representations: when it is small, abundant unlabeled data reduce the effect of teacher errors along non-overlapping directions. A synthetic newsvendor experiment and a comment moderation experiment based on real-world data provide empirical evidence consistent with the theory.

</details>


### [52] [RRPO: Reference-Relative Policy Optimization with Stratified Conditional Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18470)
*Yuxin Xiong,Xunyi Jiang,Rohan Surana,Xintong Li,Sheldon Yu,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Ryan A. Rossi,Jingbo Shang,Tong Yu,Julian McAuley,Junda Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了参考相对策略优化（RRPO），通过引入基于参考锚点的对比优势替代传统的正确性信号，成功将群组相对策略优化（GRPO）从可验证反馈场景推广至更通用的开放性任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的群组相对策略优化（GRPO）依赖于任务提供的正确性信号进行组内比较，但许多实际任务的成功标准无法用单一的正确性准则来衡量，这限制了GRPO在不可验证场景下的应用。

Method: 提出RRPO方法：1）采用分层条件推演构建正负锚点集合；2）训练一个度量投影头用于候选推演与锚点之间的集合对比；3）利用得到的对齐分数直接定义对比优势；4）在策略优化过程中冻结投影头，分数在推演组内标准化用于标准群组相对目标。

Result: 在可验证推理、开放式生成和后SFT等多种设置下，RRPO在与基于验证器的优化方法保持竞争力的同时，优于弱监督基线方法，并在监督微调后提供额外增益，且无需依赖任务真实验证器。

Conclusion: RRPO成功地将群组相对优化扩展到不可验证反馈场景，通过参考相对对比机制实现了有效的策略优化，为强化学习在更广泛任务中的应用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has shown strong effectiveness in reinforcement learning from verifiable feedback, where sampled rollouts can be compared within a group using task-provided correctness signals. However, extending group-relative optimization beyond verifiable settings is challenging because success in many tasks is not captured by a single correctness criterion. We propose \textbf{Reference-Relative Policy Optimization (RRPO)}, which generalizes GRPO by replacing direct correctness-based advantage construction with reference-relative contrastive comparisons. RRPO first uses \emph{stratified conditional rollouts} to construct positive and negative anchor sets, and then trains a metric projection head with a set-contrastive objective to compare candidate rollouts against these anchors. The resulting alignment scores directly define contrastive advantages: during policy optimization, the projection head is frozen, and the scores are centered within each rollout group in a standard group-relative objective. We evaluate RRPO using anchor-based contrastive advantages throughout policy optimization, without relying on task ground-truth verifiers. Across verifiable reasoning, open-ended generation, and post-SFT settings, RRPO remains competitive with verifier-based optimization, improves over weakly supervised baselines, and provides additional gains after supervised fine-tuning.

</details>


### [53] [Hybrid Latent-Structural Fusion (HLSF) for Cyber Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18479)
*Dorianis M. Perez,Maksim E. Eren,Bryan E. Kaiser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为混合潜在-结构融合（HLSF）的加权异常融合框架，结合了CP-APR结构异常分数与归一化流潜在空间密度分数，提高了恶意异常活动检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意异常活动检测是网络安全系统的基础挑战。现有的统计框架下的张量分解（CP-APR）和归一化流虽已证明是强大的无监督机器学习方法，能够建模多维数据并捕获网络安全应用中行为特征的复杂细节，但单独使用这些方法可能存在局限性。

Method: 提出混合潜在-结构融合（HLSF）框架，这是一种加权异常融合框架，将CP-APR结构异常分数与从归一化流导出的潜在空间密度分数进行整合。该框架结合了张量分解的结构化异常检测能力与归一化流的密度估计优势。

Result: 在Los Alamos国家实验室（LANL）大型企业网络红队演练期间收集的真实世界被入侵用户凭据数据集上的实验表明，与单独使用CP-APR或归一化流相比，HLSF框架显著提高了异常检测性能。

Conclusion: HLSF框架通过有效融合CP-APR的结构异常检测能力与归一化流的潜在空间密度建模优势，在真实网络安全场景中展现出更优的恶意异常活动检测性能，为网络安全异常检测提供了一种有效的混合方法。

Abstract: Malicious anomalous activity detection is a fundamental challenge for cyber security systems. Both tensor decomposition under statistical framework with CANDECOMP-PARAFAC alternating Poisson regression (CP-APR) and normalizing flows have proven to be powerful unsupervised machine learning methods that model multi-dimensional data and capture complex and multi-faceted details of behavior profiles in cyber security applications. In this study, we propose Hybrid Latent-Structural Fusion (HLSF), a weighted anomaly fusion framework integrating CP-APR structural anomaly scores with latent-space density scores derived from normalizing flows. In our experiments, we show that the HLSF framework improves anomaly detection performance on a dataset of real-world compromised user credentials collected from the large enterprise network of Los Alamos National Laboratory (LANL) during a red-teaming exercise, compared with using CP-APR or normalizing flows alone.

</details>


### [54] [Attractor Geometry Determines the Identifiability Limits of System Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18490)
*Matteo Gallo,Fabio Anselmi,Paolo Lazzari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现吸引子的几何形状（通过不变测度矩矩阵的最小特征值λ_min(M)度量）决定了从数据中发现控制方程的理论上限。该指标可预测SINDy和PySR等算法的可辨识性，且在不同动力系统间具有迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号发现方法主要关注算法设计和数据量，但忽略了吸引子几何形状对可恢复性的根本限制。研究旨在揭示吸引子如何限制方程发现能力，以及不同算法在不同动力学区域下的表现差异。

Method: 在Lorenz-84系统中采用系统内设计，通过单一强迫参数生成不动点、极限环和混沌三种区域；引入不变测度矩矩阵的最小特征值λ_min(M)作为可辨识性上限指标；对比稀疏回归(SINDy)和进化符号回归(PySR)；提出Soft F1结构度量指标。

Result: λ_min(M)准确预测了两种算法的可辨识性上限：当其为零时任何算法都无法恢复方程，随其增大算法性能提升；混沌通过扩散吸引子提高λ_min(M)，但也扩大吸引子并放大噪声；由于噪声对SINDy线性影响、对PySR超线性影响，相同转换可使两种方法表现相反；机制性评分在Lorenz-96上成功迁移；提出了预测混沌何时无法改善条件数的准则。

Conclusion: 方程发现的首要问题不是选择哪种算法，而是吸引子允许发现什么。吸引子对函数空间的覆盖程度决定了发现的理论边界，这一机制性约束普遍适用于各类算法。

Abstract: Symbolic discovery of governing equations from data is limited not only by algorithm design and data volume, but by the geometry of the attractor: what the long-run dynamics allow to be recovered. Using a within-system design on Lorenz-84, where one forcing parameter drives fixed-point, limit-cycle, and chaotic regimes while the governing equations and library stay fixed, we show that a single number, $λ_{\min}(M)$, the smallest eigenvalue of the invariant-measure moment matrix, sets the identifiability ceiling for both sparse regression (SINDy) and evolutionary symbolic regression (PySR). Derived from the Birkhoff ergodic theorem and obtained from a short reference trajectory before any run, $λ_{\min}(M)$ measures how fully the attractor covers function space: where it vanishes, recovery is impossible for any algorithm, sparse or combinatorial alike; as it grows, both algorithms improve. Chaos raises $λ_{\min}(M)$ by spreading the attractor, but also enlarges it and amplifies noise; because noise enters SINDy's regression bottleneck linearly and PySR's discrimination channel superlinearly, the same transition can push the two methods in opposite directions, so deeper chaos is not uniformly better. Parameter-free mechanistic scores from this framework transfer without refitting to a held-out Lorenz-96 system, confirming mechanism rather than curve-fitting; a criterion read from the equations predicts when added chaos will not improve conditioning. We also introduce Soft F1, a coefficient-weighted structural metric that resolves performance differences invisible to binary-success and predictive scores. The first question of discovery is then not which algorithm, but what the attractor permits.

</details>


### [55] [Now We Know? A Systematic Comparison of TerraMind and THOR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18504)
*Frederick Schindlegger,Kenzo Bounegta,Eva Gmelich Meijling,Johannes Jakubik,Arnt-Børre Salberg,Theodor Forgaard,Nicolas Longepe,Valerio Marsocci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过控制性对比分析两个欧洲空间局Φ-lab开发的地理空间基础模型(THOR和TerraMind)，发现架构设计选择（特别是补丁大小和解码器类型）比模型本身更能解释性能差异，两个模型体现了互补的投资策略，提出了可推广的诊断消融方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前地理空间基础模型基准测试越来越多地通过聚合分数对模型进行排名，但这种排名掩盖了模型差异的根本原因：架构、解码器容量和用例特定伪影各占多少差距。缺乏对模型性能差异来源的深入理解。

Method: 采用控制性比较方法，对比两个具有对比设计理念的GFMs：THOR（支持可变补丁大小的计算自适应架构，统一Sentinel-1、-2、-3数据的原生分辨率）和TerraMind（双尺度令牌/像素目标预训练的多模态生成式GFM，支持任意跨模态生成）。在十个用例（涵盖分割和回归任务，包括气候灾害响应、甲烷泄漏检测、积雪监测、海冰制图等）中调查补丁大小、解码器复杂度、微调机制、输入模态和模型规模等维度差异。

Result: 架构设计选择（特别是补丁大小和解码器类型）比模型身份本身更能解释性能差异；两个模型体现了互补的投资策略（TerraMind的预训练时规模 vs THOR的推理时令牌化）；正确解释结果需要数据集级别的特征描述。

Conclusion: 研究结果不是单一获胜者，而是一组假设和诊断消融方法论，预期可推广至未来超越THOR和TerraMind的地理空间基础模型，为模型设计和评估提供了系统性框架。

Abstract: Benchmarks for Geospatial Foundation Models (GFMs) increasingly rank models by aggregate score, but such rankings obscure why models differ: how much of the gap is architecture, how much is decoder capacity, and how much is a use-case-specific artefact? This study addresses that gap through a controlled comparison of two GFMs developed under European Space Agency's $Φ$-lab with contrasting design philosophies: THOR, which introduces a compute-adaptive architecture supporting variable patch sizes and unifies Sentinel-1, -2, and -3 data at their native resolutions; and TerraMind, a multimodal generative GFM pretrained with a dual-scale token/pixel objective that enables any-to-any cross-modal generation (Thinking-in-Modalities) to infer missing sensors at inference time. Rather than reporting a single leaderboard, we investigate the axes along which the two architectures actually differ - patch size, decoder complexity, finetuning regime, input modality, and model scale - across ten use cases spanning segmentation and regression in diverse domains, including climate disaster response, methane leak detection, snow monitoring, or sea ice mapping. We find that architectural design choices - patch size and decoder type in particular - explain more performance variance than model identity itself, that the two models embody complementary investment strategies (pretraining-time scale for TerraMind versus inference-time tokenisation for THOR), and that correctly interpreting results requires dataset-level characterisation. The resulting picture is not a single winner but a set of hypotheses and a diagnostic ablation methodology that we expect to generalise to future GFMs beyond THOR and TerraMind.

</details>


### [56] [Automated Data Engineering and Feature Selection for the Case Study of Warpage Detection in Fused Deposition Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18515)
*Saleh Valizadeh Sotubadi,Nazanin Mahjourian,Vinh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个自动化数据处理(ADP)框架，通过强化学习和SHAP可解释AI技术，在217个FDM数据集上自动选择最优的机器学习模型和特征组合，将测试集AUC从0.9248提升至0.9731，平均奖励值提高50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对熔融沉积成型(FDM)工艺数据集的预测任务，需要开发自动化框架来评估并强化最优的机器学习模型-特征组合，以提高预测性能和稳定性。

Method: 采用强化学习启发的策略更新机制，结合基于Shapley的可解释AI(SHAP XAI)特征选择方法。在217个数据集上训练多个机器学习模型，比较全特征集和SHAP筛选的特征子集的性能。每个回合评估模型-特征对的预测准确率和F1分数，计算标量奖励并更新Q值以指导未来模型选择。

Result: ADP框架成功收敛至最优模型-特征配置，测试集AUC从基线全特征配置的0.9248提升至0.9731，平均奖励值相比基线提高超过50%。策略在多回合中持续演化，奖励分布显示性能稳定。

Conclusion: 利用基于XAI算法的ADP框架能够有效实现模型-特征配置的自动优化，在保持特征信息量的同时降低维度，显著提升预测准确性和稳定性，为FDM工艺预测任务提供了有效的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: This study contributes toward development of an Automated Data Processing (ADP) framework designed to evaluate and reinforce optimal machine learning model-feature combinations for predictive tasks in fused deposition modeling (FDM) process datasets. The methodology is centered around a reinforcement learning-inspired policy updating mechanism, where multiple machine learning models are trained on both full feature sets and feature subsets selected through Shapley-based Explainable AI (SHAP XAI) across 217 datasets. At each episode, the framework assesses the predictive accuracy and F1-scores of each model-feature pair, computes a scalar reward, and updates $Q$ values to guide future model selection. SHAP XAI feature importance was employed to generate reduced yet informative feature subsets to enable the framework to explore performance with dimensionality. The policy was shown to evolve over multiple episodes, with reward distributions used to visualize performance stability. Overall, results indicate that leveraging the ADP framework through XAI algorithms successfully converges toward optimal model-feature configurations with improved accuracy and stability. Specifically, the proposed framework improves the test-set AUC from 0.9248 to 0.9731 and increases the mean reward value by more than fifty percent compared with the baseline full-feature configuration.

</details>


### [57] [Signed Rectified Flow: Negativity-Controlled Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18516)
*Runlong Liao,Baiyu Su,Lizhang Chen,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Signed Rectified Flow (Signed RF)，一种将负信息引入生成模型的新框架，通过促进目标分布同时抑制不期望分布，在图像生成质量、反记忆化和内容安全等方面取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型难以有效利用负信息和排除约束。传统Rectified Flow只能建模正概率分布，无法处理"不希望生成什么"的信息。研究者希望将"负样本"概念引入生成过程，使模型能够主动避免某些不期望的区域或特征。

Method: 提出Signed Rectified Flow，目标符号测度π^{sign} = (1+α)π^+ - απ^-，其中π^+为促进分布，π^-为抑制分布。通过分析符号连续性方程，采用带电粒子解释说明负质量如何形成排除屏障。基于此理论推导出实用的自适应引导算法，在正分布区域集中概率同时在负分布主导区域有效排除。

Result: 在多个应用中验证有效性：(1) ImageNet上改善保真度-多样性权衡；(2) 反记忆化实验中降低最近邻相似度；(3) Stable Diffusion 3.5中减少对抗性提示诱导的裸露内容，同时保持CLIP和美学评分。

Conclusion: Signed RF为生成建模提供了一种将负信息和排除约束纳入的原理性框架。该方法在理论上具有坚实基础，在实践中能够有效应用于多种生成任务，为构建更安全、更可控的生成模型开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We introduce Signed Rectified Flow (Signed RF), a generalization of Rectified Flow that targets the signed measure $π^{sign} = (1+α)π^+ - απ^-$, where $α>0$, $π^+$ is the distribution to promote, and $π^-$ is the distribution to suppress. Although direct sampling from a signed measure is not well-defined, Signed RF induces a valid generative process that concentrates probability in regions where the signed measure is positive while provably excluding regions dominated by its negative component. It therefore provides a principled framework for incorporating negative information and exclusion constraints into generative modeling. We analyze the signed continuity equation underlying Signed RF and use a charged-particle interpretation to explain how negative mass forms exclusion barriers. This theory further motivates practical adaptive guidance algorithms. Across several applications, Signed RF improves the fidelity-diversity trade-off on ImageNet, reduces nearest-neighbor similarity in anti-memorization experiments, and reduces nudity induced by adversarial prompts in Stable Diffusion 3.5 while preserving CLIP and aesthetic scores.

</details>


### [58] [Adaptive Two-Stage Online Learning for Service-Affecting Failure Detection in Mobile Core Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18522)
*J. du Toit,G. Fita,J. Salzwedel,A. Stoltz,R. Wolhuter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段在线学习框架，用于移动核心网络中基于流量的故障检测，有效解决了强时序结构、非平稳性、测量伪影和极端类别不平衡等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络运营商通过监控聚合流量来评估核心网络基础设施的运行健康状况。然而，由于强时序结构、非平稳性、测量伪影和极端类别不平衡等因素，传统的静态阈值监控方法在可靠故障检测方面存在明显局限性，难以准确识别真实的网络故障。

Method: 提出一种两阶段在线学习框架：第一阶段使用轻量级回归模型结合时间感知特征增量式建模正常流量动态；第二阶段分析预测残差和上下文指标来检测真实的影响服务的网络故障。该框架采用预quential评估协议，实现完全在线操作，具有低计算开销和持续适应能力。

Result: 在线性和非线性模型对比中，所提出的两阶段架构实现了最佳的精确率-召回率权衡，在可接受的误报率下获得了最高的召回率、F1分数和AUC值。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了显式残差分解对于流式移动核心网络数据中可靠故障检测的重要性，为网络运营商提供了一种有效的在线故障检测解决方案。

Abstract: Mobile network operators monitor aggregated traffic volumes to assess the operational health of core network infrastructure. Reliable failure detection is challenging due to strong temporal structure, non-stationarity, measurement artefacts, and extreme class imbalance, which limit static threshold-based monitoring. This paper proposes a two-stage online learning framework for traffic-based failure detection in mobile core networks. Stage I incrementally models normal traffic dynamics using lightweight regression with time-aware features. Stage II analyses prediction residuals together with contextual indicators to detect genuine service-affecting network failures. The framework operates fully online under a prequential evaluation protocol, enabling continuous adaptation with low computational overhead. Across linear and non-linear models, the proposed two-stage architecture achieves the best precision-recall trade-off, attaining the highest recall, F1-score, and AUC at acceptable false positive rates. These results demonstrate the importance of explicit residual decomposition for reliable failure detection in streaming mobile core network data.

</details>


### [59] [Censoring-Aware In-Context Learning for Generalized Supplier Lead Time Estimation in Supply Chain Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18530)
*Christopher Wang,Sebastien Ouellet,Behrouz Haji Soleimani,Ali Etemad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LT-ICL模型，一种针对右删失数据的概率性交货期预测方法。该模型在24个工业数据集上表现优异，15个数据集点预测误差最低，14个数据集概率预测误差最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供应商交货期预测是物料需求计划、库存优化和供应链风险管理的关键输入。然而，工业交货期数据集天然存在右删失问题：在需要预测时，部分订单尚未到达。标准回归和分类方法会丢弃这些信息，而传统生存模型需要针对特定任务建模，缺乏通用性。

Method: 提出LeadTime-ICL (LT-ICL)模型，结合Transformer骨干网络和条件标准化流头，生成完整的交货期预测分布。模型在合成的右删失交货期任务上预训练，无需针对特定任务进行参数更新即可适应新的工业数据集。理论分析表明，超额CRPS由先验误设和摊销近似误差界定。

Result: 在跨越7个行业的24个私有供应链数据集上进行评估。LT-ICL在15个数据集上取得最低点预测误差，在14个数据集上取得最低概率预测误差，在两项指标上均获得最佳平均排名。实验结果支持将右删失概率预测作为供应商交货期预测的实用方案。

Conclusion: 研究证明了预训练的上下文学习模型可以为工业规划系统提供准确、低适应成本的预测，右删失概率预测是供应商交货期预测的可行方案，具有重要的工业应用价值。

Abstract: Supplier lead time forecasting is a central input to material requirements planning, inventory optimization, and supply chain risk management. However, many industrial lead time datasets are naturally right-censored: at the time forecasts are required, some orders have not yet arrived. Standard regression and classification approaches discard this information, while conventional survival models require task-specific modeling. We propose LeadTime-ICL (LT-ICL), a censoring-aware in-context learning model for probabilistic lead time forecasting. LT-ICL combines a transformer backbone with a conditional normalizing-flow head, producing a full predictive distribution over lead times. The model is pretrained on synthetic right-censored lead time tasks, enabling in-context adaptation to new industrial datasets without task-specific parameter updates. We provide theoretical support for this formulation by showing that excess CRPS is bounded by prior misspecification and amortized approximation errors, providing clear direction for improving forecasting performance. We evaluate LT-ICL on 24 proprietary supply-chain datasets spanning seven industries. LT-ICL achieves the lowest point-forecasting error on 15 of the 24 datasets, and the lowest probabilistic forecasting error on 14 datasets, yielding the best average rank across both. These results support right-censored probabilistic forecasting as a practical formulation for supplier lead time prediction and demonstrate that pretrained in-context models can provide accurate, low-adaptation-cost forecasting for industrial planning systems.

</details>


### [60] [Operational Proto-Introspection in Looped Language Models: Process-Quality Taps, Executable Branching, and the Readout-Control Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18553)
*Jan Kirin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现语言模型的隐藏状态可被外部探针读取以预测计算质量（AUROC提升0.066），但基于此的外部干预未能转化为能力提升，提出了"操作性原内省"概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索两个核心问题：（1）语言模型是否能被读取其正在进行的计算质量；（2）外部干预是否能将这种读数转化为更好的输出结果。研究旨在验证是否可以通过读取模型隐藏状态来预测任务成功，并尝试通过干预改进模型表现。

Method: 使用冻结的2.6B参数循环变换器Ouro-RLTT作为测试平台。在GSM8K数据集上部署严格的前答案探针，排除答案区域和黄金值，仅通过隐藏状态结合长度和对数概率特征进行预测。构建了分支/携带/剪枝机制，包含分支特定的缓存谱系和位精确的残差捕获拼接技术。设置非循环控制组以测试递归的必要性。

Result: 隐藏状态预测能力：前答案探针AUROC达0.797，相比基线0.731提升0.066（95% CI [+0.021, +0.112]）。低容量探针成功读取角色特化属性：任务不相交分支存活率达0.9697，内容排序达0.6310宏top-1，生成分支正确性AUROC 0.7755。非循环控制复制了候选质量读出，证明递归并非所有信号的必要条件。干预结果：所有冻结干预均未产生验证的能力增益，包括方向性引导、冻结分叉、终端选择和有界LoRA。计算效率：位精确残差捕获拼接技术节省最高88%的每分支层通过次数。

Conclusion: 模型表现出"可读取但尚不可用"的特性，定义为操作性原内省（operational proto-introspection）。关键发现是：模型并未咨询研究者的探针，而是研究者读取其隐藏轨迹，但这些读数无法转化为验证的能力提升。研究局限性包括前答案结果仅限于单一领域，且承载值使用源项不相交分割和反对称化评估。方向性引导被确认为负面结果，冻结分叉增益证据被移除，终端选择问题仍未解决且统计功效不足。

Abstract: Can a language model read the quality of ongoing computation, and can an external intervention turn that readout into better outcomes? We test both questions in a frozen 2.6B looped transformer, Ouro-RLTT. On GSM8K, a strict pre-answer probe excludes the answer region and gold value yet predicts eventual success: hidden states plus length and log-probability shortcuts reach AUROC 0.797, versus 0.731 for the shortcuts alone (incremental +0.066; task-clustered 95% CI [+0.021, +0.112]; 170 tasks, 680 candidates). Low-capacity taps also read role-specialized properties: task-disjoint branch survival reaches 0.9697 oracle retention, content ranking reaches 0.6310 macro top-1, and generated-branch correctness reaches AUROC 0.7755. A non-looped control replicates a candidate-quality readout, so recurrence is not required for every signal.
  We build branch/carry/prune machinery over Ouro's 192-slot recurrent cache, including branch-specific cache lineage and a bit-exact residual-capture splice that recomputes only the affected suffix and saves up to 88% of per-branch layer passes. No frozen intervention produces a validated capability gain. Directional steering is an established negative; a four-task matched-sampling comparison removes evidence for a frozen-fork gain but cannot estimate a general deficit; terminal selection remains unresolved and underpowered; and bounded LoRA changes surface behavior without improving net reachability. A two-null audit does not support the simplest span-misalignment explanation.
  We call this readable-but-not-yet-usable property operational proto-introspection. The model is not consulting our probes: we read its hidden trajectories, and our interventions fail to convert those readouts into validated capability. The pre-answer result is limited to one domain. Load-bearing values use source-item-disjoint splits and antisymmetrized evaluation where applicable.

</details>


### [61] [Robust Multi-View Classification under Noisy Supervision via Global Anchor Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18561)
*Yuliang Yang,Hongzhe Zhang,Huiru Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GALA方法解决多视图分类中的噪声标签问题。通过构建全局锚点、跨视图审计评分和自适应修正策略，显著提升了模型在高噪声率下的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法严重依赖精确标注，但实际场景中噪声标签普遍存在。现有方法从噪声监督模型中获得的修正信号可靠性会逐渐降低。需要开发能有效抵抗噪声标签负面影响的多视图分类方法。

Method: 提出全局锚点标签审计方法(GALA)：1) 为每个视图的每个类别构建全局锚点，聚合整个类别的样本，提供稳定的参考；2) 每个视图测量实例与观测标签锚点的接近度相对于最近竞争锚点的距离；3) 将跨视图评估与分类器置信度融合为审计评分；4) 基于审计评分对可疑样本赋予小权重，采用自适应修正策略重写标签；5) 修正后的标签反过来改进锚点并监督噪声鲁棒的表示学习。

Result: 在六个数据集上的广泛实验表明，GALA优于八种最先进方法，尤其是在高噪声率情况下表现更为突出。

Conclusion: 通过全局锚点机制和跨视图审计策略，GALA方法有效缓解了噪声标签对多视图分类的负面影响，为噪声环境下的多视图学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In recent years, multi-view learning has attracted increasing attention, as it integrates the complementary information of heterogeneous views. Most existing multi-view classification methods rely on accurate annotations to guarantee performance. However, noisy labels are ubiquitous in practice due to imperfect annotation, and the refinement signals that existing methods derive from models trained on such noisy supervision can gradually lose their reliability. To deal with this problem, we propose a novel Global Anchor-based Label Auditing method (GALA) for multi-view classification to resist the negative impact of noisy labels. Specifically, we construct a global anchor for each class in every view, which aggregates the samples of the whole class and thus offers a stable reference insensitive to individual predictions. Then, each view measures how close an instance is to the anchor of its observed label relative to the nearest competing anchor, and the per-view evaluations are fused with the classifier confidence into a cross-view audit score. Based on the audit scores, suspicious samples are assigned small weights, and an adaptive correction strategy rewrites a label only when the anchor-based candidate agrees with the classifier prediction. Finally, the corrected labels in turn refine the anchors and supervise noise-robust representation learning. Extensive experiments on six datasets demonstrate that GALA outperforms eight state-of-the-art methods, especially under high noise rates.

</details>


### [62] [AMICA-Python: Adaptive Mixture Independent Component Analysis with Anderson Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18568)
*Scott Huberty,Christian O'Reilly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推出了AMICA算法的Python实现版本（AMICA-Python），提供了与scikit-learn兼容的API接口，并引入了Anderson加速方案，在保持与原始Fortran实现高度数值一致性的同时，显著提升了算法的可访问性和运行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMICA（自适应混合独立成分分析）在EEG研究中广泛应用且性能优异，但长期以来仅有一个Fortran实现版本，通过MATLAB的EEGLAB工具箱访问，这限制了其在非MATLAB生态系统分析流程中的应用和可扩展性。

Method: 开发了AMICA-Python，一个遵循参考算法的Python实现，采用现代软件工程实践，提供与scikit-learn兼容的API接口；引入了可选的Anderson加速方案以加速收敛；在14个开源EEG数据集上与原始Fortran实现进行了性能和数值一致性对比测试。

Result: AMICA-Python与参考实现达到高度数值一致性：两者的中位数最终归一化对数似然值均为11.572，中位数相对绝对差异仅为1.07×10⁻⁸。运行效率方面，标准AMICA-Python比参考实现快17.7%，Anderson加速版本快34.1%。

Conclusion: AMICA-Python成功复现了参考实现的数值精度，运行时间具有竞争力，同时通过更易访问和可扩展的Python接口使AMICA算法得以更广泛应用，为科学计算Python生态系统提供了有价值的补充。

Abstract: Adaptive Mixture Independent Component Analysis (AMICA) is widely used in EEG research and has long been associated with strong empirical performance for blind source separation. Despite its impact, practical use has historically depended on a single Fortran implementation, accessed via the EEGLAB toolbox for MATLAB, limiting its accessibility for analytical pipelines not designed within the MATLAB ecosystem. Here we present AMICA-Python, a Python implementation of the AMICA algorithm, with a scikit-learn-conformant API designed for integration with existing scientific Python pipelines. The implementation follows the reference algorithm closely while adopting modern software engineering practices and an interface familiar to Python users. Additionally, we introduce an optional Anderson acceleration scheme that can dramatically reduce the time to convergence for this relatively slow algorithm. To evaluate numerical agreement and practical performance, we benchmarked AMICA-Python against the reference Fortran implementation on 14 open EEG recordings. After averaging 3 runs of each implementation on all 14 recordings, AMICA-Python closely matched the reference, with a median final normalized log-likelihoods of 11.572 for both the Fortran and Python implementations, and a negligible median relative absolute difference of only $1.07\times10^{-8}$ when normalized by the absolute Fortran value. Runtime was also competitive. Relative to the reference implementation, AMICA-Python was 17.7\% faster, while the Anderson-accelerated variant was 34.1\% faster. AMICA-Python reproduces the reference implementation to high numerical precision with competitive runtime, while making AMICA available through a more accessible and extensible Python interface.

</details>


### [63] [Conditioned Direct Feedback Alignment via Activity and Error Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18574)
*Houman Safaai,Varun Reddy,Bernardo L. Sabatini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别了直接反馈对齐(DFA)训练中的两种失败模式(各向异性通过前突触活动因子或局部误差因子进入)，通过调节这些因子可显著提升性能(最高提升40个百分点)，提出了一系列归一化DFA方法，但指出这主要是对局部外积规则失效的因子级研究，而非BP的通用替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFA通过使用输出误差的固定随机投影来训练隐藏层，避免了反向传播中转置权重的反向传递，但其训练效果往往不如反向传播。研究者希望深入理解DFA训练失败的原因，特别是区分开反馈质量问题和权重更新过程中的各向异性问题，从而为改进DFA方法提供理论基础。

Method: 研究者在受控合成环境中隔离分析DFA的失败模式，提出基于权重更新外积分解的调节方法：活动调节(右预条件)通过活动二阶矩的逆矩阵实现，误差调节(左预条件)通过局部误差二阶矩的逆矩阵实现，以及K-nDFA方法同时应用两种调节并单独调节阻尼参数。通过线性化后对齐计算推导输入侧谱恒等式，为两侧规则提供Kronecker因子动机。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1)在高方差方向包含任务无关干扰时，活动调节可获得约40个百分点的提升；(2)误差调节使原始DFA提升1.77-7.53个百分点；(3)独立选择活动和误差因子的组合比单纯活动调节额外提升0.40-0.90个百分点。这些结果在tanh/one-vs-rest MNIST和预注册的Fashion-MNIST上得到验证，并在ReLU/softmax MNIST模型的8个新随机种子上复现。BatchNorm被证实为强效的活动侧替代方案，但卷积网络增益仍然有限。

Conclusion: 本文将调节DFA定位为局部外积规则失效的因子级研究，而非BP的通用替代或全层卷积信用分配问题的解决方案。研究表明误差因子在欠阻尼时较为脆弱，卷积网络改进仍不完整，为理解局部学习规则失效机制提供了重要的分析框架。

Abstract: Direct feedback alignment (DFA) trains hidden layers with fixed random projections of the output error, avoiding the transposed-weight backward pass of backpropagation (BP). We study a failure mode of DFA training that is distinct from feedback quality: the local weight update is calculated by an outer product, so anisotropy can enter through either its presynaptic-activity factor or its local-error factor. Our analyses with controlled synthetic regimes isolate the first failure mode and show an approximately 40-percentage-point activity-conditioning gain when high-variance directions contain task-irrelevant nuisance. Three clean confirmations isolate a different regime: error conditioning improves raw DFA by 1.77--7.53 percentage points, and combining independently selected activity and error factors adds 0.40--0.90 points over activity conditioning. The signs hold for tanh/one-vs-rest MNIST and preregistered Fashion-MNIST, and replicate on eight fresh seeds in a ReLU/softmax MNIST model. This factorization yields a symmetric block-local family of normalized DFA (nDFA): activity nDFA right-preconditions by an inverse activity second moment, error nDFA left-preconditions by an inverse local-error second moment, and K-nDFA applies both factors with separately tuned damping. A linearized post-alignment calculation gives an exact input-side spectral identity and a Kronecker-factor motivation for the two-sided rule, whereas norm matching rules out a scalar step-size explanation. The error factor is fragile when under-damped, BatchNorm is a strong activity-side alternative, and convnet gains remain partial. We therefore frame conditioned DFA as a factor-level study of when local outer-product rules fail, not as a general replacement for BP or a solution to all-layer convolutional credit assignment.

</details>


### [64] [On the Diverse Dynamical Behaviors Arising in Deep Linear Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18584)
*Sixu Li,Thomas Jacob Maranzatto,Jan Peszek,Trevor Teolis,Semih Akkoc,Konstantin Riedl,Sennur Ulukus,Nicolás García Trillos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将线性编码器Transformer的推理过程建模为粒子相互作用系统。在二维嵌入维度下，发现其动力学等价于具有二阶谐波耦合的广义Kuramoto模型，表现出聚类、振荡和分岔等丰富的长期行为，并通过实验验证这些特性可推广至更高维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度学习模型的推理行为是当今重要的研究方向。本文旨在通过粒子相互作用系统的视角，深入理解线性编码器Transformer在推理过程中的动力学行为，为其理论基础提供新的见解。

Method: 将Transformer中的token视为粒子，通过线性自注意力层进行动态交互。对于二维嵌入维度，证明该动力学可以重构为广义Kuramoto型模型，并应用Watanabe-Strogatz理论和Ott-Antonsen流形进行分析。同时揭示隐藏的Hamiltonian结构，并建立结构稳定性结果。

Result: 在二维情形下，无论参数矩阵如何选取，系统动力学本质上都是低维的。对于特定初始条件，参数矩阵诱导出丰富的长期行为模式，包括聚类、振荡和分岔。结构稳定性保证了在OA流形附近的动力学行为与流形上相同。数值实验表明这些行为可推广至更高维度。

Conclusion: 线性Transformer的推理行为可以通过粒子相互作用系统理论精确刻画，展现出类似Kuramoto模型的丰富动力学特性，包括聚类、振荡和分岔现象。这为理解Transformer的推理机制提供了新的理论框架，数值结果表明该理论框架可能适用于更高维度的实际情况。

Abstract: We study the inference-time behavior of deep linear encoder-only transformers through the lens of interacting particle systems. In this perspective, tokens are modeled as particles that interact dynamically through successive linear self-attention layers. We show that in embedding dimension two, for any key, query, and value matrices, the dynamics can be reformulated as a generalized Kuramoto-type model with pure second-harmonic coupling. This formulation is amenable to Watanabe--Strogatz theory which reveals the dynamics are intrinsically low-dimensional regardless of the parameter matrices. For a class of token initializations associated with the Ott--Antonsen (OA) manifold, we show that the parameter matrices induce a diverse variety of long-time behaviors in linear transformers, including clustering, oscillations, and bifurcations. The oscillations and bifurcations are characterized by uncovering a hidden Hamiltonian structure in the dynamics. By establishing a structural stability result, we further show that dynamics initialized near the OA manifold exhibit the same long-time behavior as those initialized exactly on the manifold. Motivated by our theory in dimension two, we conduct numerical experiments for analogous parameter regimes in higher-dimensional transformers. Our numerical experiments suggest that the long-time behaviors characterized in our theoretical results persist in higher dimensions.

</details>


### [65] [Planning as Emergent Behavior in Reinforcement Learning with Relational Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18589)
*Armin Sommer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究发现，在无模型强化学习中，神经架构的隐藏状态结构是规划行为涌现的决定性因素。通过关系隐藏状态网络，智能体能够学习环境转移结构并实现规划，为理解人类大脑中的规划机制提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明纯无模型强化学习可以涌现出规划行为，但这种现象在纯奖励最大化目标下出现的条件尚不明确。本文旨在揭示导致这种涌现性规划行为的关键因素。

Method: 设计了一种具有关系隐藏状态的神经网络架构，每个隐藏状态锚定到环境状态，并通过学习的关系进行消息传递。同时设置匹配的控制智能体进行对比实验，控制智能体需要额外发现哪些单元代表哪些状态。

Result: 实验发现关系隐藏状态网络能够在其学习的关系中恢复环境的转移结构，并通过在学习的图上进行规划来改善决策时的策略。而控制智能体没有出现这种绑定现象，也没有产生规划行为。

Conclusion: 神经架构中的隐藏状态结构是决定无模型强化学习中规划行为涌现的关键因素。这一发现解释了观察到的涌现性规划现象，并提出了人类大脑可能通过神经架构先验从纯奖励最大化中涌现出规划机制的新假说。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is conventionally divided into model-based and model-free methods. In this taxonomy, model-based methods perform lookahead planning over a learned world model, whereas model-free methods learn a reactive state-action mapping. Recent work, however, has shown that planning can emerge from model-free reinforcement learning alone. The conditions under which this behavior emerges from a pure reward-maximization objective have so far remained unclear. In this paper, we present evidence that, in the observed cases, the hidden-state structure of the neural architecture is the deciding factor. We find that a network of relational hidden states, each anchored to an environment state and exchanging messages along learned relations, acquires a planning mechanism. These hidden states recover the environment's transition structure in their learned relations, and improve the policy at decision time by planning over the learned graph. In a matched control agent that must additionally discover which cells represent which states, no such binding arises, and no planning follows from it. We argue that this explains the observed phenomenon of emergent planning in model-free reinforcement learning and raises the question of how common such emergent planning might be more generally. Finally, we hypothesize that the discovered mechanism could describe how planning emerges from pure reward maximization in the human brain through a neural architectural prior.

</details>


### [66] [BRIDGE: Bottleneck-Aware Regulator-Set Inference and Diagnosis for Cooperative Gene Regulatory Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18602)
*Maryam Rahimimovassagh,Clayton Thomas Barham,Ivan Garibay,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了BRIDGE框架用于恢复完整调控因子集合，以及TRACE诊断套件用于识别基因调控网络推断中的失败瓶颈。Residual HOS2方法在协同调控子集合恢复任务上优于传统成对方法，但精确恢复率仍然较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因调控网络推断方法主要输出成对的调控因子-靶点排序，无法有效识别协同起作用的调控因子组合。协同基因调控通常依赖多个调控因子共同作用，因此需要开发能够恢复完整调控因子集合的方法，并理解现有方法在精确恢复协同调控子集合时失败的原因。

Method: 提出两个核心方法：(1) BRIDGE框架，用于完整调控因子集合恢复；(2) TRACE诊断套件，通过归因分析识别检索、集合层面评分、解码和评估等瓶颈。引入无泄漏的机制-失配协同性压力测试，避免特征-机制循环问题。开发Residual HOS2方法，直接在原始表达向量上操作，无需手工设计的乘积-相关特征。

Result: 在30个匹配的种子协同性设置上，Residual HOS2相比可分解成对集合评分器(PairS2)将Jaccard相似度从0.382提升至0.460，召回率从0.522提升至0.597，精确恢复率从0.053提升至0.113。在SERGIO DS3数据集上，PairS2提议后Residual HOS2重新排序可减少94-97%的候选集合，同时基本保持精确恢复性能。

Conclusion: 研究区分了边排序、候选检索、集合层面评分和精确协同调控因子集合恢复作为独立目标。候选覆盖是必要但非充分条件，集合层面误排序是精确恢复失败的主要来源。精确恢复率仍然较低，表明该领域仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Cooperative gene regulation often depends on groups of regulators acting jointly, but most gene regulatory network (GRN) inference methods output pairwise regulator-target rankings. We introduce Bottleneck-Aware Regulator-Set Inference and Diagnosis (BRIDGE), a framework for complete regulator-set recovery, and Targeted Recovery Attribution for Cooperative Evaluation (TRACE), a diagnostic suite that attributes failures to retrieval, set-level scoring, decoding, and evaluation bottlenecks.
  TRACE includes a leak-free mechanism-mismatch cooperativity stress test in which cooperative targets are generated by random nonlinear mechanisms rather than product interactions. This design avoids feature-mechanism circularity: Residual higher-order set scoring (Residual HOS2) operates on raw expression vectors without handcrafted product-correlation features. Across 30 matched seed-cooperativity settings, Residual HOS2 improves Jaccard similarity from 0.382 to 0.460, recall from 0.522 to 0.597, and exact recovery from 0.053 to 0.113 over a decomposable pairwise set scorer (PairS2), although exact recovery remains low.
  On SERGIO DS3, oracle retrieval and TRACE show that candidate coverage is necessary but insufficient because set-level misranking remains the dominant source of exact-recovery failure. PairS2 proposal followed by Residual HOS2 reranking reduces HOS2-scored candidate sets by 94-97% while largely preserving exact-recovery behavior. These results distinguish edge ranking, candidate retrieval, set-level scoring, and exact cooperative regulator-set recovery as separate objectives.

</details>


### [67] [Graph Neural Network-based Algorithm Selection for the Traveling Salesman Problem: A Systematic Study of Cost and Rank Losses under Distinct Budget Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18632)
*Zhaoxuan Li,Jiale Yang,Yifei Lu,Mustafa Misir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GNNAS-TSP框架，利用图神经网络直接从原始图数据学习TSP实例表示，实现了自动化算法选择，在10秒和60秒计算预算下均优于单一最佳求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旅行商问题(TSP)的求解器性能强烈依赖于具体问题实例，传统的自动化算法选择方法依赖手工特征工程，存在局限性。需要开发一种能够直接从原始图数据学习实例表示的方法，避免手工特征设计的复杂性。

Method: 提出基于图神经网络(GNN)的算法选择框架GNNAS-TSP，将算法选择问题表述为联合成本预测和排序任务。评估了三种学习目标：基于成本的目标(MSE、MAE、Huber)、基于排序的目标(RankNet、ListNet、LambdaRank)以及混合目标。算法组合包括Chained Lin-Kernighan、Edge Assembly Crossover、Lin-Kernighan-Helsgaun、Multiagent Optimization System和Concorde。

Result: 在固定计算预算(10秒和60秒)下，GNNAS-TSP在留出测试集上选择的配置在归一化求解成本方面优于单一最佳求解器(SBS)。在10秒预算下，算法选择相比SBS获得了实质性且统计显著的改进。

Conclusion: GNNAS-TSP是一种有效的元求解策略，当求解器性能存在可利用的变异性时，能够通过学习实例表示实现自动化算法选择，显著提升求解性能。

Abstract: Automated Algorithm Selection (AS) aims to improve problem-solving performance by selecting, for each problem instance, the most suitable algorithm from a predefined portfolio. This is particularly relevant to the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP), where solver performance is strongly instance-dependent. We introduce GNNAS-TSP, a Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based AS framework that learns TSP instance representations directly from raw graph data, avoiding manual feature engineering. GNNAS-TSP formulates AS as a joint cost-prediction and ranking task. We evaluate cost-based (mean squared error (MSE), mean absolute error (MAE), and Huber), rank-based (RankNet, ListNet, and LambdaRank), and hybrid learning objectives for a portfolio comprising Chained Lin-Kernighan, Edge Assembly Crossover, Lin-Kernighan-Helsgaun, Multiagent Optimization System, and Concorde. Experiments use fixed computational budgets of 10 and 60 seconds. On the held-out test set, the selected configurations improve on the Single Best Solver (SBS) in normalized solution cost at both budgets. For the 10s budget, AS achieves substantial and statistically significant cost improvement over SBS. Overall, the results suggest that GNNAS-TSP is a useful meta-solving strategy when exploitable variation exists across solver performance.

</details>


### [68] [Mark, Don't Erase: Token Inoculation for Dual-Use Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18639)
*Seunghyun Lee,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Token Inoculation方法，通过条件控制而非知识删除实现模型安全，在保持良性任务93%性能的同时将危险领域准确率降至18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双用途知识安全方法（如遗忘、过滤、拒绝训练）存在降低相邻领域能力或过度拒绝的问题。作者认为正确的操作应该是对危险知识进行条件控制而非删除，从而实现更精准的行为控制。

Method: Token Inoculation采用绑定-分支两阶段方法：1）持续预训练阶段，在双用途文档旁插入特殊标记，使模型将标记绑定到危险领域的语义；2）监督微调阶段，教导模型在存在特殊标记时正确回答危险查询，在缺失时拒绝回答，从而在不删除知识的情况下实现选择性拒绝。

Result: 在危险领域（如WMDP-Bio）将准确率从79%降至18%，同时保持基础模型在良性领域（如MMLU）93%的性能，在1B-14B模型规模上相比遗忘和拒绝训练基线实现最佳安全-效用权衡。拒绝选择性可通过条件信号质量控制，预训练中的领域特定语义绑定对条件行为泛化至关重要。

Conclusion: 安全对齐应视为条件控制问题而非遗忘问题：当敏感知识在受控访问下保留时，行为控制比知识删除更精确。这为模型安全研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: Safety interventions on dual-use knowledge typically choose between destroying hazardous content (e.g., unlearning, filtering) and suppressing it at the output layer (e.g., refusal training); both pay a tax in adjacent-domain competence or over-refusal. We argue that the right operation is conditioning, not reduction: we show that hazardous knowledge can be retained in the model and behaviorally gated by a privileged control token. Our method, Token Inoculation, introduces a binding-and-branching approach. First, during continued pre-training, we mark hazardous content by inserting a special token alongside dual-use documents, so the model binds the marker to the underlying semantics of the hazardous domain. Second, during supervised fine-tuning, we teach the model to answer hazardous queries correctly when the special token is present and to refuse them when it is absent, thereby enabling selective refusal without removing dual-use knowledge. On hazardous domain (e.g., WMDP-Bio), Token Inoculation reduces accuracy from 79% to 18% while retaining 93% of the base-model's benign-domain performance (e.g., MMLU), achieving the best safety-utility trade-off against unlearning and refusal-tuning baselines across 1B-14B model scales. We further show that refusal selectivity is controllable through the quality of the conditioning signal and that domain-specific semantic binding during pre-training is critical for the conditional behavior to generalize beyond memorized triggers. Our results suggest that safety alignment is better cast as a conditioning problem than a forgetting one: behavioral control is more precise when sensitive knowledge is retained under controlled access than when it is destroyed.

</details>


### [69] [Spaghetti Architect: A Contamination-Resistant, By-Construction-Labelled, Multi-Language Code Dataset Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18642)
*Yuxiang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Spaghetti Architect工具，通过生成受控代码数据集解决现有代码语料库缺乏控制的问题，并验证了其在评估代码模型方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有挖掘的代码语料库存在语义不可控、表面混乱度未知、难度难以量化、缺乏已知最优参考基准以及可能已被模型训练集污染等问题，限制了代码模型评估的可靠性和有效性。

Method: 提出Spaghetti Architect工具，采用语言无关的JSON中间表示作为已知最优参考基准，通过反优化转译器生成具有可调节混乱度（由严格嵌套的反模式profile控制）的冗余、完全扁平化程序，覆盖五种编程语言（Python、JavaScript、Go、Java、C++），每个实例通过编译运行和参考oracle验证以确保正确性。实例沿两条正交难度轴标注：内在难度（问题规模）和外在难度（固定语义下的呈现），并通过从私有保留种子生成新变体来抵抗污染。

Result: 在四个模型的开放排行榜上验证：（1）精确匹配率随模型规模提升；（2）内在难度旋钮即使使最强模型的算术聚合准确率降至零；（3）开发集分数与新生成的保留集对应分数差距极小（理解任务|Δ|≤0.012，重构任务≤0.011）；（4）相同程序上重构等价性（0.73→0.99）具有规模不变性，而输出预测失效；（5）消融实验表明生成器的自标注对最弱模型的虚高程度比最强模型大一个数量级（-0.173 vs -0.017）；标注后排行榜仅能解析三对相邻配对中的一对，而未标注的解析全部三对。

Conclusion: Spaghetti Architect成功提供了现有代码语料库所缺乏的语义正确性、混乱度可控性、难度可标注性和污染抵抗性，为代码模型评估提供了新的高质量基准工具，已开源且无依赖。
