Abstract: Mined code corpora are abundant but uncontrolled: a snippet's semantics, surface "messiness," and difficulty are whatever the wild contained; there is no known-optimal reference to grade against; and any public sample may already sit in a model's training set. We present Spaghetti Architect, a tool that mints code datasets with the control such corpora lack. An anti-optimization transpiler maps a clean, language-agnostic JSON intermediate representation to deliberately redundant, fully-flattened programs in five languages (Python, JavaScript, Go, Java, C++); every program is compiled, run, and checked against a reference oracle, so each instance is correct by construction. The clean IR is a known-optimal reference, messiness is dialed by strictly-nested anti-pattern profiles, each instance is labelled along two orthogonal difficulty axes, intrinsic (problem size) and incidental (presentation at fixed semantics), and contamination is resisted by minting fresh variants from a private held-out seed. We give construct-validity evidence that the quality order moves established complexity and readability metrics, and report baselines on a four-model open ladder: exact match rises with scale, and the intrinsic knob collapses arithmetic-aggregation accuracy of even the strongest model to zero. Further, development-set scores equal freshly re-minted held-out counterparts within $|Δ|\le 0.012$ (comprehension) and $\le 0.011$ (refactoring); on identical programs, refactoring equivalence ($0.73 \rightarrow 0.99$) is scale-invariant while output prediction collapses; and ablating the generator's self-annotations shows they inflate the weakest model an order of magnitude more than the strongest ($-0.173$ vs $-0.017$): the annotated ladder resolves one of three adjacent pairs where the unannotated resolves all three. Open source (MIT), dependency-free, archived under a persistent DOI.

</details>


### [70] [Exposure-Based Reinforcement Learning to Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18689)
*Harrie Oosterhuis,Rolf Jagerman,Zhen Qin,Xuanhui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于文档曝光分布的强化学习排序方法，通过方差减少和GPU计算优化，实现了比现有方法更快的收敛速度和更高的排序性能，同时避免了复杂的自定义梯度实现，提高了方法的易用性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习排序方法虽然能优化各种排序目标，但面临动作空间巨大导致的计算成本高和效率低问题。现有方法通过自定义梯度计算提高效率，但实现复杂且与自动微分不兼容，限制了其实际应用价值。

Method: 本文放弃了自定义梯度方法，转而关注方差减少和GPU计算优化。通过基线校正和部分边缘化提高样本效率，提出文档曝光分布抽象层，将梯度估计封装在该分布背后，实现与自动微分框架的无缝集成。

Result: 实验结果表明，新方法收敛速度显著快于现有自定义梯度方法，排序性能明显提升。在GPU上使用时无额外计算时间开销，且避免了现有方法在长时间训练中出现的严重稳定性问题。

Conclusion: 本文通过基于曝光的强化学习排序方法，在保持计算效率的同时显著提高了方法的有效性、稳定性和易用性，成功解决了现有方法实现复杂、稳定性差的问题，为强化学习排序的实际应用提供了更好的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) methods for learning-to-rank (LTR) can optimize (almost) any ranking goal, e.g., from precision or discounted cumulative gain to fairness-of-exposure or ranking distillation. However, standard RL is ineffective and computationally costly due to the enormous action space in LTR settings. Existing methods reach computational efficiency through custom gradient computation algorithms, but they are very complex to implement and often clash with auto-differentiation. Consequently, existing RL for LTR is not attractive to many practitioners. We reconsider RL for LTR while actively avoiding reliance on custom gradients. Contrary to the existing approaches, we focus on variance reduction and GPU computation. In doing so, we discover that high sample-efficiency can be reached through baseline corrections and partial marginalization. Furthermore, we propose an abstraction that places gradient estimation behind a document-exposure distribution, this enables seamless plug-and-play integration with auto-differentiation. Thereby, one only has to implement a loss as a differentiable function of exposure and RL for LTR can optimize it using auto-differentiation. Our experimental results reveal that our new exposure-based RL for LTR approach converges considerably faster and at significantly higher ranking performance than existing custom gradients, with no additional costs in computation time when using GPUs. In contrast, existing custom gradients result in severe stability issues when converging over many epochs, which never occur for our methods. Thus, we considerably improve RL for LTR methodology by increasing its effectiveness, efficiency, and ease of application.

</details>


### [71] [Stale but Stable: Staleness-Adaptive Trust Regions for Stabilizing Asynchronous Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18722)
*Junyao Yang,Yucheng Shi,Zongxia Li,Zhongzhi Li,Ruhan Wang,Xiangxin Zhou,Kishan Panaganti,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Staleness-Adaptive Trust Region (SAT)方法，通过基于陈旧度的自适应裁剪区间来解决异步强化学习中的陈旧性问题，在Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base上实现了AIME24最佳性能35.83。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步强化学习虽然通过解耦rollout生成和优化提高了吞吐量，但不可避免地产生陈旧性(staleness)问题，这是由策略延迟、引擎延迟和混合专家路由复合导致的。从信任区域角度看，PPO的裁剪机制仅对采样输出进行门控，在异步场景下对高陈旧度更新的控制力不足。

Method: 提出Staleness-Adaptive Trust Region (SAT)方法：1) 使用分离的采样log-ratio作为陈旧度代理；2) 通过基于陈旧度的核缩放识别批次内的高失配尾部；3) 仅收缩名义PPO区间的符号选择端点。同时结合自适应裁剪和路由重放作为互补稳定器。在Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base上构建解耦异步RL系统，使用SGLang作为推理引擎、Megatron进行训练。

Result: SAT-GSPO w/ R3在AIME24 avg@8上取得最佳性能：lag=1时达到35.83，lag=8时达到34.79。SAT-GSPO单独在lag=1时达到34.17。证明了局部区间包含和相对于PPO的点态悲观性，展示了自适应规则如何在异构陈旧度下重塑更新几何。

Conclusion: 将裁剪区间与陈旧度异构性对齐能有效稳定异步强化学习。SAT方法在保留普通token基准行为的同时，对新拦截的外部band强制执行更保守的更新，成功解决了异步RL中的陈旧性挑战。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning improves throughput by decoupling rollout generation from optimization, but staleness is an inevitable byproduct compounded by policy lag, engine delays, and mixture-of-experts routing. From a trust-region perspective, this mismatch is critical: training-inference divergence governs approximation error in finite-horizon bounds, whereas PPO clipping only gates sampled outward updates, acting as a sampled surrogate rather than a full-policy constraint. As a result, high-staleness updates remain weakly controlled in the asynchronous regime where stale rollouts matter most.
  We introduce the Staleness-Adaptive Trust Region (SAT), which uses the detached sampled log-ratio as a practical staleness proxy, identifies high-mismatch tails within each batch via staleness-based kernel scaling, and contracts only the sign-selected endpoint of the nominal PPO interval. This preserves baseline behavior on ordinary tokens while enforcing more conservative updates on newly intercepted outward bands. We prove local interval containment and pointwise pessimism relative to PPO, showing how the adaptive rule reshapes update geometry under heterogeneous staleness.
  We evaluate SAT in a decoupled asynchronous RL setup built on Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base, using SGLang as the inference engine and Megatron for training. In this setting, SAT-GSPO w/ R3 achieves the best observed AIME24 avg@8, reaching 35.83 at lag 1 and 34.79 at lag 8, while SAT-GSPO reaches 34.17 at lag 1. Adaptive clipping and routing replay act as complementary stabilizers targeting mismatch tails and routing inconsistency, respectively. Overall, aligning clip intervals with staleness heterogeneity effectively stabilizes asynchronous RL.

</details>


### [72] [Contraction-Gauge Preconditioning for Quantized Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18745)
*Piyush Sao,Narasinga Miniskar,Pedro Valero-Lara,Keita Teranishi,Sudip Seal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种精确的低精度矩阵乘积误差分析方法，并开发了收缩-规范预调节技术来优化量化策略，在图像分类器上显著降低了乘积误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低精度计算中，当两个因子都被量化时，矩阵乘积C=AB会产生显著的精度损失。现有方法缺乏对这种双因子量化误差的精确建模，且缺少系统性的预调节方法来降低量化误差。作者旨在建立精确的误差统计模型，并开发有效的预调节技术。

Method: 1) 在独立零均值逐项误差和已知方差场的条件下，推导出期望平方乘积误差的精确有限维等式，适用于非过载减性抖动和独立随机舍入；2) 基于乘积保持等价性AB=(AT)(T^{-1}B)提出收缩-规范预调节：联合选择因子表示及其在量化前的共享模式；3) 在正对角规范（折叠）的有界族内，使用几何规划计算全局最优共享折叠，用线性规划判定恒等折叠是否已最优；4) 针对其他族推导可计算的选择统计量。

Result: 在训练好的三块图像分类器的十二个线性乘积上测试：抖动模型预测与确定性RTN误差的乘积内秩相关系数中位数在8位时为0.937，4位时为0.918；GP折叠相比恒等折叠，在几何平均上减少保留乘积误差18.0%（8位）和20.5%（4位），在两种精度下都击败SmoothQuant风格网格基线并在12个产品中的10个上胜出，降低组合逻辑MSE达15.4%和26.4%。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了精确的随机乘积误差统计、对角族内的认证选择方法，以及评估RTN下可重用变换候选的共同目标，为低精度矩阵乘法提供了系统的理论框架和实用优化技术。

Abstract: We study low-precision computation of C=AB with both factors quantized. We derive an exact finite-dimensional identity for the expected squared product error under independent, zero-mean entrywise errors with known variance fields; it holds exactly for non-overloading subtractive dither and for independent stochastic rounding, and we empirically assess deterministic round-to-nearest (RTN). Using the product-preserving equivalence AB=(AT)(T^{-1}B), we formulate contraction-gauge preconditioning: jointly choosing a factor representation and its sharing pattern before quantization. Preconditioning can reduce product error but may require extra transformed, quantized copies of the opposite operand: a shared transform needs one copy, a block-specific transform up to one per block. Within the bounded family of positive diagonal gauges (folds), a geometric program computes a globally optimal shared fold and a linear program decides whether the identity fold is already optimal. For other families we derive computable selection statistics -- tail index for scaling, profile spread for partitioning, coherence and weighted-Gram energy for rotations, slice-energy covariance for hierarchy depth -- with upper bounds for ranking heuristic candidates. Across twelve linear products from a trained three-block image classifier, median within-product rank correlations between dither-model predictions and deterministic-RTN errors are 0.937 at 8 bits and 0.918 at 4 bits. The GP fold cuts held-out product error over the identity fold by 18.0% (8-bit) and 20.5% (4-bit) in geometric mean, beats a SmoothQuant-style grid baseline at both precisions and on ten of twelve products, and lowers composed logit MSE by 15.4% and 26.4%. We thus provide exact stochastic product-error accounting, certified selection within the diagonal family, and a common objective for evaluating reusable transform candidates under RTN.

</details>


### [73] [ConceptCF: Concept-based Counterfactuals for the Explainability of Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18748)
*Annemarie Jutte,Faizan Ahmed,Jeroen Linssen,Maurice van Keulen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ConceptCF方法，通过修改时间序列的可解释概念（如尺度、频带）来生成反事实解释，在有效性、置信度、邻近性、稀疏性和合理性等指标上均达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗和预测性维护等高风险领域，AI模型的可解释性至关重要，需要确保模型依赖因果关系而非虚假相关性。现有的时间序列反事实解释方法在单个点或子序列层面操作，无法保证修改的可解释性，因此需要一种能够基于有意义概念生成解释的方法。

Method: ConceptCF首先通过时间序列分解构建可解释概念（如尺度、频带），然后使用遗传算法优化概念变异，生成反事实样本，从而在概念层面提供可解释的修改建议。

Result: 通过与五种最先进方法的对比评估，ConceptCF在有效性、置信度、邻近性、稀疏性和合理性等所有评价指标上均稳定地达到顶级性能。

Conclusion: ConceptCF成功实现了基于可解释概念的反事实生成，能够提供如"如果增加运动的尺度，模型预测将从'行走'变为'坐下'"这类直观解释，为高风险领域的AI模型可解释性提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes ConceptCF, a method for counterfactual generation that operates on human-interpretable concepts. In high-stakes domains such as healthcare and predictive maintenance, artificial intelligence models can increase efficiency and safety. Explainability is key to ensure these models rely on causal relationships rather than spurious correlations. Counterfactual explanations identify minimal modifications that would change a model's predictions. Existing methods for time series operate on individual points or subsequences without ensuring interpretability of the mutations. ConceptCF instead modifies meaningful concepts. As a result we can provide explanations in terms of these concepts, for example ``the model's prediction would be `Sit' instead of `Walk' if you increase the scale of the movement''. In this paper, the concepts are constructed through time series decomposition, resulting in concepts such as scale, and frequency bands. Counterfactuals are generated using a genetic algorithm that optimizes the concept mutations. Evaluation against five state-of-the-art approaches demonstrates that ConceptCF consistently achieves top-tier performance across validity, confidence, proximity, sparsity and plausibility metrics.

</details>


### [74] [Is EEG-to-Text Feasible in Real-World Scenarios? An In-Depth Analysis Using a Neuropsychology-Inspired Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18749)
*Zihan Zhang,Yu Bao,Xiao Ding,Tianyi Jiang,Kai Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出了现有EEG2Text基准忽视EEG不稳定性的缺陷，创建了新基准COFETT，首次实现了无需教师强制的解码评估，证明EEG2Text的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG2Text模型过度依赖教师强制评估，在无此机制时无法生成有意义的解码结果，这阻碍了其在现实场景的应用，并引发了对EEG是否包含可解码语言信息的争议。研究发现现有基准忽视了EEG不稳定性这一关键缺陷。

Method: 采用神经心理学启发的范式分析EEG不稳定性问题，使用128通道高密度EEG帽构建了EEG转文本语料库（COFETT），专门用于评估EEG2Text模型，支持无需教师强制的鲁棒性评估。

Result: COFETT在区分模型性能方面达到SOTA水平，首次为无需教师强制的EEG2Text解码提供了关键证据，证明了其可行性，为实际应用开辟了道路。

Conclusion: EEG不稳定性是阻碍EEG2Text实用化的关键因素，COFETT基准通过解决这一问题，为构建实用化EEG2Text系统提供了有效评估平台，推动了非侵入式脑机接口通信技术的发展。

Abstract: Translating brain signals into text could restore communication for people with severe paralysis, yet practically usable systems to date rely on invasive electrocorticography (ECoG). Electroencephalography (EEG) offers a non-invasive alternative, and EEG-to-text (EEG2Text) has been widely explored. Interestingly, however, EEG2Text models generally rely on teacher-forcing evaluation; without it, they fail to generate meaningful decoding. This reliance prevents EEG2Text from being applied in real-world, non-academic settings. This has fueled numerous debates about whether EEG2Text is a meaningful direction, by extension, and whether EEG truly contains decodable linguistic information. Here, using a neuropsychology-informed paradigm, we find that existing EEG2Text benchmarks have neglected EEG instability, a flaw that has confounded inference and sparked debate. Our experiments furnish key evidence for the feasibility of teacher-forcing-free EEG2Text decoding. Accordingly, we assemble the Corpus OF Eeg-To-Text (COFETT) using a 128-channel high-density EEG cap, providing a benchmark dedicated to evaluating EEG2Text models. In comparisons with multiple existing benchmarks, COFETT achieves SOTA ability to distinguish among model performances and enables robust, teacher-forcing-free evaluation, thereby opening a path toward practical EEG2Text applications. COFETT is open sourced in https://github.com/baoyudu/COFETT.

</details>


### [75] [Decafs: Disentangled Conditional adversarial Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18755)
*Anirudh jain,Sakshi Varshney,Samuel Kaski,Vikas Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于李群的新型条件生成模型，解决了流模型潜在空间纠缠问题，实现了可解释的条件生成，在图像和分子生成任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流模型在生成建模领域取得了最先进的性能，但由于其复杂的潜在嵌入，难以解释。特别是，潜在空间中生成因子的纠缠阻碍了可控生成。

Method: 提出一种基于李群的新型条件生成器，解缠一个替代潜在空间，并使用对抗损失将其与潜在流空间紧密对齐。该方法促进了可解释的条件生成，同时无需扩展流空间的维度（归因于其可逆性要求）。

Result: 模型在条件图像生成任务（包括在MNIST、dSprites上优于StyleGAN）和分子生成任务（使用标准QM9、ZINC和MOSES数据集）上展现了强劲性能。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了流模型潜在空间纠缠问题，实现了可解释的条件生成，在多个任务上取得了优越性能，为可控生成提供了一种有效解决方案。

Abstract: Flow-based models have established state-of-the-art performance in generative modeling across domains, but are hard to interpret due to their complex latent embeddings. In particular, the entanglement of generative factors in the latent space hinders controlled generation. We circumvent this issue by appealing to a novel conditional generator based on Lie groups that disentangles an alternative latent space, which is aligned closely with the latent flow space using an adversarial loss. Our approach facilitates interpretable conditional generation while obviating the need to expand the dimensionality of the flow space (owing to its invertibility requirements). The proposed model demonstrates strong performance across conditional image (including, outperforming StyleGAN on MNIST, dSprites) and molecule (using standard QM9, ZINC and MOSES) generation tasks

</details>


### [76] [Relative Positions Generalize, Absolute Positions Memorize: An Implicit-Bias Account of Length Generalization in Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18759)
*Subham Singh,Ashutosh Mishra,Subha Raut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Transformer长度泛化能力差异的优化机制：相对位置编码（如旋转编码）通过等变性实现泛化，而绝对位置编码因隐式偏置固定在训练范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究观察到相对位置编码的Transformer能泛化到训练未见过的更长序列，而绝对位置编码通常不能。以往解释主要关注表达能力，但无法解释为何梯度下降会从众多拟合短序列的解中选择特定解。作者从优化和隐式偏置角度寻求解释。

Method: 设计了最小化固定偏移检索任务以分离位置选择机制。理论证明旋转位置编码使注意力logit仅依赖相对偏移（精确等变性），并推导出低秩"载波"核的数学形式。将预测与多实验条件对比验证：不同种子、偏移量、线性注意力控制，并扩展到多层多头Transformer的全序列长度泛化任务。

Result: 旋转编码训练后得到与目标偏移对齐的低秩载波核，其泛化遵循"注意力稀释定律"，实现测试长度上的精确规则复现。绝对编码的注意力头被优化到训练范围内的固定绝对位置，导致超出训练范围的位置完全不受约束。线性注意力（无softmax归一化）选择最小范数插值解，丧失泛化能力。所有预测在多种实验设置下均得到验证。

Conclusion: 长度泛化差距的根本原因在于softmax注意力的隐式偏置：等变性保证规则精确迁移，载波核导致性能衰减可预测。该机制解释了绝对位置编码的局限性，建立了注意力隐式偏置、循环模型泛化机制与RASP-L猜想之间的联系，为理解序列模型泛化提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Transformers with relative positional encodings often extrapolate to sequences longer than those seen during training, whereas transformers with learned absolute encodings typically do not. This is a robust empirical regularity, and the explanations offered for it so far are chiefly about expressivity, that is, about whether a length-generalizing solution exists. We give an optimization explanation. On a minimal fixed-offset retrieval task that isolates positional selection, the gap is governed by the implicit bias of the trained attention head: among the many solutions that fit short sequences, which one gradient descent actually selects. We prove that rotary encodings make the attention logit a function of relative offset alone, an exact equivariance, so whatever selection rule is learned at training lengths is reproduced verbatim at every longer length. Learned absolute encodings instead leave out-of-range positions unconstrained, and the trained head pins to a fixed absolute position inside the training range. We characterize the learned rotary rule as a low-rank ``carrier'' kernel aligned with the target offset, and we derive the resulting graceful accuracy decay as an attention-dilution law; both predictions are confirmed across seeds and offsets. A linear-attention control shows the mechanism is specific to softmax: without normalization, training selects a min-norm interpolant that does not extrapolate. The phenomenon, the equivariance, and the carrier all transfer to a multi-layer, multi-head transformer trained on a full-sequence length-generalization task. The account connects the implicit bias of attention, implicit bias for extrapolation in recurrent models, and the learning side of the RASP-L conjecture.

</details>


### [77] [Formulation-Level Auto-Tuning for QUBO-Based Machine Learning: A Case Study Across Multiple Quantum-Inspired Annealers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18774)
*Naoya Mizuki,Takahiro Katagiri,Daichi Mukunoki,Tetsuya Hoshino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Optuna的SVM公式级自动调优框架，通过联合优化离散化参数、核参数和惩罚系数，在多个量子启发退火器上实现了比传统网格搜索更优的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于退火的SVM需要将连续对偶变量离散化为QUBO模型，这一转换引入了三类耦合参数（表示参数、核参数、约束惩罚），它们的联合选择是混合离散-连续黑盒优化问题。传统网格搜索难以有效处理这种高维参数空间，且无法捕捉参数间的复杂耦合关系。

Method: 提出双层优化框架：内层由退火器最小化生成的QUBO，外层Optuna循环在每个试验中重构公式并最大化验证精度。使用TPE和高斯过程采样器，将相同的求解器无关过程应用于Fixstars Amplify、Toshiba SQBM+和Fujitsu Digital Annealer三种量子启发退火器，并与传统网格搜索进行对比。

Result: 在线性和非线性分类任务（0-20%标签噪声）上，相比网格搜索分别获得约0.8和2.1个百分点的平均性能提升。实验表明公式质量和后端能力必须联合评估，任务级反馈可以补偿离散化、惩罚不平衡和后端相关的近似优化带来的问题。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了求解器无关的自动调优，证明了任务级反馈机制能够有效补偿离散化误差和硬件限制。研究强调了在实际应用中需要联合考虑算法公式质量和硬件后端能力，为量子启发退火算法的实际部署提供了有效的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an Optuna-based formulation-level auto-tuning framework for support vector machines (SVMs) implemented on multiple quantum-inspired annealers. In an annealing-based SVM, continuous dual variables are discretized and converted into a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) model. This transformation introduces three coupled classes of parameters: representation parameters-the encoding base B and bit depth K-which determine numerical range, resolution, and QUBO size; the RBF kernel parameter γ, which determines classifier geometry; and the equality-constraint penalty ξ, which controls feasibility and coefficient balance. We formulate their joint selection as a mixed discrete-continuous black-box optimization problem. The framework has two optimization levels: an inner annealer minimizes the generated QUBO, while an outer Optuna loop reconstructs the formulation in every trial and maximizes validation accuracy. The same solver-agnostic procedure is applied to Fixstars Amplify Annealing Engine, Toshiba SQBM+, and Fujitsu Digital Annealer using TPE and Gaussian-process samplers and is compared with conventional grid search. Experiments on linear and nonlinear classification tasks with 0-20% label noise show mean gains over grid search of approximately 0.8 and 2.1 percentage points, respectively. The results demonstrate that formulation quality and backend capability must be evaluated jointly and that task-level feedback can compensate for discretization, penalty imbalance, and backend-dependent approximate optimization.

</details>


### [78] [PertReason: A Knowledge-Grounded Benchmark and Framework for Cell-State-Conditioned Mechanistic Reasoning of Perturbation Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18777)
*Dongkwan Kim,Yiming Gao,Yining Yang,Yang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PertReason基准测试框架，用于评估机器学习模型在科学领域中进行扰动效应推理的能力，发现了现有模型在预测准确性和机制推理之间的系统性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学领域评估机器学习模型时，需要区分正确的预测和正确的原因推理，特别是在面对真实的分布偏移时。现有基准测试无法充分评估模型是否能够生成机制上合理的解释并保持对复杂分布偏移的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出PertReasonQA基准测试，结合单细胞基因和化学扰动数据与知识图谱，通过动态地将通路条件化于细胞特异性基础状态来避免通用记忆。同时开发PertReasonLM大语言模型，通过将推理根植于上下文特异性通路并加强结果与机制之间的一致性来针对已识别的失败模式。

Result: 对最先进模型的评估揭示了预测准确性与机制推理之间的系统性差距。模型表现出对标准基准测试不可见的失败模式，包括通过错误逻辑推导正确答案、忽略细胞上下文以及生成方向不一致的机制。PertReasonLM成功缓解了这些问题。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个诊断框架，能够揭示并缓解数据丰富的科学系统中忠实推理的失败，为未来科学AI系统的评估和改进提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Evaluating machine learning in scientific domains requires separating correct predictions from correct reasons under realistic distribution shifts. We introduce PertReason, a knowledge-grounded benchmark and framework suite for cell-state--conditioned reasoning about perturbation effects. At its core, PertReasonQA is a benchmark that tests whether models can generate mechanistically faithful explanations while remaining robust to complex shifts, such as new cells and unseen perturbations. PertReasonQA combines single-cell genetic and chemical perturbation data across multiple cellular contexts with knowledge graphs, and dynamically conditions pathways on cell-specific basal states to avoid generic memorization. Evaluations on state-of-the-art models reveal systematic gaps between predictive accuracy and mechanistic reasoning. Specifically, these models exhibit failure modes largely invisible to standard benchmarks, such as deriving correct answers through flawed logic, ignoring cellular context, and generating directionally inconsistent mechanisms. As a reference probe of the benchmark, we present PertReasonLM, a large language model trained to align outcome predictions with context-specific mechanistic reasoning. Our model targets the identified failure modes by grounding rationales in context-specific pathways and tightening agreement between outcomes and mechanisms. Together, we provide a diagnostic framework for exposing and mitigating failures in faithful reasoning in data-rich scientific systems.

</details>


### [79] [QScheduler: Adaptive Gradient Sampling for Zeroth-Order On-Device Training on INT8 NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18802)
*Victor Felipe Domingues Do Amaral,Pierre Demaj,Erwan Libessart,Laurent Folliot,Anthony Kolar,Philippe Bénabès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了QScheduler自适应算法，动态调整零阶优化的梯度采样数q，在STM32N6 NPU上首次实现INT8量化端侧训练，无需预调参即可达到最优固定q配置的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化通过仅前向传播估计梯度，使NPU微控制器端侧训练成为可能。但梯度采样数q的选择存在两难：采样不足导致噪声大、性能早停，采样过多则浪费计算资源，而寻找最优q需要昂贵的超参数搜索。

Method: 提出QScheduler自适应算法，根据训练进度动态调整梯度采样数q，避免手动超参数优化。在STM32N6的Neural-ART NPU上首次实现INT8量化的端侧训练原型。

Result: 在EuroSAT和STL-10数据集上，QScheduler在ResNet18和MobileNetV2两种模型上均能达到精心调优的固定q配置的性能，无需预先进行q超参数优化。

Conclusion: QScheduler成功解决了零阶优化中梯度采样数的自动调优问题，实现了NPU上INT8量化端侧训练，为资源受限设备上的高效学习提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Zeroth-Order (ZO) optimization enables On-Device Learning (ODL) on NPU-equipped microcontrollers by estimating gradients through forward passes alone, bypassing the need for backpropagation primitives and reducing memory requirements. The number of gradient samples q critically affects training: insufficient samples produce noisy gradients that plateau early, while excessive samples consume more computational resources. However, finding an optimal q typically requires costly hyperparameter searches. This work introduces QScheduler, an adaptive algorithm that adjusts q based on training progress, and provides the first proof-of-concept of INT8 quantized on-device training on the STM32N6's Neural-ART NPU. Experiments on EuroSAT and STL-10 show that QScheduler matches well-tuned fixed-q configurations for both ResNet18 and MobileNetV2, without requiring prior q hyperparameter optimization.

</details>


### [80] [Elicitation without Backpropagation: Steering Model Behavior by Optimizing the Latent Posterior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18804)
*Garrett Baker,Vinayak Pathak,Daniel Murfet,Susan Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了后缀前缀调优（PPT），一种从贝叶斯过滤变压器（BFT）中引出高效用行为的新方法，通过优化硬提示分布而不需要变压器的前向传播或反向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在潜在后验模型中，变压器的下一个标记分布来自于基于上下文的潜在预测模型的后验分布的混合。作者利用这一模型在贝叶斯过滤变压器（BFT）上精确成立的设置，旨在开发一种方法，能够根据效用函数找到使变压器生成高效用续写的提示。

Method: PPT优化硬提示分布的参数：首先通过预测性蒙特卡洛（PMC）从BFT中一次性提取先验样本，然后通过重要性采样估计梯度。该方法不需要变压器的正向传播，也不需要通过变压器进行反向传播，且先验样本与效用无关，因此一组样本可以用于任意数量的效用函数的引出。

Result: 在Beta-Bernoulli和增强罐BFT上验证了PPT，测试了三种效用家族（反向交叉熵、频率匹配、Dyck有效性），证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: PPT成功实现了在不进行变压器前向传播和反向传播的情况下，通过优化提示分布来引出高效用行为，且边际成本极低，为变压器行为控制提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: In the \emph{latent posterior model} of transformer behavior, the next-token distribution arises from a posterior over latent predictive models conditioned on the context, mixed to generate continuations. We exploit this model in settings where it is exact, namely Bayes-filtered transformers (BFTs) meta-learned on sequences from a hierarchical prior, to introduce \textbf{Posterior Prefix Tuning (PPT)}, a new method for \emph{eliciting} behavior from a transformer: given a utility function on continuations, find a prompt under which the transformer generates continuations of high expected utility. For a BFT, the elicitation objective factors through the latent posterior, and the gradient of this objective can be estimated from samples of the prior alone. PPT optimizes the parameters of a distribution over hard prompts: it draws prior samples once from the BFT via predictive Monte Carlo (PMC), then estimates the gradient by importance sampling against them. The optimization performs no transformer forward passes and no backpropagation through the transformer, and the prior samples are utility-independent, so a single set of samples drives elicitation against any number of utilities at negligible marginal cost. We validate PPT on Beta--Bernoulli and reinforced urn BFTs across three utility families (reverse cross-entropy, frequency matching, Dyck validity).

</details>


### [81] [Countercurrent Multiplier Networks: A Renal-Inspired Iterative Operator with Provably Bounded Fixed-Point Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18829)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将哺乳动物肾脏的逆流倍增机制形式化为一种可微分的神经网络层（CCM层），作为残差迭代细化的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哺乳动物肾脏通过逆流倍增机制浓缩尿液，这种机制在现有神经架构中尚无类比。该机制利用两条反向平行流动在发夹处连接，将弱的、有界局部泵循环成大的轴向梯度，实现四倍浓度增加，而单效应梯度在任何点都不超过200 mOsm。研究动机在于探索这种独特的生物机制是否能为神经网络架构提供新的设计思路。

Method: 将逆流倍增机制形式化为一个可微分的序列算子，称为Countercurrent Multiplier（CCM）层。该层模拟肾脏中的逆流过程，通过局部泵循环构建大梯度。研究将其作为残差迭代细化的替代方案进行研究和对比分析。

Result: 成功将生物肾脏的逆流倍增机制转化为神经网络组件，能够将弱的局部效应累积成强的全局梯度，实现了类似肾脏的四倍浓度放大效果，且单效应梯度始终保持在较低水平（不超过200 mOsm）。

Conclusion: 逆流倍增机制为神经网络架构提供了新的迭代细化范式，CCM层展示了生物机制向计算架构转化的可行性，可能为深度学习中的梯度传播和特征增强提供新思路。

Abstract: The mammalian kidney concentrates urine using a mechanism with no analogue in current neural architectures: the countercurrent multiplier. Two anti-parallel flows joined at a hairpin recirculate a weak magnitude-bounded local pump into a large axial gradient achieving a four-fold concentration increase from a single-effect gradient that never exceeds 200 mOsm at any point. We formalize this mechanism as a differentiable sequence operator the Countercurrent Multiplier (CCM) layer and study it as an alternative to residual iterative refinement.

</details>


### [82] [From Trajectories to Instructions: Language-Conditioned Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18830)
*Garvit Singla,Uma Maheswari Natarajan,Raghuram Bharadwaj Diddigi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LA-MAML方法，用语言指令嵌入替代MAML中昂贵的内循环轨迹收集和梯度更新，在保持竞争力的性能同时显著降低训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MAML框架在内循环中需要收集轨迹并进行梯度更新来适应特定任务，这一过程计算成本高昂。作者观察到外循环才是驱动全局参数学习的关键，因此内循环的适应机制不必局限于基于梯度的方法，这启发作者探索更简单的任务特定信号来替代内循环。

Method: 提出LA-MAML（Language Adapted MAML）方法，利用任务伴随的语言指令作为任务特定信号，通过学习到的指令嵌入在单步内直接调整全局策略参数，完全替代了传统MAML内循环中的轨迹收集和基于梯度的更新过程。

Result: 在BabyAI基准测试上的实验表明，LA-MAML相比基线方法取得了相当或更优的性能表现，同时每次迭代的实际训练时间显著降低。

Conclusion: 语言指令是元强化学习中轨迹内循环适应的有效且高效的替代方案，证明了利用语言信息可以大幅提升MAML框架的训练效率。

Abstract: Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning (MAML) is a widely used framework for reinforcement learning (RL) that enables efficient transfer by learning global policy parameters that can be rapidly adapted to new tasks. MAML training proceeds in two loops: an inner loop where the global parameters are adapted to task-specific parameters, and an outer loop where these task-specific parameters are evaluated and losses are back-propagated to improve the global parameters. Traditionally, the inner loop adaptation is performed by collecting trajectories from the task environment and applying gradient updates on the empirical expected return, which can be a costly operation. We note that it is the outer loop that drives the actual learning of global parameters, and therefore the inner loop adaptation mechanism need not be restricted to be gradient-based. This observation leads us to ask: Can we replace the inner loop trajectory collection and gradient update with a simpler, task-specific signal? In many practical settings, tasks are naturally accompanied by language instructions. Leveraging these instructions as a direct task-specific signal, we propose LA-MAML (Language Adapted MAML), which modifies the inner loop by adapting the global policy parameters in a single step through a learned embedding of the task instruction, replacing the inner loop trajectory collection and gradient-based updates. Experiments on the BabyAI benchmark demonstrate that LA-MAML achieves competitive or improved performance compared to baselines at a significantly lower per-iteration wall-clock training time. These results demonstrate that language instructions are an effective and efficient substitute for trajectory-based inner loop adaptation in meta RL.

</details>


### [83] [ABOPD: Antibody CDR Design via On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18835)
*Zhuo Yang,Jiaying He,Jiaqing Xie,Daolang Wang,Xipeng Qiu,Yuxin Wang,Tianfan Fu,Beilun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ABOPD框架，通过策略蒸馏方法改进抗体CDR-H3环设计，将RMSD降低0.42Å，显著提升了结构恢复精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗体CDR区域是抗原识别的关键界面，现有蛋白质生成模型在下游任务的后训练策略有限。标准去噪训练与递归生成过程存在不匹配，导致CDR-H3等柔性环在去噪轨迹中累积骨架偏差，影响抗原结合界面几何结构。

Method: 提出ABOPD（基于策略蒸馏的抗体设计框架），利用训练过程中的特权原生几何结构，监督模型自身去噪轨迹上访问的中间状态，实现对生成过程的细粒度结构监督。

Result: 在RAbD CDR-H3生成任务上显著提升结构恢复性能，RMSD从2.37Å降至1.95Å，减少0.42Å；优于监督微调和离线蒸馏等对照方法。

Conclusion: ABOPD框架通过策略蒸馏实现了对生成轨迹的细粒度监督，有效解决了训练与生成过程的不匹配问题，为高保真蛋白质设计提供了新路径。

Abstract: Antibodies are essential therapeutic molecules, and their complementarity-determining regions (CDRs) form the primary antigen-recognition interface. Recent protein generative models have demonstrated broad capabilities in biomolecular design, yet post-training strategies for downstream objectives remain limited. Standard denoising training operates on noisy states obtained by perturbing native structures, whereas recursive generation proceeds through model-generated intermediate states. For flexible antibody CDR loops such as CDR-H3, this mismatch can allow backbone deviations to accumulate along the denoising trajectory and compromise antigen-facing loop geometry. We introduce ABOPD, an antibody design framework based on on-policy distillation that leverages privileged native geometry during training to supervise states visited along the model's own denoising trajectories. With this fine-grained structural supervision, ABOPD substantially improves structural recovery on RAbD CDR-H3 generation, reducing RMSD by 0.42 Å (from 2.37 Å to 1.95 Å) and outperforming supervised fine-tuning and offline distillation controls, offering a path to higher-fidelity protein design.

</details>


### [84] [Regime-Aware Physics-Guided Early Warning of Lithium-Ion Battery Thermal Runaway Using Thermo-Mechanical Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18860)
*Syed Sajid Ullah,Muhammad Zunair Zamir,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种机制感知的物理引导框架，通过融合温度、电压、力、变形和荷电状态等多模态信号，实现了锂电池热失控的早期预警，平均预警提前时间比最强基线方法提高69.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有锂电池热失控早期预警方法主要依赖温度信号，可能错过在快速加热前出现的机械前兆信号，存在预警滞后和漏报风险。

Method: 提出机制感知的物理引导框架：轻量级卷积分类器从机械信号推断安全/预警/危险三种状态机制，通过特征线性调制、物理偏置注意力和机制依赖门控机制调节因果时间卷积骨干网络，联合学习机制识别、热失控检测和灾难时间估计三个任务。

Result: 在30个机械滥用测试的留一实验交叉验证中，F1分数0.89，高温预测均方根误差12.3°C，平均预警提前时间15.6秒，检测成功率0.92，实验级误报率2.7%。预警提前时间比最强基线提高69.6%；移除力信号后预警时间减少60.3%，证实机械前兆信号的重要价值。

Conclusion: 机制感知的热-机械融合策略是受控滥用条件下更早、更可靠热失控预警的有效方法，为电动汽车和储能系统的电池安全监测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Thermal runaway in lithium-ion batteries poses a major safety risk to electric vehicles and energy storage systems. Current early-warning methods depend mainly on temperature and may therefore miss mechanical precursors that emerge before rapid heating. We introduce a regime-aware, physics-guided framework that integrates temperature, voltage, force, deformation, and state-of-charge measurements for early warning under controlled mechanical abuse. A lightweight convolutional classifier first infers safe, warning, or danger regimes from mechanical signals. These regime estimates then condition a causal temporal convolutional backbone through feature-wise linear modulation, physics-biased attention, and regime-dependent gating. Joint learning unifies regime identification, thermal-runaway detection, and time-to-disaster estimation. We evaluate the framework using leave-one-experiment-out cross-validation on 30 mechanical-abuse tests across state-of-charge levels of 10%, 50%, and 90% and two loading protocols. The method achieves an F1 score of 0.89, a high-temperature prediction root-mean-square error of 12.3 °C, a mean warning lead time of 15.6 s, a detection success rate of 0.92, and an experiment-level false alarm rate of 2.7%. Its lead time exceeds that of the strongest baseline by 69.6%. Removing force reduces the lead time by 60.3%, highlighting the value of mechanical precursors. These results support regime-aware thermo-mechanical fusion as a promising strategy for earlier and more reliable thermal-runaway warning under controlled abuse conditions.

</details>


### [85] [HindsightBench: A Black-Box Behavioral Audit Protocol for Parametric Hindsight in Time-Indexed LLM Decision Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18867)
*Haozhe Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HindsightBench，一种黑盒行为审计协议，用于检测大语言模型在时间索引决策任务中的参数化后见之明偏差，揭示了模型训练代际、有效知识截止日期及服务配置对审计结果的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型会将已实现结果的参数化知识泄露到历史金融决策任务中，这一现象已被证实，但用户缺乏一种低成本的方法来审计特定模型是否存在此问题。现有的审计方法成本高昂，需要回测、logprobs或语料库访问，因此迫切需要一种廉价、有效的黑盒审计方案。

Method: 提出HindsightBench黑盒行为审计协议：1) 设计四臂日期操纵矩阵（揭示/仅日期/掩码/移植）；2) 双记忆探针（日期恢复与结果召回）；3) 定义六个模型级度量指标（触发强度、移植效应、截断后安慰剂、可恢复性、行为有效知识截止、召回-准确性分离系数）；4) 在可识别性数据依赖处设置明确门控。协议不需要回测、logprobs或语料库访问，仅以探针级成本运行。

Result: 在15个模型、七家供应商、258节点年代正确宏观面板上应用协议发现：1) 日期触发反射跟随训练代际而非规模——2024年开源代际模型（1B-70B）无此现象，而所有测试的2026代际模型均存在，且在固定MoE架构和3B活跃参数下，Qwen3→Qwen3.6出现切换；2) 有效知识截止日期跨越22个月，且比供应商报告日期提前最多8个月，使得日历窗口安慰剂设计失效；3) 审计结果对服务配置不具不变性——FP8参考模型采用BF16服务会破坏触发估计的稳定性，而AWQ-INT4保持稳定；提供商锁定的推理机制使一探针不收敛。

Conclusion: HindsightBench提供了首个低成本、可操作的黑盒审计方案，能够系统性识别LLM参数化后见之明偏差。研究强调了审计时需固定量化方案和思维机制、披露解析器与采样策略。作者公开了面板数据、冻结预注册、每模型审计行及其美元成本、转录文本和一键复现命令，为LLM金融决策应用的风险评估提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Large language models leak parametric knowledge of realized outcomes into historical financial decision tasks. Existence is settled; what users lack is a cheap way to audit a given model for it. We present HindsightBench, a black-box behavioral audit protocol that profiles parametric hindsight in any time-indexed LLM decision task at probe-level cost (no backtests, no logprobs, no corpus access). The protocol chains a four-arm date-manipulation matrix (revealed/date-only/masked/transplanted), dual memory probes (date recovery; outcome recall), and six per-model metrics -- trigger strength, transplant effect, post-cutoff placebo, recoverability, behaviorally effective knowledge cutoff, and a recall-accuracy dissociation coefficient -- with explicit gates where identifiability is data-dependent. Applying it to 15 models from seven vendors on a 258-node vintage-correct macro panel yields three headline patterns: (i) the date-trigger reflex tracks training generation, not scale -- absent across the 2024 open-weight generation from 1B to 70B, present in every tested 2026-generation model, and switching on within one vendor lineage (Qwen3 -> Qwen3.6) at fixed MoE architecture and 3B active parameters; (ii) effective cutoffs span 22 months across vendors and precede vendor-reported dates by up to eight months, invalidating calendar-window placebo designs; (iii) audit results are not invariant to serving -- BF16 serving of an FP8-referenced model breaks the trigger estimate's stability while AWQ-INT4 preserves it, and a provider-locked reasoning regime makes one probe non-convergent -- so the protocol ships with operational requirements (pin quantization and thinking regime; disclose parser and sampling policy). We release the panel, frozen preregistrations, per-model audit rows with measured dollar costs, transcripts, and one-command regeneration.

</details>


### [86] [Reinforcement Learning for Delivery Drone-Based Participatory Sensing in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18874)
*Xin Ouyang,Songxin Lei,Xusen Guo,Yutian Jiang,Sijie Ruan,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TSRL框架，通过双层强化学习解决无人机城市感知中可扩展性和多时间尺度决策的挑战，在真实数据集上实现20.1%-46.6%的利润提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机感知方法忽视风等环境扰动对无人机速度和能效的影响，在动态环境下的配送-感知联合任务中面临两大挑战：机队规模扩大带来的可扩展性瓶颈、宏观任务调度与微观速度控制之间的多时间尺度决策异质性。

Method: 提出SensUAV问题建模和双层强化学习框架(TSRL)：宏观层采用任务嵌入感知调度器，通过分离编码任务特征和顺序评估无人机适配性来处理可扩展性；微观层采用风感知速度控制器，学习细粒度速度调度以适应动态环境变化。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，TSRL显著优于基线方法，在杭州和上海分别实现平均系统利润提升20.1%和46.6%。

Conclusion: TSRL框架成功解决了无人机城市感知中环境扰动影响下的可扩展性和多时间尺度决策问题，为动态环境下的无人机配送-感知联合任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Using Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) for urban sensing has emerged as a powerful paradigm to monitor the status of the city, e.g., air quality and noise levels, through agile aerial crowdsourcing. Despite this potential, existing UAV-based sensing approaches overlook environmental disturbances like wind that drastically impact drone velocity and energy efficiency. Consequently, directly applying existing methods to this joint delivery and sensing paradigm in dynamic environments faces two severe challenges: (1) scalability bottlenecks as fleet sizes expand; and (2) multi-timescale decision heterogeneity between macro task dispatching and micro velocity control. To tackle these, we formalize the problem as SensUAV and propose a Two TimeScale Reinforcement Learning framework (TSRL). Specifically, TSRL separates decision-making into two cooperative layers. At the macro level, a task-embedding sensing dispatcher handles scalability by separately encoding distinct task features and sequentially evaluating UAV suitability before task selection. At the micro level, a wind-aware velocity controller learns fine-grained velocity scheduling to adapt to dynamic environmental variations. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that TSRL significantly outperforms baselines, achieving average system profit improvements of 20.1% in Hangzhou and 46.6% in Shanghai.

</details>


### [87] [Physics-Informed Super-Resolution of Atmospheric Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18877)
*Chang Xu,Gencer Sumbul,Hugo Porta,Manon Béchaz,Sebastian Schemm,Devis Tuia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了物理约束的超分辨率方法（PISR）用于大气数据降尺度，通过引入流体静力原始方程约束，提高了重建数据的物理一致性、准确性和极端事件检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全球变暖背景下，极端事件频发，高分辨率大气数据的需求日益增长，而现有观测数据空间分辨率不足。虽然机器学习超分辨率方法能够高效重建高分辨率数据，但其是否满足地球系统基本物理规律尚不明确，这引发了对其在气候应用中可信度的担忧。

Method: 提出了物理约束超分辨率（PISR）方法，基于流体静力原始方程设计多尺度物理约束目标函数，使超分辨率输出服从这些方程并自然编码变量间关系。同时提出了标准化物理一致性（NPC）指标，基于原始方程量化超分辨率数据的物理一致性。

Result: 在ERA5、CERRA和COSMO数据集上的实验表明，PISR方法通过提高物理一致性、超分辨率准确性和下游极端事件检测能力，增强了重建数据的保真度。热浪和极端风案例研究验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: PISR方法成功地将物理约束融入大气数据超分辨率重建过程，确保了重建数据满足基本物理规律，提高了极端事件检测的可信度，为气候相关应用提供了更可靠的物理一致性数据重建方案。

Abstract: In the context of global warming, extreme events have become more frequent and intense, making their trustworthy detection and forecasting more important than ever. Yet, atmospheric observations lack sufficient spatial resolution, motivating atmospheric data downscaling as a way to reconstruct high-resolution data from coarse observations. This task is now being formulated as a super-resolution (SR) problem with machine learning methods featuring high efficiency. Nevertheless, it remains unclear whether the super-resolved atmospheric data still satisfies fundamental physics governing the Earth system, raising concerns about their trustworthiness in climate-related applications. In this work, we address this challenge by constraining SR models to respect hydrostatic primitive equations that represent multivariate atmospheric physics. First, we propose a Physics-Informed Super-Resolution (PISR) method involving multi-scale physics-informed objectives based on primitive equations. PISR favors the SR outputs to respect these equations and therefore naturally encodes inter-variable relationships. In addition, we propose a metric called Normalized Physical Consistency (NPC) derived from said primitive equations to measure the physical consistency of super-resolved data. Experiments on ERA5, CERRA, and COSMO demonstrate that PISR enhances the reconstruction fidelity by improving physical consistency, SR accuracy, and downstream detection of extreme events, as demonstrated by case studies in heatwaves and extreme winds.

</details>


### [88] [RAMP: Recognition parametrisation by Amortised Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18883)
*Lior Fox,Kai Biegun,James Heald,Samo Hromadka,Arielle Rosinski,Maneesh Sahani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RAMP方法，通过学习灵活的非线性摊余消息传递框架来隐式定义潜在结构，实现了在复杂高维数据的表达性非线性模型中高效恢复潜变量分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督学习的核心目标是通过潜变量解释观测数据间的依赖关系。现有概率图模型方法存在局限性：基于分布选择的方法依赖可处理的置信传播，而近似方法随模型规模和复杂度增加而扩展性差。

Method: 基于最近发展的识别参数化建模范式，提出RAMP方法。该方法通过学习灵活、非线性、摊余的消息传递框架来隐式定义潜在结构，避免了传统方法中的分布选择限制或计算瓶颈。

Result: RAMP能够在处理复杂高维数据的表达性非线性模型中，实现高效的基于似然的潜变量分布恢复，证明了方法的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: RAMP为无监督学习中的潜变量推断提供了一种新的解决思路，通过隐式定义和消息传递机制，在保持计算效率的同时提升了模型的表达能力和适用范围。

Abstract: A central aim of unsupervised learning is to uncover latent factors that explain dependencies among observations. Probabilistic models typically achieve this by introducing multiple latent variables linked through a graph of conditional relationships, with distributional parameters and their dependence learnt from data. Learning relies either on distributional choices that allow tractable belief propagation, or on approximations that scale poorly with model size and complexity. We build on the recently developed recognition-parametrised modelling paradigm to propose an alternative approach: RAMP, a method that implicitly defines latent structure by learning a flexible, nonlinear, amortised message-passing framework. We show that RAMP enables efficient likelihood-based recovery of latent-variable distributions within expressive nonlinear models acting on complex high-dimensional data.

</details>


### [89] [KALE: Kernel Alignment with Loss Equilibration for Stable CLIP-DINOv2 Alignment at Web Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18885)
*Michał Pawłowicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KALE控制器，自适应调整对齐损失权重，解决了固定权重方法在噪声数据集上失效的问题，显著提升了CLIP模型的视觉表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KUEA方法使用固定权重在ImageNet-1K上调整CLIP模型，但这种固定权重方法无法迁移到噪声较大的网络规模数据集（CC12M），因为对齐损失项的贡献在噪声数据上急剧下降至约0.2%，导致其梯度在任意固定权重下都无效。

Method: 引入KALE（Kernel-based Adaptive Loss Equilibration）损失均衡控制器：1）实时跟踪两种损失值；2）自适应重新缩放对齐权重以达到目标比率；3）无需针对每个数据集调参。研究还发现需要使用有界的高学习率和带有适度下限的衰减调度来保持稳定性。

Result: 在3.3M图像的CC12M子集上：保持图像-文本检索能力的同时，SVHN线性探测结果可重现地改善；在标准11数据集平均上零样本性能相比CLIP提升+2.00，超过KUEA的+1.29。所需权重比原始值增加约四个数量级，且依赖于具体配置。

Conclusion: 自适应损失均衡控制器KALE有效解决了固定权重方法在噪声数据上的失效问题，无需数据集特定的调参即可恢复信号。控制器能够达到均衡而非发散，在保持文本编码器兼容性的同时显著改善视觉表示质量。

Abstract: Kernel-based alignment of CLIP toward a vision centric teacher such as DINOv2 (KUEA) improves CLIP's visual representations while preserving text-encoder compatibility, using a fixed trade-off weight tuned on curated ImageNet-1K. We ask whether this transfers to noisy, web-scale data (CC12M) and find that it does not: the alignment term's weighted contribution falls to about 0.2% of the clean term, so under any fixed weight its gradient is effectively inert. We introduce KALE, a loss-equilibration controller that tracks both losses and adaptively rescales the alignment weight toward a target ratio, restoring the signal with no per-dataset tuning; reaching balance requires increasing the weight by roughly four orders of magnitude, and the required value is configuration-dependent, so no fixed scalar suffices. We characterize the resulting regime: a bounded high learning rate and a decaying schedule with a moderate floor are needed for stability, and the controller equilibrates rather than diverging. On a 3.3M-image CC12M subset, the aligned model preserves image-text retrieval and reproducibly improves SVHN linear probing; zero-shot improves by +2.00 over CLIP on the standard 11-dataset average, exceeding KUEA's +1.29. We report all results with explicit run-to-run variance and base our conclusions on the metrics that are stable across runs.

</details>


### [90] [Breaking Feedback-Blindness: Utility-Augmented Transformer for Sequential Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18910)
*Yuyang Shen,Shan Dai,Daimin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了效用增强Transformer(UAT)，通过引入效用状态调制注意力机制，解决了现有模型在非平稳环境下"反馈盲检索"的结构性瓶颈，在多个决策任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer决策模型在非平稳和部分可观测环境中存在结构瓶颈：即使奖励被用于训练或作为输入token，注意力检索仍主要由观测相似性驱动。作者将这一限制形式化为"反馈盲检索"问题，证明了在反馈信息性任务上，仅观测的注意力机制无法区分具有不同行动-奖励结果的观测等价历史，导致次优决策。

Method: 提出效用增强Transformer(UAT)，一种新的反馈条件检索注意力架构。核心机制是引入紧凑的效用状态来调制查询、键和值投影，使行动-奖励历史能够在前向传播过程中直接改变上下文检索。UAT具有精确的零门退化特性，当反馈无信息时可恢复为普通Transformer。

Result: 在四个非平稳基准测试上验证：具有隐藏目标转移的合成导航、非平稳脓毒症治疗、跨市场投资组合分配和延迟反馈推荐系统。UAT在观测仅用、测试时适应和输入级反馈基线上均表现更好，在需要更强适应能力的噪声环境中优势尤其明显。

Conclusion: 在有限视界紧性和Lipschitz假设下，证明UAT严格扩展了仅观测Transformer类，能够均匀逼近反馈依赖决策映射。UAT成功解决了反馈盲检索问题，为非平稳环境下的序列决策提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Sequential decision making in non-stationary and partially observable environments requires rapid adaptation to latent regime changes. However, existing Transformer decision models face a structural bottleneck in the retrieval mechanism: even when reward is used for training or exposed as an input token, attention retrieval remains primarily driven by observation-derived similarity. We formalize this limitation as feedback-blind retrieval, and formally show that, on feedback-informative tasks, observation-equivalent histories with different action-reward outcomes cannot be distinguished by any observation-only attention, resulting in suboptimal choice. To address this mismatch, we propose the Utility-Augmented Transformer (UAT), a new feedback-conditioned retrieval attention architecture in which a compact utility state modulates the query, key, and value projections, allowing action-reward history to directly alter context retrieval during the forward pass. UAT also enjoys an exact zero-gate degradation property that recovers the Vanilla Transformer when feedback is uninformative. Under finite-horizon compactness and Lipschitz assumptions, we prove that UAT strictly enlarges the observation-only Transformer class and can uniformly approximate feedback-dependent decision maps. Across four non-stationary benchmarks: synthetic navigation with hidden goal shifts, non-stationary sepsis treatment, cross-market portfolio allocation, and delayed-feedback recommendation, UAT consistently improves performance over observation-only, test-time adaptation, and input-level feedback baselines, with particularly large gains in noisier regimes that require stronger adaptation.

</details>


### [91] [Circuit Claims Depend on What Is Extracted and How It Is Compared](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18921)
*Yang Sheng,Jie Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 电路提取方法存在解释不确定性：行为保持不能唯一确定电路，电路成分重叠度低且对提取选择敏感，需规范报告实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路提取常被解读为目标行为背后的机制，但作者指出这种解读存在不确定性：保持行为并不能唯一确定一个电路，因为所支持的结论依赖于报告哪个电路以及如何比较不同电路。

Method: 在合成Lean策略预测基准上（预测证明的下一步），利用固定证明规则和随机表面形式的特性，将电路差异归因于提取选择而非任务本身。比较稠密和权重稀疏检查点，评估原子规则和组合规则证明，变化报告对象（紧凑预测保持电路、更广泛的图结构、最小损失阈值子图）及注意力头查询键的表示方式。

Result: 精确的组件间边缘重叠度低且对提取选择敏感，有时降至随机基线水平；但两个粗粒度总结保持稳定：所选注意力头集合，以及不同RL初始化条件的电路规模排序。RL在组合证明上的最大精度提升伴随原子电路之外最多的额外结构。

Conclusion: 电路层面的结论仅在明确说明报告哪个电路、提取所用剪枝阈值以及电路比较的粒度层次后才具有良好定义。作者将这些要求提炼为电路提取研究的报告实践规范。

Abstract: Circuit extraction identifies a small set of model components whose presence preserves a target behavior under ablation, and the resulting circuit is often read as the mechanism behind that behavior. We argue that this reading is under-determined: preserving behavior does not single out one circuit, because the claim it supports depends on which circuit is reported and how two circuits are compared. We make this concrete in a synthetic Lean tactic-prediction benchmark -- predicting the next step of a proof -- where fixed proof rules with randomized surface form let differences between extracted circuits be attributed to these choices rather than to the task. Across dense and weight-sparse checkpoints (most weights constrained to zero) of the same transformer, evaluated on atomic (single-rule) and compositional (multi-rule) proofs, we vary which extracted object is reported (a compact prediction-preserving circuit, a broader graph that also keeps surrounding read, write, and routing structure, or the smallest subgraph meeting a post-ablation loss threshold), and whether each attention head's query and key are represented jointly or separately. Exact component-to-component edge overlap is low and sensitive to these choices, at times dropping to a random baseline, while two coarser summaries stay stable: the set of selected attention heads, and the circuit-size ranking of conditions that differ in which supervised checkpoint initializes reinforcement learning (RL). The largest accuracy gains from RL on compositional proofs come with the most structure beyond the atomic circuits. A circuit-level claim is therefore well defined only once one states which circuit is reported, the pruning threshold used to extract it, and the level at which circuits are compared. We distill these requirements into a reporting practice for circuit-extraction studies.

</details>


### [92] [Visual Semantic Decoding of Electrocorticography from Video Stimuli using End-to-End Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18923)
*Stella Ho,Joel Villalobos,Joseph West,Jingyang Liu,Weijie Qi,Haruhiko Kishima,Ryohei Fukuma,Takufumi Yanagisawa,Sam E. John,David B. Grayden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究证明了基于皮层脑电图(ECoG)的端到端深度学习框架可以在少量训练样本下实现对动态视觉刺激的语义解码，解码性能良好且模型行为具有可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在复杂数据条件下，利用端到端深度学习框架从ECoG脑电信号中解码视觉语义信息的可行性，突破传统手工特征提取的限制。

Method: 使用来自17名耐药性癫痫患者的ECoG数据集，将时间序列神经信号作为输入，预测视频刺激的视觉类别；对比多种深度学习架构和频段滤波输入，最终采用mixup数据增强、Transformer编码器以及高伽马频段(80-150 Hz)的刺激后900 ms时间窗口信号。

Result: 在每类少于50个训练样本的条件下，所提出的解码系统取得了良好的解码性能；分析表明早期视觉皮层(V2-V4)、腹侧视觉通路、MT+复合体及邻近视觉区域和外侧颞叶皮层对解码贡献显著。

Conclusion: 端到端深度学习框架能够在无需手工特征的情况下从动态视觉刺激中实现有效的语义解码，且模型在频谱、时间和皮层维度上具有可解释性，与现有神经科学知识相一致。

Abstract: ECoG-based visual semantic decoding enables inference of semantic interpretation of visual perception from complex, noisy brain activity. This study examines the feasibility of visual semantic decoding using an end-to-end deep learning framework using electrocorticography (ECoG). Specifically, the decoding task is to predict visual categories from video stimuli using time-series neural inputs. A previously collected ECoG dataset from participants ($n=17$) with drug-resistant epilepsy is used for analysis. With fewer than 50 training samples per visual category, this study evaluates multiple deep learning approaches, artificial neural network architectures, and frequency-band filtered inputs. The best-performing approach is analyzed to shed light on the discriminative information it relies on across spectral, temporal, and cortical dimensions. The selected decoding system uses mixup augmentation, a Transformer-based encoder, and high-gamma (80-150 Hz) inputs with a 900 ms post-stimulus window. Further analysis shows that early visual cortex (V2-V4), ventral stream visual cortex, MT+ complex with neighbouring visual areas, and lateral temporal cortex contributed substantially to decoding performance. This study demonstrates that an end-to-end deep learning framework can yield promising decoding performance from dynamic visual stimuli without handcrafted features, while the model behavior remains interpretable through spectral, temporal, and cortical dimensions, which are broadly consistent with established neuroscience knowledge.

</details>


### [93] [Functional Equivalence and Geometric Diversity in Neural Network Approximations: An Empirical Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18930)
*Anuragine S A,Prem Jagadeesan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过几何多样性分析发现，即使是泛逼近定理保证的函数逼近，神经网络参数空间也存在大量功能等价但几何结构不同的解，表现出参数冗余性，并提出了基于简约性、估计便利性和推断效率的模型选择准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然万能逼近定理证明单隐层神经网络可以任意精度逼近紧致域上的连续函数，但神经网络表示的唯一性无法保证，这引发了对实际可识别性的担忧。研究旨在通过分析功能等价性和几何多样性来回应这一关切。

Method: 研究分析了单层神经网络和多层感知器在噪声和非噪声条件下的表现。除了网络容量外，通过"松散性"(sloppiness)研究几何性质，使用代价函数Hessian矩阵的谱特征和有效秩来量化参数空间的维度。

Result: 研究发现存在大量功能上不可区分但几何上多样化的神经网络等价类，这些网络持续表现出低有效秩和结构冗余性特征，揭示了参数空间中存在多个等价解的现象。

Conclusion: 提出了基于简约性、估计便利性和推断效率的模型选择准则，用于识别最优模型，为理解神经网络参数空间的几何结构和解决可识别性问题提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The Universal Approximation Theorem states that a neural network with a single hidden layer is sufficient to approximate any continuous univariate function on a compact domain to arbitrary error. However, the uniqueness of such neural network representations is not guaranteed, raising questions about practical identifiability. In this work, we address this concern by analyzing functional equivalence and geometric diversity of neural network approximations to a few elementary mathematical functions. The analysis includes an extensive study of single-layer neural networks and multilayer perceptrons under noisy and noise-free conditions. Beyond just network capacity, we study the geometric properties through the lens of sloppiness, characterized by the eigen spectrum of the Hessian of the cost function and the effective rank to quantify the dimensionality of parameter space. The study reveals large equivalence classes of functionally indistinguishable yet geometrically diverse networks that consistently exhibit low effective rank and structural redundancy. Finally, a model select criterion is proposed for identifying optimal models based on parsimony, ease of estimation, and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [94] [H$^2$SD: Hybrid Hindsight Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18955)
*Qiye Cai,Yichuan Ma,Linyang Li,Peiji Li,Yongkang Chen,Qipeng Guo,Yicheng Zou,Tao Gui,Xiaocheng Feng,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了H²SD框架，根据轨迹成败差异化利用教师信号：成功轨迹调制更新幅度，失败轨迹提供纠正方向，在多个推理基准上优于现有RLVR、OPSD和RLSD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法因稀疏奖励导致token级信用分配受限；OPD需要额外教师模型；OPSD存在信息泄漏和优化不稳定问题；RLSD无法为失败推理提供明确纠正方向。

Method: 提出H²SD（混合后见之明自蒸馏）：对于成功轨迹，教师接收学生响应和重述指令，仅调制更新幅度而不改变奖励确定的方向；对于失败轨迹，教师基于包含关键推理步骤和验证答案的参考提示进行条件化，学生向教师最小化反向KL散度。

Result: 在多个挑战性推理基准测试中，H²SD持续优于代表性RLVR、OPSD和RLSD基线方法，同时保持优化稳定性和良好的生成效率。

Conclusion: H²SD通过差异化利用教师信号成功平衡了信息泄漏与纠正方向的问题，为自蒸馏强化学习提供了一种稳定有效的解决方案，在推理任务中取得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has substantially improved the reasoning capabilities of large language models on tasks such as mathematical reasoning and code generation. However, most RLVR methods assign a scalar outcome reward to an entire trajectory, resulting in sparse supervision and limited token-level credit assignment. On-policy distillation (OPD) provides denser supervision by distilling token-level distributions from a stronger teacher model, but requires an additional teacher and typically assumes a shared vocabulary. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) removes this dependency by conditioning the same model on privileged information to construct a teacher policy. However, directly matching the teacher distribution may cause information leakage and unstable optimization. RLSD avoids direct matching by using the teacher signal only to modulate update magnitudes, but it cannot provide an explicit correction direction when the sampled reasoning fails. To address this tradeoff, we introduce $\mathrm{H}^{2}\mathrm{SD}$, a hybrid hindsight self distillation framework that uses the teacher differently according to trajectory correctness. For successful trajectories, the teacher receives the student response confirmed as correct together with a rephrasing instruction, and its probabilities on the original response tokens are used to modulate update magnitudes without changing the direction determined by the reward. For failed trajectories, we condition the teacher on a reference hint containing key reasoning steps and a verified answer, and minimize the reverse KL divergence from the student to the teacher. Experiments on multiple challenging reasoning benchmarks show that H$^2$SD consistently outperforms representative RLVR, OPSD, and RLSD baselines while maintaining stable optimization and favorable generation efficiency.

</details>


### [95] [SFGA: A Statistics-First Gating Architecture with Adjudicative Escalation for Trustworthy SFT Data Procurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18960)
*Arther Tian,Alex Ding,Simon Wu,Aaron Chan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为\sys{}的统计优先门控架构，将数据采购视为在多样性、有用性和冗余性三个质量维度上的成本感知路由问题，通过统计估计和辩论机制实现高效准确的采购决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在监督微调（SFT）数据采购过程中，买方需要在下游训练开始前就判断候选语料库是否值得获取，这是一个成本敏感的决策问题。传统方法要么盲目采购要么全面验证，缺乏成本与质量的权衡机制。

Method: 提出\sys{}架构，包含三个核心组件：1）低成本盲测统计估计器，为三个质量维度（多样性、有用性、冗余性）生成置信区间估计；2）门控机制，仅在置信区间紧凑、样本量充足且维度一致时接受决策；3）辩论升级机制，当门控拒绝时启动购买方和拒绝方两个 advocate judge 的辩论，由裁判给出最终裁决。

Result: 在12个数据集（$2{\times}3{\times}2$网格）5个随机种子的控制基准测试中，门控准确率达0.90，F1值0.83，单位成本$0.017，介于始终验证基线（0.75）和oracle上界（0.98）之间，且成本低于始终升级策略（$0.020）。辩论路径的分析显示：反方胜率为0.80（$p\approx3{\times}10^{-6}$），advocate互换后立场翻转率达52%，暴露了负面偏差和位置偏差。

Conclusion: 该统计优先门控架构成功实现了成本效益平衡，在有限预算下达到接近oracle的性能。研究同时揭示了LLM作为judge时的偏见问题，强调了诚实负诊断的重要性。研究明确将注入控制参数的评估框架定为测量保真度和路由校准的控制合成基准，并将外部有效性界定为未来工作。

Abstract: Procuring supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data forces a buyer to decide, before any downstream training, whether a candidate corpus is worth acquiring. We present \sys{}, a statistics-first gating architecture that treats procurement as a cost-aware routing problem over three intrinsic quality axes -- diversity, utility, and redundancy. Cheap blind measurements are summarised into per-axis estimates with confidence intervals; a gate accepts a decision only when intervals are tight, sample sizes are adequate, and the axes agree, otherwise it escalates the case to an adjudicative debate between a buy-advocate and a reject-advocate judge, resolved by a presiding verdict. On a controlled benchmark of 12 datasets ($2{\times}3{\times}2$ grid over the three axes) with 5 seeds, the gate reaches 0.90 accuracy and 0.83 $F_1$ at \$0.017 per unit, sitting between an always-verify baseline (0.75) and an oracle upper bound (0.98) while spending less than always-escalate (\$0.020). We further report honest negative diagnostics of the debate path: a con-side win rate of 0.80 ($p\approx3{\times}10^{-6}$) and a 52\% position-flip rate under advocate swapping expose negativity and positional biases that a naive LLM-judge would hide. We frame the injected-knob evaluation explicitly as a controlled synthetic benchmark for measurement fidelity and routing calibration, and delimit external validity as future work.

</details>


### [96] [Variational meta-learning inference for low dimensional neural system identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18965)
*Matteo Rufolo,Dario Piga,Marco Forgione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变分推断的概率化流形元学习框架，在保持预测精度的同时为低数据场景下的非线性系统辨识提供了可靠的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在非线性系统辨识中虽然有效，但在低数据场景下存在过拟合问题且缺乏可靠的不确定性量化。现有的流形元学习框架虽然解决了数据效率问题，但仅限于确定性方法。

Method: 提出基于摊销变分推断的概率化流形元学习框架，学习低维参数流形上的生成式先验；任务适应阶段结合最大后验估计和拉普拉斯近似来获得数学上合理的后验近似。

Result: 在静态回归任务和Bouc-Wen动力系统基准测试中，该方法在严重低数据场景下达到了与确定性方法相当的预测精度，并成功提供了校准的不确定性边界。

Conclusion: 该概率化扩展方法有效地解决了确定性流形元学习框架缺乏不确定性量化的问题，为低数据场景下的非线性系统辨识提供了一种可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning has proven highly effective for nonlinear system identification, but heavily parameterized neural networks are prone to overfitting in low-data regimes and lack reliable uncertainty quantification. The recently developed manifold meta-learning framework addresses the data efficiency problem by restricting the model parameters to a meta-learned low-dimensional manifold. However, that method is purely deterministic. We propose a fully probabilistic extension of the manifold meta-learning framework, based on amortized Variational Inference, where a generative prior over the low-dimensional parameter manifold is learned. During task-specific adaptation, we combine Maximum A Posteriori estimation with the Laplace approximation to yield a mathematically grounded posterior approximation. Evaluated on a static regression task and the Bouc--Wen dynamical system benchmark, the proposed approach achieves predictive accuracy comparable to its deterministic counterpart while successfully providing calibrated uncertainty bounds in severely low-data regimes.

</details>


### [97] [Subject-Conditioned Glucose Forecasting in Type-1 Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19006)
*Giorgia Rigamonti,Mirko Paolo Barbato,Davide Marelli,Paolo Napoletano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SCGP，一种用于个性化血糖预测的多模态深度学习架构，通过显式分离主体特征表征与血糖动态建模，在两个基准数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能，提升了不良血糖事件检测的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的血糖预测方法主要依赖群体层面的表示或隐式个性化策略，无法有效地生成针对特定个体的预测，而精准的血糖浓度预测对于1型糖尿病管理至关重要，有助于早期发现不良血糖事件并支持及时的治疗干预。

Method: 提出了主体条件血糖预测（SCGP）架构，这是一种新颖的多模态深度学习方法。SCGP基于观测到的血糖数据和从上下文信息中学习的紧凑主体特定表示来调节血糖预测。通过显式地将主体表征与血糖动态建模分离，并避免异构输入的早期融合，该框架有效地捕捉了个体间差异性，同时保持了稳健可靠的时序建模。

Result: 在两个最先进的基准数据集上的实验表明，SCGP在多个预测时间范围内持续改善了预测性能，实现了对不良血糖事件的可靠检测。

Conclusion: 研究突显了显式主体条件化对个性化糖尿病管理的益处，证明了通过将主体特征与血糖动态解耦可以有效捕捉个体差异性，从而实现更精准的个性化血糖预测。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of blood glucose concentration is key in the management of Type 1 Diabetes, facilitating early detection of adverse glycemic events and supporting timely therapeutic interventions. Despite recent advances in glucose prediction, most existing approaches rely on population-level representations or implicit personalization strategies that fail to deliver effective subject-specific forecasts. In this work, we propose Subject-Conditioned Glucose Prediction (SCGP), a novel multimodal deep learning architecture conceived for personalized blood glucose prediction. SCGP conditions glucose predictions based on observed glucose data and a compact subject-specific representation learned from contextual information. By explicitly separating subject characterization from glucose dynamics modeling and avoiding early fusion of heterogeneous inputs, the proposed framework effectively captures inter-subject variability while preserving robust and reliable temporal modeling. Experiments on two state-of-the-art benchmark datasets demonstrate that SCGP consistently improves forecasting performance, enabling reliable detection of adverse glycemic events across multiple prediction horizons, highlighting the benefits of explicit subject conditioning for personalized diabetes management.

</details>


### [98] [Biological Amnesia in ICU Time-Series Prediction: A Drift-Adaptive Two-Stream Architecture with Temporal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19020)
*Fatema Ferdous Tamanna,K. M. Merajul Arefin,Md. Abdul Masud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结构化解耦生理与治疗表示的自适应临床决策支持架构，通过选择性更新治疗流并保持生理表示稳定，实现了ICU干预预测模型的持续演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策支持系统随着治疗方案的演进会出现静默退化，而现有的适应方法将模型视为整体块，无法区分稳定的患者生理特征与变化的机构实践。

Method: 提出自适应临床智能架构，结构化解耦生理与治疗表示。在双重分布和准确性触发条件下，仅将参数更新限制在治疗流。自动审计日志记录驱动每次适应事件的治疗特征及其重要性变化。推理时，归因驱动的时序RAG模块将每次预测锚定到患者特异性、时代匹配的PubMed证据，并基于患者主导生理特征。

Result: 使用84,792例MIMIC-IV住院数据（2008-2022）的严格时间分割实验表明：漂移完全局限于治疗流，验证了结构性先验；选择性适应在血管活性药物和脓毒性休克判别、校准上优于静态源模型；完全重新训练的基线整体判别性能略高，但遗漏了框架正确识别的26例脓毒性休克病例；框架保持了与适应前源模型的检索一致性，而重新训练基线明显退化。

Conclusion: 将适应结构化约束于漂移组件并保留稳定的生理表示，使临床AI能够与实践共同演进而不扭曲已学习的患者生物学。该架构为高风险临床环境中自适应模型的可治理、可解释部署提供了模板。

Abstract: Background: Clinical decision support systems degrade silently as treatment protocols evolve, yet standard adaptation methods treat models as monolithic blocks, unable to distinguish stable patient physiology from shifting institutional practice. Methods: We propose an adaptive clinical intelligence architecture for ICU intervention prediction that structurally decouples physiological from treatment representations, confining parameter updates to the treatment stream upon a dual distributional and accuracy trigger. Automated audit logs record which treatment features drove each adaptation event and how their importance shifted. At inference, an attribution-driven Temporal RAG module grounds each prediction in patient-specific, era-matched PubMed evidence anchored to the patient's dominant physiological features. Experiments used 84,792 MIMIC-IV stays (2008-2022) under strict chronological split. Results: Drift localised entirely to the treatment stream, validating the structural prior. Selective adaptation improved vasopressor and septic shock discrimination and calibration over the static source model. A fully retrained baseline yielded marginally higher aggregate discrimination but missed 26 septic shock cases the framework correctly identified, with none in the reverse direction; retrieval consistency with the pre-adaptation source model was preserved by the framework but degraded substantially in the retrained baseline. Conclusions: Structurally constraining adaptation to drifting components while preserving stable physiological representations enables clinical AI to evolve with practice without distorting learned patient biology. This architecture offers a template for governable, interpretable deployment of adaptive models in high-stakes clinical environments.

</details>


### [99] [Unsupervised Multi-kernel Learning for Automated Algorithm Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19031)
*Yihang Lu,Tome Eftimov,Carola Doerr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多核聚类的无监督算法选择方法，通过融合多种异构特征表示在DE算法集上取得最优性能，并在PSO上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的黑箱优化自动算法选择方法依赖监督学习模型，这些模型训练成本高、依赖基准测试数据，且往往难以泛化到未见过的优化问题类别。为克服这些限制，本文探索一种无监督的替代方案。

Method: 采用多核k-means聚类方法，在聚类阶段不使用性能标签，而是联合学习聚类分配和核权重。方法整合四种异构特征表示（ELA、DeepELA、DoE2Vec和TransOptAS），通过严格的三阶段评估协议将聚类结果映射到求解器推荐。

Result: 在基于BBOB的DE和PSO算法选择任务上，多核聚类方法在DE算法集上获得最强平均性能，在PSO算法集上与领先基线方法保持竞争力并略占优势。学习到的核权重保留了ELA和TransOptAS，而DeepELA和DoE2Vec权重为零，提供了任务特定的可解释性。

Conclusion: 多核聚类为黑箱优化中的算法选择提供了一种有效的无监督替代方案，能够自动识别并保留有价值的特征表示，避免了监督方法的泛化问题。

Abstract: Automated algorithm selection in black-box optimization typically relies on supervised models that map landscape features to algorithm performance labels. Such models are costly to train, benchmark-dependent, and often fail to generalize to unseen problem classes. We study an unsupervised alternative: multi-kernel clustering over heterogeneous landscape representations, in which problem instances are grouped without using performance labels in the clustering stage, and the resulting clusters are mapped post hoc to solver recommendations through a strictly separated three-stage evaluation protocol. Drawing on two decades of advances in multiple kernel learning, we adopt a multi-kernel k-means formulation that jointly learns cluster assignments and kernel weights over four heterogeneous landscape views: ELA, DeepELA, DoE2Vec, and TransOptAS. On affine BBOB-derived selector tasks for Differential Evolution (DE) and Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) at a fixed evaluation budget, we report mean plus or minus standard deviation selector profiles over 50 independent random seeds for stochastic configurations. Multi-kernel clustering obtains the strongest mean profile on the DE portfolio and remains competitive with, and nominally ahead of, the leading baselines on the more compressed PSO portfolio, where differences among the best methods are small relative to stochastic variation. In representative median-seed runs used for visualization, the learned kernel weights retain ELA and TransOptAS while assigning zero weight to DeepELA and DoE2Vec, providing a task-specific interpretation of which representations are retained by the multi-kernel model for selector-oriented grouping.

</details>


### [100] [Spectral Higher-Order Neural Networks Have Sharp Expressivity Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19042)
*Gianluca Peri,Diego Febbe,Duccio Fanelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了谱高阶神经网络(SHONNs)，通过谱属性参数化方法解决了神经超图参数爆炸问题，并在N位奇偶校验任务上进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经超图作为现代机器学习参考模型神经网络的自然推广，其部署面临参数爆炸的挑战——加权超边数量导致计算成本难以处理。已有研究提出利用谱属性的新型参数化方法，通过权重共享方案回收参数并显著降低计算成本。

Method: 基于谱高阶框架，采用谱属性参数化方法实现神经超图的权重共享机制。在N位奇偶校验任务这一公认具有挑战性的测试平台上对谱高阶神经网络(SHONNs)进行基准测试和评估。

Result: 初步测试表明谱高阶架构在性能和可解释性方面都有显著改进。SHONNs具有多功能且高度可调的假设空间。

Conclusion: 谱高阶神经网络(SHONNs)通过谱参数化方法成功解决了神经超图的参数爆炸问题，在N位奇偶校验等挑战性任务上展现出优异性能，证明了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Neural hypergraphs are a natural generalization of neural networks, the reference models in modern machine learning. Yet, their deployment has proven demanding: the number of weighted hyperedges required leads to an intractable parameter explosion. However, a novel parametrization that leverages spectral attributes for neural hypergraphs has been recently proposed, that enables to recycle parameters via a weight sharing scheme and consequently yields a significant reduction of the associated computational cost. Preliminary tests carried out on spectral higher-order architectures pointed to meaningful improvements in both performance and interpretability. Building on these results, we advance the benchmarking efforts by evaluating the spectral higher order framework on N-bit parity tasks, a well-established testbed known to be particularly challenging. As we will convincingly argue, Spectral Higher-Order Neural Networks (SHONNs) possess a versatile and highly tunable hypothesis space.

</details>


### [101] [Adopting Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards for Molecular Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19044)
*Mingxuan Ouyang,Hao Lan,Wanyu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了LLMol框架，通过结合监督学习和带有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），利用GRPO算法解决分子生成中的目标对齐问题，在多种分子设计任务上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用大语言模型进行分子生成的方法主要依赖监督训练或微调，数据集有限，难以捕捉复杂的分子设计目标；虽然有方法尝试引导生成特定目标，但缺乏直接优化机制，难以使生成的分子与期望属性对齐。

Method: 提出LLMol框架，采用两阶段训练范式：第一阶段通过监督微调使大语言模型掌握化学语法和分子分布；第二阶段引入可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR），将属性奖励信号直接整合以引导分子生成，并采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）算法解决离散序列优化中的高方差和不稳定性问题。

Result: LLMol在单属性目标（如penalized logP、QED）和结构约束优化等多种分子设计任务上均优于现有方法，实现了更高的成功率和更好的效率。

Conclusion: LLMol通过将分子设计形式化为目标条件序列预测任务，并有效整合可验证奖励进行强化学习优化，为分子生成提供了一个稳健有效的解决方案，显著提升了分子设计的性能。

Abstract: Leveraging large language models (LLMs) for molecular generation has shown remarkable potential in chemical and drug design. Current methods primarily rely on supervised training or fine-tuning with limited datasets, which are insufficient to capture complex molecular design objectives. While some approaches attempt to guide generation toward specific goals, they often lack direct optimization mechanisms, making it difficult to align generated molecules with desired properties. To tackle these challenges, we propose \textbf{LLMol}, a principled reinforcement learning framework that directly incorporates verifiable rewards for targeted molecule generation. The key insight is to formulate molecular design as a goal-conditioned sequence prediction task, where verifiable rewards serve as explicit supervision to drive generation toward desired objectives. LLMol follows a two-stage training paradigm combining supervised learning and reinforcement learning. In the first stage, large language models are supervised fine-tuned to capture chemical syntax and molecular distributions. In the second stage, we introduce Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), which directly integrates property-based reward signals to guide molecular generation toward task-specific objectives. To address the high variance and instability common in discrete sequence optimization, we adopt Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), a stable on-policy algorithm that smooths reward signals and improves training robustness. This framework enables LLMol to effectively handle a range of molecular design tasks, including single-property targeting (e.g., penalized logP, QED) and structure-constrained optimization. Experimental results demonstrate that LLMol consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving higher success rates and improved efficiency across diverse molecular benchmarks.

</details>


### [102] [Probabilistic Physics-Aware Machine Learning Predictions of Electric Truck Energy Consumption with Field Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19054)
*Hannes Nilsson,Rafael Basso,Balázs Kulcsár,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合物理原理的数据驱动方法用于车辆能耗预测，通过将第一性原理物理模型集成到机器学习模型中，显著提高了能耗预测的准确性和可靠性，并建立了不确定性量化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的数据驱动方法在车辆能耗预测中缺乏物理约束，可能导致预测结果不够可靠。作者希望将第一性原理物理知识融入到数据驱动方法中，以解决车辆运营过程中不同能量损耗来源的建模问题，从而提高能耗预测的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 提出了一种物理感知的车辆能耗模型，考虑了车辆运营过程中的各种能量损耗来源。基于该物理模型，采用了多种机器学习方法：1）贝叶斯线性回归；2）神经网络；3）梯度提升回归树。开发了不确定性量化框架，通过预测标准差来估计能耗预测的不确定性。与标准版本的机器学习模型进行对比实验。

Result: 基于物理感知模型的贝叶斯线性回归相比标准线性回归提高了能耗预测的可靠性。神经网络和梯度提升回归树等复杂机器学习模型基于同样的物理模型，在能耗预测准确性上进一步提升，显著优于标准版本的相同机器学习模型。所有模型都能合理地学习估计预测不确定性。

Conclusion: 将第一性原理物理知识融入数据驱动方法能够有效提高车辆能耗预测的准确性和可靠性。物理感知的机器学习模型在性能上显著优于传统的纯数据驱动方法。开发的不确定性量化框架能够为能耗预测提供可靠的不确定性估计，增强了模型在实际应用中的可用性。

Abstract: In this work, we incorporate first principle physics into the construction of data-driven methods by considering a model that accounts for the different sources of energy losses during vehicle operations. Our results show that Bayesian linear regression based on this physics-aware model can improve the reliability of the expected energy consumption, as compared with standard linear regression. Further, it is shown that more complex machine learning models such as neural networks and gradient boosted regression trees, based on the same physical model, can further improve the accuracy in energy forecasting and significantly outperform standard versions of the same machine learning models. In addition to point predictions of the energy consumption, we develop a framework for estimating the corresponding uncertainty in the form of predicted standard deviation. Our results show that all of the models learn to estimate the uncertainty reasonably well.

</details>


### [103] [Where Should Optimizer State Live? Tiered State Allocation for Memory-Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19058)
*Nuemaan Malik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MoE训练中优化器状态占用内存过大的问题，提出SkewAdam优化器，针对MoE的三类参数（主干网络、专家、路由器）采用差异化的状态存储策略，将优化器状态从50.6GB压缩至1.29GB（仅为AdamW的2.6%），峰值内存从81.4GB降至31.3GB，同时验证困惑度优于AdamW及其他基线优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型训练中优化器状态占用大量内存。在6.78B参数的MoE语言模型中，AdamW需要50.6GB存储一二阶矩来更新12.6GB的bfloat16权重。MoE的三类参数（密集主干网络、专家、路由器）在规模和梯度统计特性上存在显著差异，但传统优化器对它们采用相同的状态管理，造成了内存浪费。

Method: 提出SkewAdam优化器，采用分层策略管理三类参数的优化器状态：(1) 主干网络（5%参数）：保留float32动量+分解二阶矩；(2) 专家（95%参数）：仅保留分解二阶矩；(3) 路由器（<0.01%参数）：保留精确二阶矩。这种差异化分配根据各参数群体的规模和梯度特性定制状态存储。

Result: 在82M token的受控对比实验中，SkewAdam验证困惑度达到108.4，优于AdamW(126.8)、Muon(120.2)和Lion(393.7)；路由器负载均衡收敛至均匀基准的1%以内。优化器状态从50.6GB降至1.29GB（2.6%），峰值内存从81.4GB降至31.3GB，适配40GB加速器预算。分层策略本身不损失精度（消融实验表明20倍状态量可达相同效果），精度提升来自保留动量。对基线学习率调优后差距缩小但未消除（最佳AdamW达118.5）。

Conclusion: 优化器状态的分配方式至少与状态量同等重要。通过针对MoE不同参数群体采用差异化状态管理，可在几乎不损失精度的情况下大幅压缩内存占用，使大模型训练可在有限显存设备上完成。研究表明，动量的保留是精度提升的关键，而分层策略实现了内存压缩与精度的解耦。

Abstract: Optimizer state is the largest single line item in the memory budget of mixture-of-experts (MoE) training: on a 6.78B-parameter MoE language model, AdamW keeps 50.6 GB of first and second moments to update 12.6 GB of bfloat16 weights. We study SkewAdam, an optimizer built on the observation that the three parameter populations of an MoE - the dense backbone, the experts, and the router - differ enough in size and gradient statistics that they should not receive the same state. SkewAdam keeps float32 momentum plus a factored second moment for the backbone (5% of parameters), a factored second moment alone for the experts (95%), and an exact second moment for the router (<0.01%). The resulting state occupies 1.29 GB, 2.6% of AdamW's, and peak training memory falls from 81.4 GB to 31.3 GB, within the budget of a 40 GB accelerator. In a controlled comparison from identical initializations over 82M tokens, SkewAdam reaches validation perplexity 108.4, ahead of AdamW (126.8), Muon (120.2), and Lion (393.7), and settles router load balance to within 1% of its uniform floor. The allocation is not what earns that perplexity: a tier ablation matches it with twenty times the state, and Adafactor, which shares the factored estimator but drops momentum, plateaus 40 points behind. The tiers buy memory at no cost to accuracy; the accuracy comes from keeping momentum, which a uniform optimizer shares too. Sweeping the baselines' learning rates narrows but does not close the gap: the best tuned AdamW reaches 118.5, tuned Adafactor 139.7. Where optimizer state lives, these results suggest, matters at least as much as how much of it there is.

</details>


### [104] [Deep learning-based prediction of time-resolved adhesive forces in viscoelastic Hertzian contacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19060)
*Ali Maghami,Merten Stender,Michele Ciavarella,Antonio Papangelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于深度学习的方法，使用LSTM网络快速预测粘弹性接触问题的完整力-位移响应，实现了毫秒级推理速度，为软机器人实时控制和优化设计提供了高效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值模拟方法预测粘弹性接触响应计算成本高且强依赖参数，无法满足软机器人抓取操作等实时应用和设计优化循环的需求，亟需一种快速准确的预测方法。

Method: 构建标量条件化、有状态的序列到序列深度学习模型；引入固定测量步长(FMS)表示法处理异构时间尺度；对比训练LSTM、时间卷积网络(TCN)和时间分布密集层三种架构及三种Tabor参数条件化机制。

Result: 最优模型为带有拼接条件化的LSTM架构，在测试集上达到均方误差5.0×10⁻⁴、拉脱力误差中位数≈2.2%、迟滞误差中位数≈1.1%，推理时间中位数仅0.16秒。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了对未见参数组合的准确泛化，能够替代重复性数值评估，为软机器人的控制导向应用提供了可靠的快速预测工具。
